```python
import json
import datetime
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.models import MonitoringZone, SensorDevice, SensorReading, RiskPrediction, Alert, AuditLog
from app.ml.predict import predict_landslide_risk
from app.services.alert_service import evaluate_and_create_alert
from app.seed_data import ZONES_SEED

# Global in-memory live timeline store
EVENT_TIMELINE = [
    {
        "timestamp": datetime.datetime.utcnow().strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
        "type": "SYSTEM",
        "title": "AVERTIS System Baseline Initialized",
        "detail": "22 NER monitoring zones and 88 IoT telemetry streams operational."
    }
]

def add_timeline_event(event_type: str, title: str, detail: str):
    global EVENT_TIMELINE
    EVENT_TIMELINE.insert(0, {
        "timestamp": datetime.datetime.utcnow().strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
        "type": event_type,
        "title": title,
        "detail": detail
    })
    if len(EVENT_TIMELINE) > 40:
        EVENT_TIMELINE = EVENT_TIMELINE[:40]

def get_event_timeline():
    return EVENT_TIMELINE

def simulate_heavy_rainfall(db: Session, target_zone_ids: list[str] = None):
    """
    SIMULATE HEAVY RAINFALL:
    1. Rainfall increases (155-185mm)
    2. Soil moisture increases (85-92%)
    3. AI recalculates risk
    4. Vulnerable zones become HIGH / CRITICAL
    5. Alerts are generated & Event timeline records changes
    """
    if not target_zone_ids:
        target_zone_ids = ["ZONE-NER-001", "ZONE-NER-004", "ZONE-NER-005", "ZONE-NER-015"]
        
    add_timeline_event("WEATHER", "Heavy Monsoon Influx Detected", "24h rainfall rate surged past 150mm across multiple NER hill ridges.")
    
    updated_zones = []
    for zone_id in target_zone_ids:
        zone = db.query(MonitoringZone).filter(MonitoringZone.id == zone_id).first()
        if not zone:
            continue
            
        # Update sensors in this zone
        rain_val = 168.5
        soil_val = 88.0
        
        for s in zone.sensors:
            if "RAIN" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = rain_val
                s.last_seen = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
            elif "SOIL" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = soil_val
                s.last_seen = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
                
        # Store reading logs
        db.add(SensorReading(
            sensor_id=f"SENS-{zone.id[9:]}-RAIN-01",
            zone_id=zone.id,
            rainfall_24h=rain_val,
            soil_moisture=soil_val,
            battery_level=90,
            signal_strength=-62,
            is_simulated=True
        ))

        # Recalculate AI Risk
        features = {
            "zone_id": zone.id,
            "rainfall_24h": rain_val,
            "rainfall_72h": rain_val * 1.7,
            "rainfall_7d": rain_val * 2.5,
            "soil_moisture": soil_val,
            "slope_degree": zone.slope_degree,
            "elevation": zone.elevation,
            "historical_landslide_count": zone.historical_landslide_count,
            "distance_to_previous_landslide": zone.distance_to_previous_landslide,
            "satellite_change_indicator": zone.satellite_change_indicator,
            "citizen_report_count": zone.citizen_report_count,
            "tilt_angle": 1.2,
            "vibration_level": 0.08
        }
        
        pred = predict_landslide_risk(features)
        
        old_level = zone.current_risk_level
        zone.current_risk_score = pred["risk_score"]
        zone.current_risk_level = pred["risk_level"]
        zone.updated_at = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
        
        # Risk history
        db.add(RiskPrediction(
            zone_id=zone.id,
            risk_probability=pred["risk_probability"],
            risk_score=pred["risk_score"],
            risk_level=pred["risk_level"],
            contributors_json=json.dumps(pred["contributors"]),
            explainability_summary=pred["explainability_summary"],
            model_version=pred["model_version"],
            is_simulated=True
        ))
        
        # Trigger alert
        alert = evaluate_and_create_alert(
            db=db,
            zone=zone,
            risk_score=pred["risk_score"],
            risk_level=pred["risk_level"],
            contributors=pred["contributors"],
            summary=pred["explainability_summary"],
            recs=pred["recommendations"]
        )
        
        add_timeline_event(
            "RISK",
            f"AI Risk Escalated: {zone.name}",
            f"Risk score rose from {old_level} to {pred['risk_level']} ({pred['risk_score']}/100) due to heavy rainfall & high soil moisture."
        )
        if alert:
            add_timeline_event(
                "ALERT",
                f"Emergency Early Warning Generated ({alert.severity})",
                f"Alert {alert.id} broadcast for {zone.district}: {alert.reason[:80]}..."
            )
            
        updated_zones.append(zone.id)
        
    db.commit()
    return {"status": "SUCCESS", "scenario": "HEAVY_RAINFALL", "updated_zones": updated_zones}

def simulate_extreme_rainfall(db: Session):
    """SIMULATE EXTREME RAINFALL: Widespread torrential monsoon surge (210-250mm)."""
    add_timeline_event("WEATHER", "Extreme Cloudburst Scenario Triggered", "Widespread extreme precipitation recorded (>220mm/24h) across NER.")
    all_zones = db.query(MonitoringZone).all()
    updated = []
    
    for zone in all_zones:
        r_val = 225.0 if zone.slope_degree > 32 else 140.0
        s_val = 94.0 if zone.slope_degree > 32 else 80.0
        
        for s in zone.sensors:
            if "RAIN" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = r_val
            elif "SOIL" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = s_val
                
        features = {
            "zone_id": zone.id,
            "rainfall_24h": r_val,
            "rainfall_72h": r_val * 1.8,
            "rainfall_7d": r_val * 2.8,
            "soil_moisture": s_val,
            "slope_degree": zone.slope_degree,
            "elevation": zone.elevation,
            "historical_landslide_count": zone.historical_landslide_count,
            "distance_to_previous_landslide": zone.distance_to_previous_landslide,
            "satellite_change_indicator": zone.satellite_change_indicator,
            "citizen_report_count": zone.citizen_report_count,
            "tilt_angle": 1.6,
            "vibration_level": 0.12
        }
        pred = predict_landslide_risk(features)
        zone.current_risk_score = pred["risk_score"]
        zone.current_risk_level = pred["risk_level"]
        zone.updated_at = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
        
        evaluate_and_create_alert(
            db=db,
            zone=zone,
            risk_score=pred["risk_score"],
            risk_level=pred["risk_level"],
            contributors=pred["contributors"],
            summary=pred["explainability_summary"],
            recs=pred["recommendations"]
        )
        updated.append(zone.id)
        
    add_timeline_event("RISK", "Multiple Zones Upgraded to CRITICAL", f"{len(updated)} zones updated with extreme hazard probability.")
    db.commit()
    return {"status": "SUCCESS", "scenario": "EXTREME_RAINFALL", "updated_zones_count": len(updated)}

def simulate_ground_movement(db: Session, target_zone_ids: list[str] = None):
    """
    SIMULATE GROUND MOVEMENT:
    1. Tilt increases (3.8° - 4.6°)
    2. Vibration level surges (0.55 - 0.85g)
    3. AI recalculates with IoT strain factors
    4. Proves IoT sensing value beyond rainfall alone
    """
    if not target_zone_ids:
        target_zone_ids = ["ZONE-NER-009", "ZONE-NER-012", "ZONE-NER-004"]
        
    add_timeline_event("IOT", "Slope Inclinometer & Vibration Spike", "MPU6050 shear displacement and micro-seismic activity detected.")
    
    updated_zones = []
    for zone_id in target_zone_ids:
        zone = db.query(MonitoringZone).filter(MonitoringZone.id == zone_id).first()
        if not zone:
            continue
            
        tilt_val = 4.25
        vib_val = 0.68
        
        for s in zone.sensors:
            if "TILT" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = tilt_val
                s.last_seen = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
            elif "VIB" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = vib_val
                s.last_seen = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
                
        features = {
            "zone_id": zone.id,
            "rainfall_24h": 65.0,
            "rainfall_72h": 110.0,
            "rainfall_7d": 190.0,
            "soil_moisture": 72.0,
            "slope_degree": zone.slope_degree,
            "elevation": zone.elevation,
            "historical_landslide_count": zone.historical_landslide_count,
            "distance_to_previous_landslide": zone.distance_to_previous_landslide,
            "satellite_change_indicator": 0.45,
            "citizen_report_count": zone.citizen_report_count,
            "tilt_angle": tilt_val,
            "vibration_level": vib_val
        }
        
        pred = predict_landslide_risk(features)
        
        old_level = zone.current_risk_level
        zone.current_risk_score = pred["risk_score"]
        zone.current_risk_level = pred["risk_level"]
        zone.updated_at = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
        
        db.add(RiskPrediction(
            zone_id=zone.id,
            risk_probability=pred["risk_probability"],
            risk_score=pred["risk_score"],
            risk_level=pred["risk_level"],
            contributors_json=json.dumps(pred["contributors"]),
            explainability_summary=pred["explainability_summary"],
            model_version=pred["model_version"],
            is_simulated=True
        ))
        
        alert = evaluate_and_create_alert(
            db=db,
            zone=zone,
            risk_score=pred["risk_score"],
            risk_level=pred["risk_level"],
            contributors=pred["contributors"],
            summary=pred["explainability_summary"],
            recs=pred["recommendations"]
        )
        
        add_timeline_event(
            "RISK",
            f"Active Slope Creep in {zone.name}",
            f"Tilt reached {tilt_val}° with ground vibration {vib_val}g. Risk upgraded to {pred['risk_level']} ({pred['risk_score']}/100)."
        )
        if alert:
            add_timeline_event(
                "ALERT",
                f"Emergency Warning Broadcast ({alert.severity})",
                f"Alert {alert.id} triggered by active ground displacement."
            )
            
        updated_zones.append(zone.id)
        
    db.commit()
    return {"status": "SUCCESS", "scenario": "GROUND_MOVEMENT", "updated_zones": updated_zones}

def simulate_sensor_failure(db: Session, sensor_id: str = None):
    """
    SIMULATE SENSOR FAILURE:
    1. Stops readings from selected sensor
    2. Marks status as OFFLINE
    3. AI engine gracefully continues using available inputs with reduced confidence notice
    """
    if not sensor_id:
        sensor = db.query(SensorDevice).filter(SensorDevice.sensor_type == "SOIL_MOISTURE").first()
    else:
        sensor = db.query(SensorDevice).filter(SensorDevice.id == sensor_id).first()
        
    if not sensor:
        return {"status": "ERROR", "message": "Sensor not found"}
        
    sensor.status = "OFFLINE"
    sensor.signal_strength = -110
    
    add_timeline_event(
        "IOT",
        f"Sensor Outage: {sensor.id}",
        f"Node {sensor.id} ({sensor.sensor_type}) stopped transmitting. Status transitioned to OFFLINE. AI engine operating in imputation fallback mode."
    )
    
    db.commit()
    return {
        "status": "SUCCESS",
        "scenario": "SENSOR_FAILURE",
        "sensor_id": sensor.id,
        "new_status": "OFFLINE",
        "system_resilience": "AI inference maintained via surrogate hydrological estimation."
    }

def reset_simulation(db: Session):
    """
    RESET SIMULATION:
    Returns all zones, sensors, alerts to baseline state.
    """
    add_timeline_event("SYSTEM", "Reset Simulation Executed", "Restoring baseline environmental values and clearing demo simulation alerts.")
    
    # Reset zones from ZONES_SEED
    for z in ZONES_SEED:
        zone = db.query(MonitoringZone).filter(MonitoringZone.id == z["id"]).first()
        if not zone:
            continue
            
        # Reset sensors
        for s in zone.sensors:
            s.status = "ONLINE"
            s.battery_level = 92
            s.signal_strength = -64
            if "SOIL" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = z["soil"]
            elif "RAIN" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = z["r24"]
            elif "TILT" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = z["tilt"]
            elif "VIB" in s.sensor_type:
                s.latest_reading = z["vib"]
                
        # Recalculate baseline risk
        features = {
            "zone_id": zone.id,
            "rainfall_24h": z["r24"],
            "rainfall_72h": z["r24"] * 1.8,
            "rainfall_7d": z["r24"] * 3.0,
            "soil_moisture": z["soil"],
            "slope_degree": z["slope"],
            "elevation": z["elevation"],
            "historical_landslide_count": z["hist"],
            "distance_to_previous_landslide": z["dist"],
            "satellite_change_indicator": z["sat"],
            "citizen_report_count": 0,
            "tilt_angle": z["tilt"],
            "vibration_level": z["vib"]
        }
        pred = predict_landslide_risk(features)
        
        zone.current_risk_score = pred["risk_score"]
        zone.current_risk_level = pred["risk_level"]
        zone.updated_at = datetime.datetime.utcnow()

    # Clear excess alerts generated during simulation (keep only the 2 initial seed ones)
    db.query(Alert).filter(~Alert.id.in_(["ALT-2026-001", "ALT-2026-002"])).delete(synchronize_session=False)
    
    db.commit()
    return {"status": "SUCCESS", "scenario": "RESET", "message": "Baseline restored."}

```

```python
import re
import logging
import secrets
from typing import Dict, Any, Tuple
import requests
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger("avertis.sms")
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)

def generate_secure_otp() -> str:
    """
    Generates a cryptographically strong, non-predictable 6-digit random numeric OTP.
    Utilizes Python's secrets module (CSPRNG).
    """
    return f"{secrets.randbelow(900000) + 100000}"

def extract_10_digit_phone(phone: str) -> str:
    """Extracts raw 10-digit Indian phone number suitable for domestic SMS gateways."""
    digits = re.sub(r'\D', '', str(phone))
    return digits[-10:] if len(digits) >= 10 else digits

def send_sms_otp(phone_number: str, otp_code: str) -> Tuple[bool, str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Dispatches 6-digit OTP to the requested mobile number via configured SMS Gateway.
    
    Returns:
        (success: bool, delivery_summary: str, provider_response: dict)
    """
    raw_10_digit = extract_10_digit_phone(phone_number)
    normalized_e164 = phone_number if phone_number.startswith('+') else f"+91{raw_10_digit}"
    
    # 1. If SMS Gateway is disabled or API Key not provided, fallback to simulated dispatch
    if not settings.SMS_ENABLED or not settings.SMS_API_KEY:
        logger.info(
            f"[SMS DISPATCH SIMULATION] Provider: {settings.SMS_GATEWAY_PROVIDER} | "
            f"To: {normalized_e164} | Code: {otp_code} | Mode: LOCAL_PROTOTYPE"
        )
        return (
            True,
            f"Simulated SMS dispatch to {normalized_e164} (SMS_ENABLED=false)",
            {"status": "SIMULATED", "phone": normalized_e164, "otp": otp_code}
        )

    provider = settings.SMS_GATEWAY_PROVIDER.upper()
    api_key = settings.SMS_API_KEY.strip()

    try:
        # 2. FAST2SMS Provider Integration
        if provider == "FAST2SMS":
            url = "https://www.fast2sms.com/dev/bulkV2"
            headers = {
                "authorization": api_key,
                "Content-Type": "application/json"
            }
            
            # OTP route payload
            if settings.SMS_ROUTE.lower() == "otp":
                payload = {
                    "variables_values": otp_code,
                    "route": "otp",
                    "numbers": raw_10_digit
                }
            else:
                payload = {
                    "route": settings.SMS_ROUTE,
                    "message": f"Your AVERTIS Disaster Intelligence verification code is {otp_code}. Valid for 5 minutes. Do not share.",
                    "language": "english",
                    "flash": 0,
                    "numbers": raw_10_digit
                }

            response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=6)
            res_data = response.json() if response.headers.get("content-type", "").startswith("application/json") else {"text": response.text}

            if response.status_code == 200 and res_data.get("return") is True:
                logger.info(f"[SMS FAST2SMS SUCCESS] Sent OTP to {normalized_e164}. Request ID: {res_data.get('request_id')}")
                return True, f"OTP SMS delivered to {normalized_e164} via Fast2SMS", res_data
            else:
                logger.warning(f"[SMS FAST2SMS FAILED] {response.status_code}: {res_data}")
                return False, f"Fast2SMS delivery error: {res_data.get('message', 'Unknown error')}", res_data

        # 3. MSG91 Provider Integration
        elif provider == "MSG91":
            url = "https://control.msg91.com/api/v5/otp"
            headers = {
                "authkey": api_key,
                "Content-Type": "application/json"
            }
            payload = {
                "template_id": settings.SMS_TEMPLATE_ID or "65f0a0b123456789",
                "mobile": normalized_e164.replace('+', ''),
                "otp": otp_code
            }

            response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=6)
            res_data = response.json() if response.headers.get("content-type", "").startswith("application/json") else {"text": response.text}

            if response.status_code == 200 and res_data.get("type") == "success":
                logger.info(f"[SMS MSG91 SUCCESS] Sent OTP to {normalized_e164}. Message: {res_data.get('message')}")
                return True, f"OTP SMS delivered to {normalized_e164} via MSG91", res_data
            else:
                logger.warning(f"[SMS MSG91 FAILED] {response.status_code}: {res_data}")
                return False, f"MSG91 delivery error: {res_data.get('message', 'Unknown error')}", res_data

        # 4. Fallback Generic Webhook / Provider
        else:
            logger.warning(f"[SMS PROVIDER UNKNOWN] Provider '{provider}' not directly supported. Logging simulation.")
            return (
                True,
                f"Simulated dispatch for provider {provider}",
                {"status": "SIMULATED", "provider": provider, "phone": normalized_e164}
            )

    except requests.RequestException as req_err:
        logger.error(f"[SMS GATEWAY NETWORK ERROR] {req_err}")
        return False, f"SMS Gateway connection timeout / error: {str(req_err)}", {"error": str(req_err)}
    except Exception as ex:
        logger.error(f"[SMS GATEWAY UNEXPECTED ERROR] {ex}")
        return False, f"SMS Gateway unexpected exception: {str(ex)}", {"error": str(ex)}

```

```python
# Helper to generate polygon GeoJSON around center (lat, lon)
def make_geojson_polygon(lat: float, lon: float, offset: float = 0.025) -> str:
    coords = [
        [lon - offset, lat - offset * 0.7],
        [lon + offset, lat - offset * 0.7],
        [lon + offset * 1.2, lat + offset * 0.8],
        [lon - offset * 0.8, lat + offset * 0.9],
        [lon - offset, lat - offset * 0.7]
    ]
    geojson = {
        "type": "Polygon",
        "coordinates": [coords]
    }
    return json.dumps(geojson)

def seed_database(db: Session):
    """Populates all required DEMO entities for SIH26001 Prototype."""
    # Ensure existing seeded demo users have phone numbers mapped
    demo_phone_updates = {
        "admin": "+919876543210",
        "authority_ner": "+919436100001",
        "authority": "+919436100001",
        "field_shillong": "+919436123456",
        "field_gangtok": "+919436123457",
        "citizen_demo": "+919862099887",
        "citizen": "+919862099887"
    }
    for uname, phone in demo_phone_updates.items():
        existing_u = db.query(User).filter(User.username == uname).first()
        if existing_u and not existing_u.phone_number:
            existing_u.phone_number = phone
    db.commit()

    # Check if already seeded
    if db.query(MonitoringZone).count() > 0:
        print("[AVERTIS SEED] Database already populated. Skipping initial seed.")
        return

    print("[AVERTIS SEED] Seeding users, zones, sensors, historical incidents, and alerts...")
    
    # 1. Users
    demo_users = [
        User(username="admin", phone_number="+919876543210", email="admin@avertis.gov.in", hashed_password="pbkdf2_demo_admin_pwd", full_name="State Disaster Controller", role="ADMIN"),
        User(username="authority_ner", phone_number="+919436100001", email="authority@nesac.gov.in", hashed_password="pbkdf2_demo_auth_pwd", full_name="NESAC / SDMA Officer", role="AUTHORITY"),
        User(username="field_shillong", phone_number="+919436123456", email="field.shillong@avertis.gov.in", hashed_password="pbkdf2_demo_field_pwd", full_name="Officer L. Kharkongor", role="FIELD_OFFICER", assigned_zone_id="ZONE-NER-001"),
        User(username="field_gangtok", phone_number="+919436123457", email="field.gangtok@avertis.gov.in", hashed_password="pbkdf2_demo_field_pwd", full_name="Officer D. Bhutia", role="FIELD_OFFICER", assigned_zone_id="ZONE-NER-004"),
        User(username="citizen_demo", phone_number="+919862099887", email="citizen@gmail.com", hashed_password="pbkdf2_demo_cit_pwd", full_name="Community Reporter", role="CITIZEN")
    ]
    db.add_all(demo_users)
    db.commit()

    # 2. Zones & Sensors & Environmental Data & AI Predictions
    for z in ZONES_SEED:
        zone_id = z["id"]
        geojson = make_geojson_polygon(z["lat"], z["lon"])
        
        # Calculate AI Risk for initial state
        features = {
            "zone_id": zone_id,
            "rainfall_24h": z["r24"],
            "rainfall_72h": z["r24"] * 1.8,
            "rainfall_7d": z["r24"] * 3.0,
            "soil_moisture": z["soil"],
            "slope_degree": z["slope"],
            "elevation": z["elevation"],
            "historical_landslide_count": z["hist"],
            "distance_to_previous_landslide": z["dist"],
            "satellite_change_indicator": z["sat"],
            "citizen_report_count": 0,
            "tilt_angle": z["tilt"],
            "vibration_level": z["vib"]
        }
        pred = predict_landslide_risk(features)
        
        zone = MonitoringZone(
            id=zone_id,
            name=z["name"],
            district=z["district"],
            state=z["state"],
            latitude=z["lat"],
            longitude=z["lon"],
            boundary_geojson=geojson,
            slope_degree=z["slope"],
            elevation=z["elevation"],
            lithology_type=z["lithology"],
            vegetation_cover=z["veg"],
            historical_landslide_count=z["hist"],
            distance_to_previous_landslide=z["dist"],
            satellite_change_indicator=z["sat"],
            citizen_report_count=0,
            current_risk_score=pred["risk_score"],
            current_risk_level=pred["risk_level"],
            active=True
        )
        db.add(zone)
        db.commit()

        # Add 4 sensors for this zone (SOIL, RAIN, TILT, VIB)
        sensor_configs = [
            ("SOIL", "SOIL_MOISTURE", "%", z["soil"], "SENS-" + zone_id[9:] + "-SOIL-01", 0.003, 0.002),
            ("RAIN", "RAIN_GAUGE", "mm", z["r24"], "SENS-" + zone_id[9:] + "-RAIN-01", -0.002, 0.004),
            ("TILT", "TILT_MPU6050", "°", z["tilt"], "SENS-" + zone_id[9:] + "-TILT-01", 0.001, -0.003),
            ("VIB", "VIBRATION_PIEZO", "g", z["vib"], "SENS-" + zone_id[9:] + "-VIB-01", -0.003, -0.002),
        ]
        
        for prefix, stype, unit, val, sid, lat_off, lon_off in sensor_configs:
            # Add sensor
            s_dev = SensorDevice(
                id=sid,
                zone_id=zone_id,
                sensor_type=stype,
                model_name="ESP32-LoRa-SensorNode-V2",
                unit=unit,
                latitude=z["lat"] + lat_off,
                longitude=z["lon"] + lon_off,
                latest_reading=val,
                battery_level=92,
                signal_strength=-64,
                status="ONLINE",
                is_simulated=True
            )
            db.add(s_dev)
            
            # Initial reading
            s_reading = SensorReading(
                sensor_id=sid,
                zone_id=zone_id,
                soil_moisture=z["soil"] if "SOIL" in stype else 0.0,
                rainfall_24h=z["r24"] if "RAIN" in stype else 0.0,
                tilt_angle=z["tilt"] if "TILT" in stype else 0.0,
                vibration_level=z["vib"] if "VIB" in stype else 0.0,
                battery_level=92,
                signal_strength=-64,
                is_simulated=True
            )
            db.add(s_reading)

        # Environmental log
        env_log = EnvironmentalReading(
            zone_id=zone_id,
            rainfall_24h=z["r24"],
            rainfall_72h=z["r24"] * 1.8,
            rainfall_7d=z["r24"] * 3.0,
            soil_moisture=z["soil"],
            tilt_angle=z["tilt"],
            vibration_level=z["vib"],
            slope_degree=z["slope"],
            elevation=z["elevation"],
            historical_landslide_count=z["hist"],
            distance_to_previous_landslide=z["dist"],
            satellite_change_indicator=z["sat"],
            citizen_report_count=0
        )
        db.add(env_log)

        # Risk Prediction record
        risk_rec = RiskPrediction(
            zone_id=zone_id,
            risk_probability=pred["risk_probability"],
            risk_score=pred["risk_score"],
            risk_level=pred["risk_level"],
            contributors_json=json.dumps(pred["contributors"]),
            explainability_summary=pred["explainability_summary"],
            model_version=pred["model_version"],
            is_simulated=True
        )
        db.add(risk_rec)
        db.commit()

    # 3. Seed Realistic Historical Incidents
    incidents = [
        LandslideIncident(
            id="INC-2026-001",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-015",
            title="Tupul Yard Debris Flow & Embankment Breach",
            description="Major slope failure triggered by continuous monsoon precipitation. Highway blocked for 36 hours. Emergency clearance cleared by SDRF.",
            location_name="Tupul Railway Cut Km 42, Noney District",
            latitude=24.8420,
            longitude=93.6320,
            severity="CRITICAL",
            reporter_name="Inspector S. Haokip",
            reporter_role="FIELD_OFFICER",
            verification_status="RESOLVED",
            timestamp=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(days=12),
            verified_by="SDMA Manipur Directorate",
            resolution_notes="Geotechnical retaining wall reinforcement initiated."
        ),
        LandslideIncident(
            id="INC-2026-002",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-005",
            title="Dikchu-Mangan Road Slope Slump",
            description="Regolith slip following 110mm rainfall event. Partial road subsidence observed.",
            location_name="NH-310A Milepost 18, North Sikkim",
            latitude=27.5020,
            longitude=88.5420,
            severity="HIGH",
            reporter_name="Officer T. Lepcha",
            reporter_role="FIELD_OFFICER",
            verification_status="VERIFIED",
            timestamp=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(days=4),
            verified_by="GSI Gangtok Regional Centre"
        ),
        LandslideIncident(
            id="INC-2026-003",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-009",
            title="Laipuitlang Crest Tension Crack Development",
            description="Active ground tension crack spanning 45m along upper residential road terrace.",
            location_name="Laipuitlang Hillcrest, Aizawl",
            latitude=23.7430,
            longitude=92.7240,
            severity="HIGH",
            reporter_name="Officer R. Lalrinzuala",
            reporter_role="FIELD_OFFICER",
            verification_status="UNDER_REVIEW",
            timestamp=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(days=1)
        )
    ]
    db.add_all(incidents)

    # 4. Seed Realistic Citizen Reports
    citizen_reports = [
        CitizenReport(
            id="CR-2026-001",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-004",
            report_type="Ground Crack",
            description="Noticed fresh 3-inch wide fissure across roadside drainage wall after morning shower.",
            location_name="Near Tathangchen Lower School Road, Gangtok",
            latitude=27.3460,
            longitude=88.6210,
            severity="HIGH",
            reporter_name="Pema Sherpa",
            reporter_phone="+91 98451 22345",
            reporter_role="CITIZEN",
            status="UNDER_REVIEW",
            timestamp=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(hours=6)
        ),
        CitizenReport(
            id="CR-2026-002",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-001",
            report_type="Rockfall",
            description="Multiple boulders detached from overhang onto road shoulder.",
            location_name="Mawsmai Cave Road Corridor, Cherrapunji",
            latitude=25.2980,
            longitude=91.6980,
            severity="MEDIUM",
            reporter_name="B. Marbaniang",
            reporter_phone="+91 97740 88123",
            reporter_role="CITIZEN",
            status="VERIFIED",
            timestamp=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(hours=18)
        ),
        CitizenReport(
            id="CR-2026-003",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-012",
            report_type="Slope Movement",
            description="Mud oozing from retaining wall weep holes; road surface slightly heaving.",
            location_name="Phesama Checkpost Curve, Kohima",
            latitude=25.6410,
            longitude=94.1160,
            severity="HIGH",
            reporter_name="K. Angami",
            reporter_phone="+91 94360 44901",
            reporter_role="CITIZEN",
            status="UNVERIFIED",
            timestamp=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(hours=2)
        )
    ]
    db.add_all(citizen_reports)

    # 5. Seed Demonstration Alerts
    demo_alerts = [
        Alert(
            id="ALT-2026-001",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-015",
            district="Noney",
            severity="HIGH",
            risk_score=72.5,
            title="AVERTIS DEMONSTRATION ALERT: High Landslide Vulnerability",
            reason="Continuous rainfall (82mm/24h) + High soil saturation (82%) + Inclinometer tilt (1.5°)",
            message="Zone Tupul-Noney Railway Incline Sector crossed HIGH hazard threshold. Vulnerability heightened by steep terrain gradient and saturated regolith.",
            recommended_actions_json=json.dumps([
                "Escalate sensor telemetry monitoring interval from 15m to 2m.",
                "Issue advisory to local administrative circles and village disaster committees.",
                "Inspect culverts and slope drainage channels for debris blockages."
            ]),
            status="ACTIVE",
            created_at=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(hours=3)
        ),
        Alert(
            id="ALT-2026-002",
            zone_id="ZONE-NER-005",
            district="North Sikkim",
            severity="HIGH",
            risk_score=68.0,
            title="AVERTIS DEMONSTRATION ALERT: Slope Saturation Warning",
            reason="Rainfall accumulation 85mm/24h with high pore water pressure on 46° slope gradient",
            message="Dikchu-Mangan Highway Sector (NH-310A) exhibiting elevated slip probability.",
            recommended_actions_json=json.dumps([
                "Deploy patrol to monitor road subsidence.",
                "Alert emergency clearing crew at Chungthang staging node."
            ]),
            status="ACTIVE",
            created_at=datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(hours=5)

    ]
    db.add_all(demo_alerts)

    # 6. Audit Log
    db.add(AuditLog(
        action="SYSTEM_INIT_SEED",
        entity_type="SYSTEM",
        entity_id="SEED-001",
        details_json=json.dumps({"status": "SUCCESS", "zones_count": len(ZONES_SEED), "notice": "DEMO DATA"})
    ))

    db.commit()
    print("[AVERTIS SEED] Database seeding completed successfully.")
```

```python
import app.main
from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.models import MonitoringZone, SensorDevice, Alert, CitizenReport, LandslideIncident
from app.ml.predict import predict_landslide_risk
from app.services.simulation_service import simulate_heavy_rainfall, reset_simulation

db = SessionLocal()
try:
    zones_cnt = db.query(MonitoringZone).count()
    sensors_cnt = db.query(SensorDevice).count()
    alerts_cnt = db.query(Alert).count()
    reports_cnt = db.query(CitizenReport).count()
    incidents_cnt = db.query(LandslideIncident).count()
    
    print("[1] Database Entities:")
    print(f"    - Monitoring Zones: {zones_cnt} (22 across 8 NER states)")
    print(f"    - IoT Sensor Nodes: {sensors_cnt} (4 per zone: Soil, Rain, Tilt, Vib)")
    print(f"    - Demonstration Alerts: {alerts_cnt}")
    print(f"    - Citizen Reports: {reports_cnt}")
    print(f"    - Verified Landslide Incidents: {incidents_cnt}")

    # Test ML Predictor
    pred = predict_landslide_risk({
        'rainfall_24h': 160.0,
        'rainfall_72h': 280.0,
        'rainfall_7d': 450.0,
        'soil_moisture': 88.0,
        'slope_degree': 38.5,
        'elevation': 1430.0,
        'historical_landslide_count': 7,
        'distance_to_previous_landslide': 0.8,
        'satellite_change_indicator': 0.35,
        'citizen_report_count': 1,
        'tilt_angle': 2.5,
        'vibration_level': 0.12
    })
    print("\n[2] AI/ML Risk Engine:")
    print(f"    - Risk Score: {pred['risk_score']} / 100")
    print(f"    - Classification: {pred['risk_level']}")
    print(f"    - Contributors: {pred['contributors']}")

    # Test Simulation Service
    sim = simulate_heavy_rainfall(db)
    print("\n[3] Simulation Engine:")
    print(f"    - Trigger Heavy Rainfall: {sim['status']} (Zones modified: {sim['updated_zones']})")
    
    reset = reset_simulation(db)
    print(f"    - Reset Simulation: {reset['status']} (Restored to baseline)")

    print("\n=======================================================")
    print(">>> COMPLETE AVERTIS SYSTEM VERIFIED & FULLY OPERATIONAL <<<")
    print("=======================================================")
finally:
    db.close()

```

<a id="file-prototype-frontend-src-app-tsx"></a>
## File 28/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/App.tsx`

- **Path:** `Prototype/frontend/src/App.tsx`
- **Category:** 07. Frontend - Application Shell, Layout & Styling
- **Language:** tsx
- **Lines:** 412
- **Size:** 13,620 bytes

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { App as CapApp } from '@capacitor/app';
import { 
  MonitoringZone, 
  SensorDevice, 
  AlertItem, 
  CitizenReport, 
  LandslideIncident, 
  DashboardStats, 
  TimelineEvent, 
  UserRole,
  AuthUser
} from './types';
import { api } from './services/api';

// Components
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { SimulationToolbar } from './components/SimulationToolbar';
import { MetricsCards } from './components/MetricsCards';
import { GISMap } from './components/GISMap';
import { ZoneDetailPanel } from './components/ZoneDetailPanel';
import { EventTimeline } from './components/EventTimeline';
import { SensorsView } from './components/SensorsView';
import { AlertsView } from './components/AlertsView';
import { CitizenReportingView } from './components/CitizenReportingView';
import { FieldOfficerMode } from './components/FieldOfficerMode';
import { IncidentsView } from './components/IncidentsView';
import { AnalyticsView } from './components/AnalyticsView';
import { MonitoringZonesView } from './components/MonitoringZonesView';
import { ReferencesView } from './components/ReferencesView';
import { SystemHealthView } from './components/SystemHealthView';
import { LandingPage } from './components/LandingPage';
import { GlobalSearchModal } from './components/GlobalSearchModal';
import { QuickTourModal } from './components/QuickTourModal';
import { LoginModal } from './components/LoginModal';

export const App: React.FC = () => {
  const [currentTab, setCurrentTab] = useState<string>('map');
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<AuthUser | null>(() => api.getStoredUser());
  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<UserRole>(() => {
    const stored = api.getStoredUser();
    return stored?.role || 'ADMIN';
  });
  const [isAuthOpen, setIsAuthOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Theme State: defaults to 'light'
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<'light' | 'dark'>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('avertis_theme');
    return (saved === 'dark' || saved === 'light') ? saved : 'light';
  });

  // Synchronize document element class with active theme
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (theme === 'dark') {
      root.classList.add('dark');
      root.classList.remove('light');
    } else {
      root.classList.add('light');
      root.classList.remove('dark');
    }
    localStorage.setItem('avertis_theme', theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme((prev) => (prev === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'));
  };

  // Application Data States
  const [zones, setZones] = useState<MonitoringZone[]>([]);
  const [sensors, setSensors] = useState<SensorDevice[]>([]);
  const [alerts, setAlerts] = useState<AlertItem[]>([]);
  const [reports, setReports] = useState<CitizenReport[]>([]);
  const [incidents, setIncidents] = useState<LandslideIncident[]>([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<DashboardStats | null>(null);
  const [timeline, setTimeline] = useState<TimelineEvent[]>([]);
  const [selectedZone, setSelectedZone] = useState<MonitoringZone | null>(null);

  // Modal States
  const [isSearchOpen, setIsSearchOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isTourOpen, setIsTourOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isStandalone, setIsStandalone] = useState<boolean>(api.isStandalone());

  // Loading & Error States
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [errorNotice, setErrorNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Master Data Refresh Callback
  const refreshAllData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [
        fetchedZones,
        fetchedSensors,
        fetchedAlerts,
        fetchedReports,
        fetchedIncidents,
        fetchedStats,
        fetchedTimeline
      ] = await Promise.all([
        api.getZones(),
        api.getSensors(),
        api.getAlerts(),
        api.getReports(),
        api.getIncidents(),
        api.getDashboardStats(),
        api.getTimeline()
      ]);

      setZones(fetchedZones);
      setSensors(fetchedSensors);
      setAlerts(fetchedAlerts);
      setReports(fetchedReports);
      setIncidents(fetchedIncidents);
      setStats(fetchedStats);
      setTimeline(fetchedTimeline);

      // Update selected zone if active
      if (selectedZone) {
        const updated = fetchedZones.find((z) => z.id === selectedZone.id);
        if (updated) setSelectedZone(updated);
      } else if (fetchedZones.length > 0) {
        setSelectedZone(fetchedZones[0]);
      }
      setErrorNotice(null);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn('Backend unavailable, active on standalone demo store:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [selectedZone]);

  // Global Keyboard Shortcuts (Ctrl+K for search)
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setIsSearchOpen((prev) => !prev);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  // Android Back Button handler
  useEffect(() => {
    const backListener = CapApp.addListener('backButton', () => {
      if (isSearchOpen) {
        setIsSearchOpen(false);
      } else if (isTourOpen) {
        setIsTourOpen(false);
      } else if (currentTab !== 'map' && currentTab !== 'landing') {
        setCurrentTab('map');
      } else {
        CapApp.exitApp();
      }
    });

    return () => {
      backListener.then((l) => l.remove()).catch(() => {});
    };
  }, [currentTab, isSearchOpen, isTourOpen]);

  // Initial load & periodic background polling
  useEffect(() => {
    refreshAllData();
    const interval = setInterval(refreshAllData, 10000); // 10s auto-polling
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [refreshAllData]);

  // WebSocket Live Updates Connection
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isStandalone) return;
    let ws: WebSocket | null = null;
    try {
      const baseUrl = api.getBaseUrl();
      const wsUrl = baseUrl.replace(/^http/, 'ws') + '/ws';
      ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl);
      ws.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const payload = JSON.parse(event.data);
          if (payload.event === 'PULSE') {
            refreshAllData();
          }
        } catch {}
      };
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('WebSocket connection failed, falling back to polling.', e);
    }
    return () => {
      if (ws) ws.close();
    };
  }, [refreshAllData, isStandalone]);

  const activeAlertsCount = alerts.filter((a) => a.status === 'ACTIVE').length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 dark:bg-[#070b14] dark:text-slate-100 flex flex-col selection:bg-cyan-500 selection:text-white antialiased transition-colors duration-200">
      {/* Navigation Header */}
      <Navbar
        currentTab={currentTab}
        setCurrentTab={setCurrentTab}
        userRole={userRole}
        setUserRole={(role) => {
          setUserRole(role);
          if (currentUser) {
            const updated = { ...currentUser, role };
            setCurrentUser(updated);
            api.setStoredUser(updated);
          }
        }}
        activeAlertsCount={activeAlertsCount}
        onOpenSearch={() => setIsSearchOpen(true)}
        onOpenTour={() => setIsTourOpen(true)}
        isStandalone={isStandalone}
        onToggleStandalone={(val) => {
          setIsStandalone(val);
          refreshAllData();
        }}
        theme={theme}
        onToggleTheme={toggleTheme}
        currentUser={currentUser}
        onOpenLogin={() => setIsAuthOpen(true)}
      />

      {/* Global Search Modal */}
      <GlobalSearchModal
        isOpen={isSearchOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsSearchOpen(false)}
        zones={zones}
        sensors={sensors}
        alerts={alerts}
        onSelectZone={(zone) => setSelectedZone(zone)}
        onNavigateTab={(tab) => setCurrentTab(tab)}
      />

      {/* Interactive Quick Tour Modal */}
      <QuickTourModal
        isOpen={isTourOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsTourOpen(false)}
        onNavigateTab={(tab) => setCurrentTab(tab)}
      />

      {/* Mobile OTP Authentication & Registration Modal */}
      <LoginModal
        isOpen={isAuthOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAuthOpen(false)}
        currentUser={currentUser}
        onAuthSuccess={(user) => {
          setCurrentUser(user);
          setUserRole(user.role);
          setIsAuthOpen(false);
          refreshAllData();
        }}
        onLogout={() => {
          api.clearStoredUser();
          setCurrentUser(null);
          setUserRole('CITIZEN');
        }}
      />

      {/* Main Container */}
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-4">
        {/* Global Simulation Toolbar */}
        {currentTab !== 'landing' && (
          <SimulationToolbar onSimulationTriggered={refreshAllData} />
        )}

        {/* View Router */}
        {currentTab === 'landing' && (
          <LandingPage
            onExploreMap={() => setCurrentTab('map')}
            onEnterDashboard={() => setCurrentTab('map')}
            onOpenLogin={() => setIsAuthOpen(true)}
          />
        )}

        {currentTab === 'map' && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-fadeIn">
            {/* HUD Metrics Counters */}
            <MetricsCards stats={stats} />

            {/* GIS Center Stage: Map (Left) + Detail Panel (Right) */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4 items-start">
              <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                <GISMap
                  zones={zones}
                  sensors={sensors}
                  incidents={incidents}
                  reports={reports}
                  selectedZone={selectedZone}
                  onSelectZone={(zone) => setSelectedZone(zone)}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="lg:col-span-1">
                <ZoneDetailPanel
                  zone={selectedZone}
                  onClose={() => setSelectedZone(null)}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Bottom Live Event Timeline */}
            <EventTimeline timeline={timeline} />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'zones' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <MonitoringZonesView
              zones={zones}
              onSelectZone={(zone) => {
                setSelectedZone(zone);
                setCurrentTab('map');
              }}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'sensors' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <SensorsView
              sensors={sensors}
              onRefresh={refreshAllData}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'alerts' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <AlertsView
              alerts={alerts}
              userRole={userRole}
              onSelectZoneId={(zoneId) => {
                const target = zones.find((z) => z.id === zoneId);
                if (target) setSelectedZone(target);
                setCurrentTab('map');
              }}
              onRefresh={refreshAllData}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'citizen' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <CitizenReportingView
              reports={reports}
              onReportSubmitted={refreshAllData}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'field' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <FieldOfficerMode
              zones={zones}
              alerts={alerts}
              onRefresh={refreshAllData}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'incidents' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <IncidentsView
              incidents={incidents}
              reports={reports}
              userRole={userRole}
              onRefresh={refreshAllData}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'analytics' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <AnalyticsView />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'health' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <SystemHealthView stats={stats} />
          </div>
        )}

        {currentTab === 'references' && (
          <div className="animate-fadeIn">
            <ReferencesView />
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="bg-white border-t border-slate-200 dark:bg-[#050810] dark:border-slate-800/80 py-4 px-4 text-center text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-500 transition-colors duration-200">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">AVERTIS</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>Smart India Hackathon 2026 (SIH26001)</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span className="text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 font-bold">Team NEXORA</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            North Eastern Region Landslide Risk Monitoring & Early Warning Decision Support Platform
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
};

export default App;

```

```css
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px -5px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35);
}
.shadow-glow-amber {
  box-shadow: 0 0 18px -5px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35);
}

}

  to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
}

  animation: spinSlow 12s linear infinite;
}

  0% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; }
  50% { transform: scale(1.4); opacity: 0.4; }
  100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; }
}
.animate-ping-slow {
  animation: pingSlow 3s cubic-bezier(0, 0, 0.2, 1) infinite;
}

@keyframes fadeIn {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(4px); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}

}
```

## File 31/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/AlertsView.tsx`

- **Path:** `Prototype/frontend/src/components/AlertsView.tsx`
- **Category:** 08. Frontend - UI Components & Views
- **Language:** tsx
- **Lines:** 406
- **Size:** 21,192 bytes

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AlertItem, RiskLevel, UserRole } from '../types';
import { 
  AlertTriangle, 
  ShieldAlert, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Clock, 
  Filter, 
  ExternalLink, 
  Check, 
  RotateCcw, 
  Sparkles, 
  Info 
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../services/api';

interface AlertsViewProps {
  alerts: AlertItem[];
  userRole: UserRole;
  onSelectZoneId: (zoneId: string) => void;
  onRefresh: () => void;
}

export const AlertsView: React.FC<AlertsViewProps> = ({
  alerts,
  userRole,
  onSelectZoneId,
  onRefresh,
}) => {
  const [filterSeverity, setFilterSeverity] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'cards' | 'table'>('cards');
  const [selectedAlertForModal, setSelectedAlertForModal] = useState<AlertItem | null>(null);
  const [actionLoading, setActionLoading] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleAcknowledge = async (alertId: string) => {
    try {
      setActionLoading(alertId);
      await api.acknowledgeAlert(alertId, `${userRole} User`);
      onRefresh();
      if (selectedAlertForModal?.id === alertId) {
        setSelectedAlertForModal((prev) => prev ? { ...prev, status: 'ACKNOWLEDGED' } : null);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(null);
    }
  };

  const handleResolve = async (alertId: string) => {
    try {
      setActionLoading(alertId);
      await api.resolveAlert(alertId, 'Field inspection completed and hazard mitigated.', `${userRole} User`);
      onRefresh();
      if (selectedAlertForModal?.id === alertId) {
        setSelectedAlertForModal((prev) => prev ? { ...prev, status: 'RESOLVED' } : null);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(null);
    }
  };

  const filteredAlerts = alerts.filter((a) => {
    if (filterSeverity !== 'ALL' && a.severity !== filterSeverity) return false;
    if (filterStatus !== 'ALL' && a.status !== filterStatus) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const getSeverityBadge = (sev: RiskLevel) => {
    switch (sev) {
      case 'CRITICAL': return 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-500/40 animate-pulse font-black';
      case 'HIGH': return 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-300 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-400 dark:border-orange-500/40 font-bold';
      case 'MODERATE': return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-400 dark:border-amber-500/40 font-semibold';
      case 'LOW':
      default: return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-500/40 font-semibold';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl transition-colors duration-200">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400" />
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Early Warning & Hazard Alerts Command
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Automated threshold evaluation, early-warning advisories, and disaster-management protocol workflows.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Filters & View Toggle */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-xs">
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode('cards')}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-bold ${
                viewMode === 'cards' ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              Cards
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode('table')}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-bold ${
                viewMode === 'table' ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              Table
            </button>
          </div>

          <select
            value={filterSeverity}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterSeverity(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="ALL">All Severities</option>
            <option value="CRITICAL">CRITICAL</option>
            <option value="HIGH">HIGH</option>
            <option value="MODERATE">MODERATE</option>
          </select>

          <select
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="ALL">All Statuses</option>
            <option value="ACTIVE">ACTIVE</option>
            <option value="ACKNOWLEDGED">ACKNOWLEDGED</option>
            <option value="RESOLVED">RESOLVED</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {viewMode === 'cards' ? (
        /* Alerts Grid / Cards */
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {filteredAlerts.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="col-span-2 text-center py-12 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-slate-500 text-xs shadow-xs">
              No alerts match current filter criteria.
            </div>
          ) : (
            filteredAlerts.map((alert) => (
              <div
                key={alert.id}
                className={`bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col justify-between transition-all hover:shadow-md ${
                  alert.severity === 'CRITICAL'
                    ? 'border-red-300 dark:border-red-500/40 shadow-xs'
                    : alert.severity === 'HIGH'
                    ? 'border-orange-300 dark:border-orange-500/40 shadow-xs'
                    : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] uppercase border ${getSeverityBadge(alert.severity)}`}>
                      {alert.severity} • {alert.risk_score.toFixed(0)}/100
                    </span>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${
                      alert.status === 'ACTIVE'
                        ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border border-red-300 dark:bg-red-950 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-800/60'
                        : alert.status === 'ACKNOWLEDGED'
                        ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800/60'
                        : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800/60'
                    }`}>
                      {alert.status}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-tight">
                    {alert.title}
                  </h3>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                    District: {alert.district} ({alert.zone_id})
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mt-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/60 leading-relaxed font-normal">
                    {alert.reason}
                  </p>

                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-2.5">
                    <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                    <span>Created: {new Date(alert.created_at).toLocaleString('en-IN')}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Action Buttons */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 mt-3 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/80 gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setSelectedAlertForModal(alert)}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-cyan-50 hover:bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-200 dark:bg-cyan-500/10 dark:hover:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/30 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center space-x-1 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>View Protocol →</span>
                  </button>

                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                    {alert.status === 'ACTIVE' && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleAcknowledge(alert.id)}
                        disabled={actionLoading === alert.id}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:hover:bg-amber-500/30 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-500/40 text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs"
                      >
                        Acknowledge
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {alert.status !== 'RESOLVED' && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleResolve(alert.id)}
                        disabled={actionLoading === alert.id}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:hover:bg-emerald-500/30 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/40 text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs"
                      >
                        Resolve
                      </button>
                    )}
                    <button
                      onClick={() => onSelectZoneId(alert.zone_id)}
                      className="p-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-transparent"
                      title="Inspect Zone on Map"
                    >
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Table View */
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/90 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 font-bold">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Severity</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Location</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Risk Score</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Reason</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Created</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Status</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Action</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800/60 font-sans">
                {filteredAlerts.map((alert) => (
                  <tr key={alert.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition-colors">
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold border ${getSeverityBadge(alert.severity)}`}>
                        {alert.severity}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{alert.district}</div>
                      <div className="font-mono text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 text-[10px]">{alert.zone_id}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-sm">
                      {alert.risk_score.toFixed(0)}<span className="text-slate-400 font-normal text-xs">/100</span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 max-w-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs">
                      {alert.reason}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                      {new Date(alert.created_at).toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${
                        alert.status === 'ACTIVE'
                          ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border border-red-300 dark:bg-red-950 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-800/60'
                          : alert.status === 'ACKNOWLEDGED'
                          ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800/60'
                          : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800/60'
                      }`}>
                        {alert.status}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => setSelectedAlertForModal(alert)}
                          className="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-cyan-800 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 dark:text-cyan-300 text-xs font-semibold"
                        >
                          View
                        </button>
                        {alert.status === 'ACTIVE' && (
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleAcknowledge(alert.id)}
                            className="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-300 text-xs font-semibold"
                          >
                            Ack
                          </button>
                        )}
                        {alert.status !== 'RESOLVED' && (
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleResolve(alert.id)}
                            className="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300 text-xs font-semibold"
                          >
                            Resolve
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Demonstration Alert Modal */}
      {selectedAlertForModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1000] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gradient-to-b dark:from-slate-900 dark:via-[#10172b] dark:to-slate-900 border border-red-300 dark:border-red-500/50 rounded-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
            {/* Modal Header */}
            <div className="pb-3 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-start justify-between">
              <div>
                <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black bg-red-100 text-red-800 border border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-500/40 mb-1">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>AVERTIS DEMONSTRATION ALERT</span>
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  {selectedAlertForModal.severity} LANDSLIDE RISK
                </h3>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setSelectedAlertForModal(null)}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white text-lg font-bold"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Modal Body */}
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-xs">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/70 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold">Location:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">{selectedAlertForModal.district} ({selectedAlertForModal.zone_id})</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold">Risk Score:</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-black text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 text-sm">
                    {selectedAlertForModal.risk_score.toFixed(0)} / 100
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 font-bold block mb-1">Primary Contributors:</span>
                <p className="text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 leading-relaxed font-normal">
                  {selectedAlertForModal.reason}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-400 font-bold block mb-1">Recommended Emergency Procedure:</span>
                <ul className="text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-950/30 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 font-normal leading-relaxed">
                  <li>Review the affected zone and initiate appropriate local disaster-management procedures.</li>
                  <li>Verify slope stability with assigned field officer teams.</li>
                  <li>Notify State & District Disaster Management Authorities.</li>
                </ul>
              </div>

              <div className="p-2.5 rounded bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-500/30 text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-300 text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
                ℹ️ <b>Demonstration Notice:</b> Prototype early warning event for Smart India Hackathon 2026. Official alerts are issued exclusively by GSI and SDMA.
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Modal Footer */}
            <div className="mt-5 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex justify-end space-x-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  onSelectZoneId(selectedAlertForModal.zone_id);
                  setSelectedAlertForModal(null);
                }}
                className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-xs"
              >
                View Zone on Map
              </button>
              {selectedAlertForModal.status === 'ACTIVE' && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleAcknowledge(selectedAlertForModal.id)}
                  className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs shadow-xs"
                >
                  Acknowledge Alert
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default AlertsView;
```

## File 32/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/AnalyticsView.tsx`

- **Path:** `Prototype/frontend/src/components/AnalyticsView.tsx`
- **Category:** 08. Frontend - UI Components & Views
