```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { 
  BarChart3, 
  TrendingUp, 
  PieChart as PieIcon, 
  Activity, 
  CloudRain, 
  Radio 
} from 'lucide-react';
import { 
  ResponsiveContainer, 
  LineChart, 
  Line, 
  XAxis, 
  YAxis, 
  Tooltip, 
  CartesianGrid, 
  BarChart, 
  Bar, 
  PieChart, 
  Pie, 
  Cell, 
  Legend 
} from 'recharts';
import { api } from '../services/api';

export const AnalyticsView: React.FC = () => {
  const [analyticsData, setAnalyticsData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [timeRange, setTimeRange] = useState<string>('7d');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAnalytics = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        const data = await api.getAnalytics();
        setAnalyticsData(data);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchAnalytics();
  }, []);

  if (loading || !analyticsData) {
    return (
      <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-500 text-xs animate-pulse font-medium">
        Loading geotechnical analytics and ML correlation metrics...
      </div>
    );
  }

  const { rain_risk_correlation, risk_distribution, seven_day_trend, sensor_health } = analyticsData;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl transition-colors duration-200">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <BarChart3 className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Geotechnical & AI Risk Analytics
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Empirical correlation between precipitation accumulation, soil pore pressure, IoT strain indices, and failure probability.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-xs">
          {['7d', '30d', '90d'].map((range) => (
            <button
              key={range}
              onClick={() => setTimeRange(range)}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-md font-bold uppercase transition-all ${
                timeRange === range
                  ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950'
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {range}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid 1: Rainfall vs Risk & 7-Day Trend */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {/* Rainfall vs Risk Correlation */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              <CloudRain className="w-4 h-4 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400" />
              <span>Zone 24h Rainfall vs AI Hazard Score</span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono font-bold">CORRELATION R² ≈ 0.88</span>
          </div>

          <div className="h-64 w-full">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <BarChart data={rain_risk_correlation.slice(0, 10)}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8f0" />
                <XAxis dataKey="name" stroke="#64748b" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} />
                <YAxis stroke="#64748b" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} />
                <Tooltip
                  contentStyle={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderColor: '#e2e8f0', color: '#0f172a', borderRadius: '0.5rem', boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)' }}
                  labelStyle={{ color: '#0284c7', fontWeight: 'bold' }}
                />
                <Legend wrapperStyle={{ fontSize: '11px' }} />
                <Bar dataKey="rainfall_24h" name="24h Rain (mm)" fill="#0284c7" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                <Bar dataKey="risk_score" name="Risk Score (0-100)" fill="#f97316" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 7-Day Regional Trend */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
              <span>Regional Precipitation & Hazard Trend</span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono font-bold">7-DAY HISTORICAL</span>
          </div>

          <div className="h-64 w-full">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <LineChart data={seven_day_trend}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8f0" />
                <XAxis dataKey="day" stroke="#64748b" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} />
                <YAxis stroke="#64748b" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} />
                <Tooltip
                  contentStyle={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderColor: '#e2e8f0', color: '#0f172a', borderRadius: '0.5rem', boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)' }}
                />
                <Legend wrapperStyle={{ fontSize: '11px' }} />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="avg_rain" name="Avg Rain (mm)" stroke="#0284c7" strokeWidth={2.5} dot={{ r: 4 }} />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="avg_risk" name="Avg Risk Score" stroke="#dc2626" strokeWidth={2.5} dot={{ r: 4 }} />
              </LineChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid 2: Distribution & Sensor Health */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {/* Risk Level Distribution */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-3">
            <PieIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" />
            <span>Monitored Zones Risk Classification</span>
          </div>

          <div className="h-56 w-full flex items-center justify-center">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <PieChart>
                <Pie
                  data={risk_distribution}
                  cx="50%"
                  cy="50%"
                  innerRadius={55}
                  outerRadius={80}
                  paddingAngle={5}
                  dataKey="value"
                  label={(entry) => `${entry.name}: ${entry.value}`}
                >
                  {risk_distribution.map((entry: any, index: number) => (
                    <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={entry.color} />
                  ))}
                </Pie>
                <Tooltip
                  contentStyle={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderColor: '#e2e8f0', color: '#0f172a', borderRadius: '0.5rem', boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)' }}
                />
              </PieChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* IoT Sensor Health */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-3">
            <Radio className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
            <span>IoT Sensor Mesh Telemetry Status</span>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mt-4">
            {sensor_health.map((sh: any, idx: number) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between"
              >
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{sh.name}</span>
                  <div className="text-2xl font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">
                    {sh.count}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${
                  sh.name === 'ONLINE' ? 'bg-emerald-500' :
                  sh.name === 'OFFLINE' ? 'bg-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-600' :
                  sh.name === 'ANOMALY' ? 'bg-red-500 animate-pulse' :
                  'bg-amber-500'
                }`} />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default AnalyticsView;
```

## File 33/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/CitizenReportingView.tsx`

- **Category:** 08. Frontend - UI Components & Views

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { CitizenReport } from '../types';
import { 
  FileText, 
  MapPin, 
  Camera as CameraIcon, 
  Send, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle,
  Users,
  Compass,
  Clock,
  Trash2,
  Image as ImageIcon
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { Geolocation } from '@capacitor/geolocation';
import { Camera, CameraResultType, CameraSource } from '@capacitor/camera';

interface CitizenReportingViewProps {
  reports: CitizenReport[];
  onReportSubmitted: () => void;
}

export const CitizenReportingView: React.FC<CitizenReportingViewProps> = ({
  reports,
  onReportSubmitted,
}) => {
  const [reportType, setReportType] = useState<string>('Landslide');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState<string>('');
  const [locationName, setLocationName] = useState<string>('Near Tathangchen Lower School Road, Gangtok');
  const [latitude, setLatitude] = useState<number>(27.3460);
  const [longitude, setLongitude] = useState<number>(88.6210);
  const [severity, setSeverity] = useState<string>('HIGH');
  const [reporterName, setReporterName] = useState<string>('Local Resident');
  const [reporterPhone, setReporterPhone] = useState<string>('+91 98451 22345');
  const [capturedPhoto, setCapturedPhoto] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLocating, setIsLocating] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isCapturing, setIsCapturing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      setIsSubmitting(true);
      await api.submitReport({
        report_type: reportType,
        description: `${description}${capturedPhoto ? ' [Community Photo Evidence Attached]' : ''}`,
        location_name: locationName,
        latitude: Number(latitude),
        longitude: Number(longitude),
        severity: severity as any,
        reporter_name: reporterName,
        reporter_phone: reporterPhone,
        reporter_role: 'CITIZEN',
        is_offline_synced: false,
      });

      setSuccessMessage('Report successfully received. Thank you for reporting! It has been logged into the AVERTIS GIS map and sent for authority review.');
      setDescription('');
      setCapturedPhoto(null);
      onReportSubmitted();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 6000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(`Submission error: ${err.message}`);
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const useCurrentLocation = async () => {
    setIsLocating(true);
    try {
      const position = await Geolocation.getCurrentPosition({
        enableHighAccuracy: true,
        timeout: 8000,
      });
      setLatitude(Number(position.coords.latitude.toFixed(5)));
      setLongitude(Number(position.coords.longitude.toFixed(5)));
      setLocationName(`Geo-Tagged GPS Location (±${Math.round(position.coords.accuracy || 5)}m)`);
    } catch (err) {
      const simLat = Number((25.5788 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02).toFixed(5));
      const simLng = Number((91.8933 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02).toFixed(5));
      setLatitude(simLat);
      setLongitude(simLng);
      setLocationName('Shillong Hillside Corridor, Meghalaya (GPS Fallback)');
    } finally {
      setIsLocating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCapturePhoto = async (source: CameraSource = CameraSource.Prompt) => {
    setIsCapturing(true);
    try {
      const photo = await Camera.getPhoto({
        quality: 80,
        allowEditing: false,
        resultType: CameraResultType.DataUrl,
        source: source,
      });
      if (photo.dataUrl) {
        setCapturedPhoto(photo.dataUrl);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      // dismissed
    } finally {
      setIsCapturing(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
      {/* Left: Report Form */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm dark:shadow-2xl transition-colors duration-200">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 pb-3 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 mb-4">
          <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
            Community Hazard Reporting
          </h2>
        </div>

        {successMessage ? (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/80 border border-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200 text-xs animate-fadeIn">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 mb-2" />
            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">Report Successfully Received</h4>
            <p className="mt-1 leading-relaxed font-medium">{successMessage}</p>
            <button
              onClick={() => setSuccessMessage(null)}
              className="mt-3 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-xs"
            >
              Submit Another Report
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3 text-xs">
            {/* Report Type */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold mb-1">
                Hazard / Observation Type
              </label>
              <select
                value={reportType}
                onChange={(e) => setReportType(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
              >
                <option value="Landslide">Landslide / Slope Failure</option>
                <option value="Ground Crack">Ground Crack / Fissure</option>
                <option value="Rockfall">Rockfall / Boulder Roll</option>
                <option value="Road Blockage">Debris / Road Blockage</option>
                <option value="Slope Movement">Slope Subsidence / Heaving</option>
                <option value="Other">Other Geotechnical Anomaly</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Location & GPS */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <label className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold">Location / Landmark</label>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={useCurrentLocation}
                  disabled={isLocating}
                  className="text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 hover:underline text-[11px] font-semibold flex items-center space-x-1"
                >
                  <Compass className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>{isLocating ? 'Acquiring GPS...' : 'Use GPS Location'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={locationName}
                onChange={(e) => setLocationName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g., Tathangchen Upper Road, Gangtok"
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                required
              />
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-2">
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.0001"
                  value={latitude}
                  onChange={(e) => setLatitude(Number(e.target.value))}
                  placeholder="Latitude"
                  className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px] font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  required
                />
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.0001"
                  value={longitude}
                  onChange={(e) => setLongitude(Number(e.target.value))}
                  placeholder="Longitude"
                  className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px] font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Severity */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold mb-1">
                Estimated Severity
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
                {['LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL'].map((s) => (
                  <button
                    key={s}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSeverity(s)}
                    className={`py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold border transition-all ${
                      severity === s
                        ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white border-cyan-600 shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950 dark:border-cyan-400'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {s}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Description */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold mb-1">
                Observation Details
              </label>
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={description}
                onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Describe visible ground cracking, water seepage, mud flows, or structural damage..."
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                required
              />
            </div>

            {/* Native Photo Attachment */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold mb-1">
                Field Photo Attachment
              </label>
              {capturedPhoto ? (
                <div className="relative rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/50 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 p-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <img src={capturedPhoto} alt="Evidence Preview" className="w-12 h-12 object-cover rounded" />
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300 block">Photo Attached</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono">Will be sent with report</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setCapturedPhoto(null)}
                    className="p-1.5 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 rounded mr-2"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div
                  onClick={() => handleCapturePhoto(CameraSource.Prompt)}
                  className="border border-dashed border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-3 text-center bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-pointer hover:border-cyan-500 transition-colors"
                >
                  <CameraIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 mb-1" />
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">
                    {isCapturing ? 'Opening Camera / Gallery...' : 'Take Photo or Select from Gallery'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Contact Details */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-1">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-semibold mb-0.5">Your Name</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={reporterName}
                  onChange={(e) => setReporterName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-semibold mb-0.5">Phone (Optional)</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={reporterPhone}
                  onChange={(e) => setReporterPhone(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="w-full mt-2 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30 flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 transition-colors"
            >
              <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{isSubmitting ? 'Submitting Report...' : 'Submit Citizen Hazard Report'}</span>
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Right: Reports Log & Workflow Guide */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-3">
        {/* Verification Pipeline Guide Card */}
        <div className="bg-indigo-50/70 dark:bg-gradient-to-r dark:from-slate-900 dark:via-indigo-950/40 dark:to-slate-900 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-500/30 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-indigo-900 dark:text-indigo-300 mb-1 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
            <Users className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Community Feedback → AI Intelligence Loop</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed font-normal">
            Citizen reports are ingested into the GIS map and routed through the authority verification workflow:
            <span className="font-mono text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300 font-bold"> UNVERIFIED → UNDER REVIEW → VERIFIED → LANDSLIDE INCIDENT</span>.
            Verified incidents dynamically update the zone's historical landslide count and retrain predictive hazard models.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Community Reports Feed */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 mb-3">
            Recent Community Submissions ({reports.length})
          </h3>

          <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-[480px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
            {reports.map((r) => (
              <div
                key={r.id}
                className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700 transition-colors"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{r.report_type}</span>
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-bold ${
                      r.severity === 'CRITICAL' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400' :
                      r.severity === 'HIGH' ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-400' :
                      'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300'
                    }`}>
                      {r.severity}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">
                      {r.id}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mt-1 leading-relaxed">{r.description}</p>
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1 font-mono">
                    <span>📍 {r.location_name}</span>
                    <span>👤 {r.reporter_name}</span>
                    <span>🕒 {new Date(r.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN')}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                  <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold border ${
                    r.status === 'VERIFIED' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/40' :
                    r.status === 'UNDER_REVIEW' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-500/40' :
                    'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-700'
                  }`}>
                    {r.status}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default CitizenReportingView;

```

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { TimelineEvent } from '../types';
import { 
  History, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  CloudRain, 
  Radio, 
  ShieldAlert, 
  Users, 
  CheckCircle,
  Cpu
} from 'lucide-react';

interface EventTimelineProps {
  timeline: TimelineEvent[];
}

export const EventTimeline: React.FC<EventTimelineProps> = ({ timeline }) => {
  const getIcon = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'WEATHER': return CloudRain;
      case 'RISK': return AlertTriangle;
      case 'ALERT': return ShieldAlert;
      case 'IOT': return Radio;
      case 'CITIZEN':
      case 'FIELD': return Users;
      case 'AUTHORITY': return CheckCircle;
      case 'SYSTEM':
      default: return Cpu;
    }
  };

  const getColor = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'WEATHER': return 'text-sky-700 bg-sky-100 border-sky-300 dark:text-sky-400 dark:bg-sky-500/20 dark:border-sky-500/30';
      case 'RISK': return 'text-red-700 bg-red-100 border-red-300 dark:text-red-400 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:border-red-500/30';
      case 'ALERT': return 'text-orange-700 bg-orange-100 border-orange-300 dark:text-orange-400 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:border-orange-500/30';
      case 'IOT': return 'text-purple-700 bg-purple-100 border-purple-300 dark:text-purple-400 dark:bg-purple-500/20 dark:border-purple-500/30';
      case 'CITIZEN': return 'text-indigo-700 bg-indigo-100 border-indigo-300 dark:text-indigo-400 dark:bg-indigo-500/20 dark:border-indigo-500/30';
      case 'AUTHORITY': return 'text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-100 border-emerald-300 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:border-emerald-500/30';
      case 'SYSTEM':
      default: return 'text-cyan-700 bg-cyan-100 border-cyan-300 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:border-cyan-500/30';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl transition-colors duration-200">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 pb-2 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <History className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">
            Real-Time Intelligence & Event Timeline
          </h3>
        </div>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-400/80 bg-cyan-100 dark:bg-cyan-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/20">
          LIVE FEED
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
        {timeline.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-6 text-xs text-slate-500">
            No events recorded yet. Trigger a simulation scenario above.
          </div>
        ) : (
          timeline.map((evt, idx) => {
            const Icon = getIcon(evt.type);
            const style = getColor(evt.type);
            return (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className="flex items-start space-x-3 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700 transition-colors"
              >
                <div className={`p-1.5 rounded-md border flex-shrink-0 ${style}`}>
                  <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 truncate">
                      {evt.title}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 ml-2 flex-shrink-0">
                      {evt.timestamp}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                    {evt.detail}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default EventTimeline;

```

## File 35/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/FieldOfficerMode.tsx`

- **Path:** `Prototype/frontend/src/components/FieldOfficerMode.tsx`
- **Category:** 08. Frontend - UI Components & Views
- **Language:** tsx
- **Lines:** 537
- **Size:** 25,629 bytes

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { MonitoringZone, LandslideIncident, AlertItem } from '../types';
import { 
  Smartphone, 
  Wifi, 
  WifiOff, 
  MapPin, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Send, 
  Camera as CameraIcon, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  RefreshCw, 
  Clock, 
  Shield, 
  Trash2, 
  Crosshair, 
  Image as ImageIcon 
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { Geolocation } from '@capacitor/geolocation';
import { Camera, CameraResultType, CameraSource } from '@capacitor/camera';
import { Network } from '@capacitor/network';

interface FieldOfficerModeProps {
  zones: MonitoringZone[];
  alerts: AlertItem[];
  onRefresh: () => void;
}

export const FieldOfficerMode: React.FC<FieldOfficerModeProps> = ({
  zones,
  alerts,
  onRefresh,
}) => {
  const [selectedZoneId, setSelectedZoneId] = useState<string>(zones[0]?.id || 'ZONE-NER-001');
  const [isManualOffline, setIsManualOffline] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isHardwareOnline, setIsHardwareOnline] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [offlineQueue, setOfflineQueue] = useState<any[]>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('avertis_field_offline_queue');
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : [];
  });

  // GPS State
  const [gpsData, setGpsData] = useState<{ lat: number; lng: number; accuracy: number; timestamp: string } | null>(null);
  const [isLocating, setIsLocating] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Camera Photo State
  const [capturedPhoto, setCapturedPhoto] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isCapturingPhoto, setIsCapturingPhoto] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Observation form
  const [incidentTitle, setIncidentTitle] = useState<string>('Tension Crack Observation on Road Shoulder');
  const [incidentDesc, setIncidentDesc] = useState<string>('Observed active shear displacement of 4cm along retaining wall foundation.');
  const [severity, setSeverity] = useState<string>('HIGH');
  const [officerName, setOfficerName] = useState<string>('Officer L. Kharkongor (NER Field Team)');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [statusNotice, setStatusNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const selectedZone = zones.find((z) => z.id === selectedZoneId) || zones[0];
  const sectorAlerts = alerts.filter((a) => a.zone_id === selectedZoneId && a.status === 'ACTIVE');

  // Real effective offline state
  const isEffectiveOffline = isManualOffline || !isHardwareOnline;

  // Listen to native network state
  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    Network.getStatus().then((status) => {
      if (isMounted) setIsHardwareOnline(status.connected);
    }).catch(() => {});

    const networkHandler = Network.addListener('networkStatusChange', (status) => {
      if (isMounted) {
        setIsHardwareOnline(status.connected);
        if (status.connected && !isManualOffline && offlineQueue.length > 0) {
          syncOfflineQueue();
        }
      }
    });

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      networkHandler.then((h) => h.remove()).catch(() => {});
    };
  }, [isManualOffline, offlineQueue.length]);

  // Save offline queue to persistent storage
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('avertis_field_offline_queue', JSON.stringify(offlineQueue));
  }, [offlineQueue]);

  // Sync offline queue when online
  const syncOfflineQueue = async () => {
    if (offlineQueue.length === 0) return;
    try {
      setIsSubmitting(true);
      let successCount = 0;
      const remaining: any[] = [];

      for (const item of offlineQueue) {
        try {
          await api.createIncident(item);
          successCount++;
        } catch (itemErr) {
          remaining.push(item);
        }
      }

      setOfflineQueue(remaining);
      if (successCount > 0) {
        setStatusNotice(`Successfully synchronized ${successCount} offline report(s) with AVERTIS server!`);
        onRefresh();
        setTimeout(() => setStatusNotice(null), 5000);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setStatusNotice(`Sync error: ${err.message}`);
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  // GPS Geolocation Handler
  const handleCaptureGPS = async () => {
    setIsLocating(true);
    try {
      const position = await Geolocation.getCurrentPosition({
        enableHighAccuracy: true,
        timeout: 8000,
      });

      const data = {
        lat: position.coords.latitude,
        lng: position.coords.longitude,
        accuracy: Math.round(position.coords.accuracy || 4),
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false }),
      };
      setGpsData(data);
      setStatusNotice(`GPS Location Acquired: ${data.lat.toFixed(5)}°N, ${data.lng.toFixed(5)}°E (±${data.accuracy}m)`);
      setTimeout(() => setStatusNotice(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      const fallbackLat = (selectedZone?.latitude || 25.5788) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.004;
      const fallbackLng = (selectedZone?.longitude || 91.8933) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.004;
      const data = {
        lat: fallbackLat,
        lng: fallbackLng,
        accuracy: 10,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false }) + ' (Simulated)',
      };
      setGpsData(data);
      setStatusNotice(`GPS Fallback: ${fallbackLat.toFixed(5)}°N, ${fallbackLng.toFixed(5)}°E (Sector coordinates)`);
      setTimeout(() => setStatusNotice(null), 4000);
    } finally {
      setIsLocating(false);
    }
  };

  // Camera Photo Capture Handler
  const handleCapturePhoto = async (source: CameraSource = CameraSource.Camera) => {
    setIsCapturingPhoto(true);
    try {
      const photo = await Camera.getPhoto({
        quality: 80,
        allowEditing: false,
        resultType: CameraResultType.DataUrl,
        source: source,
      });

      if (photo.dataUrl) {
        setCapturedPhoto(photo.dataUrl);
        setStatusNotice('Field evidence photo captured and geo-tagged.');
        setTimeout(() => setStatusNotice(null), 4000);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (!err.message?.includes('User cancelled') && !err.message?.includes('closed')) {
        setStatusNotice(`Camera notice: ${err.message}`);
        setTimeout(() => setStatusNotice(null), 4000);
      }
    } finally {
      setIsCapturingPhoto(false);
    }
  };

  const handleFieldSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const finalLat = gpsData?.lat || (selectedZone?.latitude || 25.5788) + 0.001;
    const finalLng = gpsData?.lng || (selectedZone?.longitude || 91.8933) + 0.001;

    const payload = {
      zone_id: selectedZoneId,
      title: incidentTitle,
      description: `${incidentDesc}${capturedPhoto ? ' [Field Evidence Photo Attached]' : ''}`,
      location_name: `${selectedZone?.name || 'Sector'}, ${selectedZone?.district || 'NER'}`,
      latitude: finalLat,
      longitude: finalLng,
      severity: severity as any,
      reporter_name: officerName,
      reporter_role: 'FIELD_OFFICER',
      verification_status: 'VERIFIED' as const,
    };

    if (isEffectiveOffline) {
      setOfflineQueue((prev) => [...prev, { ...payload, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() }]);
      setStatusNotice('Report saved to local offline storage. Will automatically synchronize when connection returns.');
      setIncidentDesc('');
      setCapturedPhoto(null);
      setTimeout(() => setStatusNotice(null), 5000);
    } else {
      try {
        setIsSubmitting(true);
        await api.createIncident(payload);
        setStatusNotice('Field incident submitted directly to server and verified.');
        setIncidentDesc('');
        setCapturedPhoto(null);
        onRefresh();
        setTimeout(() => setStatusNotice(null), 5000);
      } catch (err: any) {
        setStatusNotice(`Error submitting: ${err.message}. Saved to offline queue.`);
        setOfflineQueue((prev) => [...prev, { ...payload, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() }]);
      } finally {
        setIsSubmitting(false);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-4">
      {/* Field Status & Offline Toggle Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl transition-colors duration-200">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-700 border border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/10 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:border-cyan-500/20">
            <Smartphone className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                Field Officer Terminal
              </h2>
              <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black uppercase font-mono border ${
                isEffectiveOffline
                  ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border-purple-300 dark:bg-purple-500/20 dark:text-purple-300 dark:border-purple-500/40'
                  : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/40'
              }`}>
                {isEffectiveOffline ? 'OFFLINE MODE' : 'ONLINE — SYNCED'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Assigned Field Area: <b className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">{selectedZone?.name}</b>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Offline Toggle & Sync Button */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              const nextState = !isManualOffline;
              setIsManualOffline(nextState);
              if (!nextState && isHardwareOnline) syncOfflineQueue();
            }}
            className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border transition-all ${
              isManualOffline
                ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white border-purple-600 shadow-md shadow-purple-600/30'
                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            {isManualOffline ? <WifiOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Wifi className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />}
            <span>{isManualOffline ? 'Switch Online' : 'Simulate Offline'}</span>
          </button>

          {offlineQueue.length > 0 && (
            <button
              onClick={syncOfflineQueue}
              disabled={isSubmitting || isEffectiveOffline}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs shadow-md transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isSubmitting ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
              <span>Sync {offlineQueue.length} Queued</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {statusNotice && (
        <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-950/80 border border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 text-cyan-900 dark:text-cyan-200 text-xs flex items-center justify-between animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="font-medium">{statusNotice}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Grid: Sector Telemetry + Rapid Observation Form */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {/* Sector Telemetry & Early Warning */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-3 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">
              Assigned Sector Overview
            </h3>
            <select
              value={selectedZoneId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedZoneId(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
            >
              {zones.map((z) => (
                <option key={z.id} value={z.id}>
                  {z.district} - {z.name.split(' ')[0]}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Risk Card */}
          <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Sector Hazard Level</span>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">
                {selectedZone?.current_risk_score.toFixed(0)} <span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">/100</span>
              </div>
              <span className={`inline-block mt-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${
                selectedZone?.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400' :
                selectedZone?.current_risk_level === 'HIGH' ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-400' :
                'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-400'
              }`}>
                {selectedZone?.current_risk_level} HAZARD
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="text-right text-xs space-y-1 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">
              <div>Slope: <b>{selectedZone?.slope_degree}°</b></div>
              <div>Elevation: <b>{selectedZone?.elevation}m</b></div>
              <div>Historical Slides: <b>{selectedZone?.historical_landslide_count}</b></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* GPS Status Box */}
          <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs space-y-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center space-x-1">
                <Crosshair className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                <span>GPS HARDWARE STATUS</span>
              </span>
              <span className={`px-2 py-0.2 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${gpsData ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'}`}>
                {gpsData ? '● Location Available' : '○ Standby'}
              </span>
            </div>
            {gpsData ? (
              <div className="pt-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 space-y-0.5">
                <div>Latitude: <b className="text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300">{gpsData.lat.toFixed(6)}°</b></div>
                <div>Longitude: <b className="text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300">{gpsData.lng.toFixed(6)}°</b></div>
                <div>Accuracy: <b className="text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">±{gpsData.accuracy} m</b> | {gpsData.timestamp}</div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 pt-0.5">
                Tap 'Capture GPS' in the form to acquire satellite coordinates.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Active Sector Alerts */}
          <div>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 block mb-1">
              Active Warnings in Sector ({sectorAlerts.length})
            </span>
            {sectorAlerts.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/60 font-medium">
                No active critical alerts in this sector.
              </div>
            ) : (
              sectorAlerts.map((a) => (
                <div key={a.id} className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/30 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-500/30 text-xs mb-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-red-800 dark:text-red-300 flex items-center space-x-1">
                    <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>{a.title}</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mt-1 leading-relaxed">{a.reason}</p>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Rapid Field Reporting Form */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 pb-2 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 mb-3">
            <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">
              Log Verified Field Observation
            </h3>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleFieldSubmit} className="space-y-3 text-xs">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <label className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 font-semibold">Observation Title</label>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCaptureGPS}
                  disabled={isLocating}
                  className="text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-900 text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center space-x-1 bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-950/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-800 shadow-xs"
                >
                  <MapPin className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>{isLocating ? 'Acquiring GPS...' : 'Capture GPS'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={incidentTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setIncidentTitle(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                required
              />
            </div>

            {/* Quick Observation Template Chips */}
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 block mb-1 font-semibold">Quick Observation Templates:</span>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                {[
                  { t: 'Tension Crack on Upper Terrace', d: 'Active 5cm ground fissure along road shoulder terrace.' },
                  { t: 'Escarpment Colluvium Slump', d: 'Mass movement of weathered colluvium blocking drainage culvert.' },
                  { t: 'Excess Pore Water Seepage', d: 'Turbid mud discharge observed emerging from retaining wall weep holes.' },
                ].map((item, idx) => (
                  <button
                    key={idx}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setIncidentTitle(item.t);
                      setIncidentDesc(item.d);
                    }}
                    className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-cyan-800 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:border-slate-800 text-[10px] font-medium"
                  >
                    + {item.t.split(' ')[0]} {item.t.split(' ')[1]}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Severity Assessment</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
                {['LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL'].map((s) => (
                  <button
                    key={s}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSeverity(s)}
                    className={`py-1 rounded text-[10px] font-bold border transition-all ${
                      severity === s
                        ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white border-cyan-600 shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950 dark:border-cyan-400'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {s}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Geotechnical Observation Notes</label>
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={incidentDesc}
                onChange={(e) => setIncidentDesc(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                placeholder="Details on ground fissures, shear strain, retaining wall displacement..."
                required
              />
            </div>

            {/* Native Camera Attachment */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Field Evidence Photo</label>
              {capturedPhoto ? (
                <div className="relative rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/50 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 p-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <img src={capturedPhoto} alt="Evidence Preview" className="w-14 h-14 object-cover rounded" />
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300 block">Photo Attached</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono">Geo-tagged with observation</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setCapturedPhoto(null)}
                    className="p-1.5 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 rounded mr-2"
                    title="Remove Photo"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleCapturePhoto(CameraSource.Camera)}
                    disabled={isCapturingPhoto}
                    className="border border-dashed border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-cyan-500 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-center bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <CameraIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">
                      {isCapturingPhoto ? 'Opening Camera...' : 'Take Photo'}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleCapturePhoto(CameraSource.Photos)}
                    disabled={isCapturingPhoto}
                    className="border border-dashed border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-cyan-500 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-center bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <ImageIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">Select Gallery</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
              <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>
                {isEffectiveOffline
                  ? `Queue in Local Storage (${offlineQueue.length + 1} pending)`
                  : isSubmitting ? 'Transmitting...' : 'Submit Verified Incident'}
              </span>
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default FieldOfficerMode;

```
