```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import L from 'leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
import { MonitoringZone, SensorDevice, LandslideIncident, CitizenReport } from '../types';
import { Layers, Eye, Compass, MapPin, Maximize2, Minimize2, RotateCcw, Filter, Radio, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

// Fix standard Leaflet default icon issues in bundlers
delete (L.Icon.Default.prototype as any)._getIconUrl;
L.Icon.Default.mergeOptions({
  iconRetinaUrl: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/images/marker-icon-2x.png',
  iconUrl: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/images/marker-icon.png',
  shadowUrl: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/images/marker-shadow.png',
});

interface GISMapProps {
  zones: MonitoringZone[];
  sensors: SensorDevice[];
  incidents: LandslideIncident[];
  reports: CitizenReport[];
  selectedZone: MonitoringZone | null;
  onSelectZone: (zone: MonitoringZone) => void;
}

const REGIONAL_HUBS: Record<string, { lat: number; lon: number; zoom: number }> = {
  'Meghalaya': { lat: 25.5000, lon: 91.6000, zoom: 9 },
  'Sikkim': { lat: 27.3500, lon: 88.5500, zoom: 10 },
  'Assam': { lat: 25.8000, lon: 92.4000, zoom: 8 },
  'Mizoram': { lat: 23.4000, lon: 92.7000, zoom: 9 },
  'Nagaland': { lat: 25.9000, lon: 94.2000, zoom: 9 },
  'Manipur': { lat: 24.9000, lon: 93.8000, zoom: 9 },
  'Arunachal Pradesh': { lat: 27.2500, lon: 92.8000, zoom: 8 },
  'Tripura': { lat: 23.9000, lon: 91.8000, zoom: 9 },
};

// Basemap style definition
type BasemapStyle = 'topo' | 'osm' | 'satellite' | 'dark';

export const GISMap: React.FC<GISMapProps> = ({
  zones,
  sensors,
  incidents,
  reports,
  selectedZone,
  onSelectZone,
}) => {
  const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapInstanceRef = useRef<L.Map | null>(null);

  // Default to Topographic (crisp terrain relief contours)
  const [basemapStyle, setBasemapStyle] = useState<BasemapStyle>('topo');

  // Layer groups refs
  const baseTileLayersRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup>(L.layerGroup());
  const zonesLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup>(L.layerGroup());
  const sensorsLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup>(L.layerGroup());
  const incidentsLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup>(L.layerGroup());
  const roadsLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup>(L.layerGroup());
  const villagesLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup>(L.layerGroup());
  const reportsLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup>(L.layerGroup());

  // UI state
  const [selectedState, setSelectedState] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [selectedRiskFilter, setSelectedRiskFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [isFullscreen, setIsFullscreen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showLayerMenu, setShowLayerMenu] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Layer visibility state
  const [showZones, setShowZones] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showSensors, setShowSensors] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showIncidents, setShowIncidents] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showRoads, setShowRoads] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showVillages, setShowVillages] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showReports, setShowReports] = useState<boolean>(true);

  // Major NER Arterial Roads dataset
  const NER_ROADS = [
    { name: 'NH-310A (Dikchu - Mangan Corridor)', coords: [[27.34, 88.61], [27.42, 88.58], [27.50, 88.54], [27.60, 88.52]] },
    { name: 'NH-29 (Dimapur - Kohima - Phesama Bypass)', coords: [[25.90, 93.73], [25.75, 93.95], [25.67, 94.10], [25.63, 94.12]] },
    { name: 'NH-54E (Haflong - Jatinga Hill Highway)', coords: [[25.30, 92.85], [25.22, 92.95], [25.18, 93.03], [25.10, 93.12]] },
    { name: 'NH-2 (Imphal - Senapati - Mao Gate)', coords: [[24.81, 93.93], [25.05, 93.98], [25.27, 94.02], [25.50, 94.10]] },
    { name: 'GS Road (Guwahati - Shillong Corridor)', coords: [[26.14, 91.73], [25.95, 91.80], [25.75, 91.85], [25.57, 91.89]] },
    { name: 'Trans-Arunachal Hwy (Bhalukpong - Tenga - Bomdila)', coords: [[27.01, 92.65], [27.18, 92.58], [27.27, 92.42], [27.35, 92.35]] },
    { name: 'NH-108 (Agartala - Baramura Cut)', coords: [[23.83, 91.28], [23.86, 91.52], [23.90, 91.75]] },
  ];

  // Vulnerable NER Reference Villages dataset
  const NER_VILLAGES = [
    { name: 'Mawsmai Village', district: 'East Khasi Hills', state: 'Meghalaya', lat: 25.295, lon: 91.705, pop: 1250 },
    { name: 'Tathangchen Basti', district: 'East Sikkim', state: 'Sikkim', lat: 27.348, lon: 88.625, pop: 3400 },
    { name: 'Jatinga Ridge Settlement', district: 'Dima Hasao', state: 'Assam', lat: 25.175, lon: 93.038, pop: 1800 },
    { name: 'Phesama Village', district: 'Kohima', state: 'Nagaland', lat: 25.635, lon: 94.118, pop: 2200 },
    { name: 'Tupul Station Village', district: 'Noney', state: 'Manipur', lat: 24.838, lon: 93.635, pop: 980 },
    { name: 'Ungma Heritage Village', district: 'Mokokchung', state: 'Nagaland', lat: 26.315, lon: 94.525, pop: 4100 },
    { name: 'Bhalukpong Gate Settlement', district: 'West Kameng', state: 'Arunachal Pradesh', lat: 27.015, lon: 92.645, pop: 2100 },
    { name: 'Vanghmun Village (Jampui)', district: 'North Tripura', state: 'Tripura', lat: 23.960, lon: 92.275, pop: 1650 },
  ];

  // Initialize Map
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;
    if (mapInstanceRef.current) return;

    // Center over North Eastern Region of India (approx lat 25.8, lon 92.5)
    const map = L.map(mapContainerRef.current, {
      center: [25.8, 92.5],
      zoom: 7,
      zoomControl: true,
      attributionControl: false,
    });

    // Add base tile layer group first
    baseTileLayersRef.current.addTo(map);

    // Add overlay layer groups to map
    zonesLayerRef.current.addTo(map);
    sensorsLayerRef.current.addTo(map);
    incidentsLayerRef.current.addTo(map);
    roadsLayerRef.current.addTo(map);
    villagesLayerRef.current.addTo(map);
    reportsLayerRef.current.addTo(map);

    mapInstanceRef.current = map;

    return () => {
      map.remove();
      mapInstanceRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  // Handle Basemap Switcher (Keyless, crisp & reliable basemaps)
  useEffect(() => {
    const layer = baseTileLayersRef.current;
    layer.clearLayers();

    if (basemapStyle === 'dark') {
      // Esri World Dark Gray Canvas Base + Reference Labels
      const base = L.tileLayer(
        'https://services.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/rest/services/Canvas/World_Dark_Gray_Base/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}',
        { maxZoom: 16, maxNativeZoom: 16 }
      );
      const labels = L.tileLayer(
        'https://services.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/rest/services/Canvas/World_Dark_Gray_Reference/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}',
        { maxZoom: 16, maxNativeZoom: 16 }
      );
      layer.addLayer(base);
      layer.addLayer(labels);
    } else if (basemapStyle === 'satellite') {
      // Esri High-Res World Imagery + Administrative Boundaries & Labels
      const imagery = L.tileLayer(
        'https://services.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}',
        { maxZoom: 19 }
      );
      const labels = L.tileLayer(
        'https://services.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/rest/services/Reference/World_Boundaries_and_Places/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}',
        { maxZoom: 19 }
      );
      layer.addLayer(imagery);
      layer.addLayer(labels);
    } else if (basemapStyle === 'topo') {
      // Esri World Topographic (elevation contours & terrain relief)
      const topo = L.tileLayer(
        'https://services.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/rest/services/World_Topo_Map/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}',
        { maxZoom: 19 }
      );
      layer.addLayer(topo);
    } else if (basemapStyle === 'osm') {
      // Standard OpenStreetMap
      const osm = L.tileLayer(
        'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',
        { maxZoom: 19 }
      );
      layer.addLayer(osm);
    }
  }, [basemapStyle]);

  // Filter Zones
  const filteredZones = zones.filter((z) => {
    if (selectedState !== 'ALL' && z.state !== selectedState) return false;
    if (selectedRiskFilter !== 'ALL' && z.current_risk_level !== selectedRiskFilter) return false;
    return true;
  });

  // Render Zones GeoJSON Boundaries and Markers
  useEffect(() => {
    const layer = zonesLayerRef.current;
    layer.clearLayers();
    if (!showZones) return;

    filteredZones.forEach((zone) => {
      let color = '#10b981'; // LOW
      let fillColor = '#10b981';
      let fillOpacity = 0.25;

      if (zone.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL') {
        color = '#ef4444';
        fillColor = '#ef4444';
        fillOpacity = 0.45;
      } else if (zone.current_risk_level === 'HIGH') {
        color = '#f97316';
        fillColor = '#f97316';
        fillOpacity = 0.35;
      } else if (zone.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE') {
        color = '#f59e0b';
        fillColor = '#f59e0b';
        fillOpacity = 0.30;
      }

      const isSelected = selectedZone?.id === zone.id;

      if (zone.boundary_geojson) {
        try {
          const geoData = typeof zone.boundary_geojson === 'string' ? JSON.parse(zone.boundary_geojson) : zone.boundary_geojson;
          const polygonLayer = L.geoJSON(geoData, {
            style: {
              color: isSelected ? '#0284c7' : color,
              weight: isSelected ? 4 : 2,
              fillColor,
              fillOpacity: isSelected ? 0.6 : fillOpacity,
              dashArray: isSelected ? undefined : '4, 4',
            },
          });

          polygonLayer.on('click', () => onSelectZone(zone));
          polygonLayer.bindTooltip(
            `<div class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900">${zone.name}</div>
             <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-600">${zone.district}, ${zone.state}</div>
             <div class="text-[11px] font-mono mt-1 font-bold ${
               zone.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL' ? 'text-red-600' :
               zone.current_risk_level === 'HIGH' ? 'text-orange-600' :
               zone.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE' ? 'text-amber-600' : 'text-emerald-600'
             }">${zone.current_risk_score}/100 • ${zone.current_risk_level} RISK</div>`,
            { direction: 'top', sticky: true, opacity: 0.95 }
          );

          layer.addLayer(polygonLayer);
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('GeoJSON parse error for zone:', zone.id, e);
        }
      }

      // Center Marker Badge
      const markerHtml = `
        <div class="cursor-pointer group">
          <div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px] font-mono shadow-md transition-transform group-hover:scale-125 border-2 ${
            isSelected ? 'ring-4 ring-cyan-500 scale-110' : ''
          } ${
            zone.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL' ? 'bg-red-600 text-white border-white animate-pulse' :
            zone.current_risk_level === 'HIGH' ? 'bg-orange-500 text-white border-white' :
            zone.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 border-white' :
            'bg-emerald-600 text-white border-white'
          }">
            ${Math.round(zone.current_risk_score)}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      const customIcon = L.divIcon({
        html: markerHtml,
        className: 'custom-zone-marker',
        iconSize: [28, 28],
        iconAnchor: [14, 14],
      });

      const marker = L.marker([zone.latitude, zone.longitude], { icon: customIcon });
      marker.on('click', () => onSelectZone(zone));
      layer.addLayer(marker);
    });
  }, [filteredZones, selectedZone, showZones, onSelectZone]);

  // Render IoT Sensors
  useEffect(() => {
    const layer = sensorsLayerRef.current;
    layer.clearLayers();
    if (!showSensors) return;

    sensors.forEach((s) => {
      const isOffline = s.status === 'OFFLINE';
      const isMoisture = s.sensor_type === 'SOIL_MOISTURE';
      const isRain = s.sensor_type === 'RAIN_GAUGE';
      const isTilt = s.sensor_type === 'TILT_INCLINOMETER';

      const iconBg = isOffline ? 'bg-slate-400' : isMoisture ? 'bg-emerald-500' : isRain ? 'bg-sky-500' : 'bg-purple-500';

      const sensorHtml = `
        <div class="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full ${iconBg} border-2 border-white shadow-md cursor-pointer ${isOffline ? 'opacity-50' : ''}"></div>
      `;

      const icon = L.divIcon({
        html: sensorHtml,
        className: 'sensor-marker',
        iconSize: [14, 14],
        iconAnchor: [7, 7],
      });

      const marker = L.marker([s.latitude, s.longitude], { icon });
      marker.bindTooltip(
        `<div class="font-bold text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-900">${s.id}</div>
         <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-600">${s.model_name}</div>
         <div class="text-[11px] text-cyan-700 font-mono mt-0.5">Reading: <b>${s.latest_reading} ${s.unit}</b></div>
         <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Status: ${s.status} • Bat: ${s.battery_level}%</div>`,
        { direction: 'top', sticky: true }
      );
      layer.addLayer(marker);
    });
  }, [sensors, showSensors]);

  // Render Arterial Roads
  useEffect(() => {
    const layer = roadsLayerRef.current;
    layer.clearLayers();
    if (!showRoads) return;

    NER_ROADS.forEach((road) => {
      const line = L.polyline(road.coords as any, {
        color: '#d97706',
        weight: 3.5,
        opacity: 0.85,
        dashArray: '6, 6',
      });
      line.bindTooltip(
        `<div class="font-bold text-xs text-amber-800">${road.name}</div>
         <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-600">Strategic Transportation Arterial</div>`,
        { direction: 'top', sticky: true }
      );
      layer.addLayer(line);
    });
  }, [showRoads]);

  // Render Vulnerable Settlements
  useEffect(() => {
    const layer = villagesLayerRef.current;
    layer.clearLayers();
    if (!showVillages) return;

    NER_VILLAGES.forEach((v) => {
      const vIcon = L.divIcon({
        html: `<div class="w-3 h-3 rounded-xs bg-indigo-600 border border-white shadow-sm transform rotate-45"></div>`,
        className: 'village-marker',
        iconSize: [12, 12],
        iconAnchor: [6, 6],
      });
      const marker = L.marker([v.lat, v.lon], { icon: vIcon });
      marker.bindTooltip(
        `<div class="font-bold text-xs text-indigo-900">${v.name}</div>
         <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-600">${v.district}, ${v.state}</div>
         <div class="text-[10px] text-cyan-700 font-mono">Pop: ~${v.pop} residents</div>`,
        { direction: 'top', sticky: true }
      );
      layer.addLayer(marker);
    });
  }, [showVillages]);

  // Handle Fly-To on Selected Zone or Regional Hub
  const flyToRegion = (stateName: string) => {
    setSelectedState(stateName);
    if (!mapInstanceRef.current) return;
    if (stateName === 'ALL') {
      mapInstanceRef.current.flyTo([25.8, 92.5], 7, { duration: 1.2 });
    } else if (REGIONAL_HUBS[stateName]) {
      const { lat, lon, zoom } = REGIONAL_HUBS[stateName];
      mapInstanceRef.current.flyTo([lat, lon], zoom, { duration: 1.2 });
    }
  };

  const handleRecenter = () => {
    setSelectedState('ALL');
    setSelectedRiskFilter('ALL');
    mapInstanceRef.current?.flyTo([25.8, 92.5], 7, { duration: 1.0 });
  };

  return (
    <div className={`flex flex-col h-full bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-sm hover:shadow-md dark:shadow-2xl overflow-hidden relative transition-colors duration-200 ${
      isFullscreen ? 'fixed inset-4 z-[999]' : 'min-h-[540px]'
    }`}>
      {/* Top Filter Bar: 8 NER States + Hazard Level Filter */}
      <div className="p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#0a1120] border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
        {/* State Filter Pills */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 overflow-x-auto py-0.5 scrollbar-none max-w-full">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest pl-1 mr-1 hidden sm:inline">
            NER State:
          </span>
          {['ALL', 'Meghalaya', 'Sikkim', 'Assam', 'Mizoram', 'Nagaland', 'Manipur', 'Arunachal Pradesh', 'Tripura'].map((st) => (
            <button
              key={st}
              onClick={() => flyToRegion(st)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex-shrink-0 ${
                selectedState === st
                  ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950'
                  : 'bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:border-slate-800'
              }`}
            >
              {st === 'Arunachal Pradesh' ? 'Arunachal' : st}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Action Buttons: Layers, Recenter, Fullscreen */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 ml-auto">
          {/* Layer Menu Toggle */}
          <div className="relative">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowLayerMenu(!showLayerMenu)}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs flex items-center space-x-1 transition-all ${
                showLayerMenu
                  ? 'bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-900 border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/50'
                  : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
              title="Toggle GIS Map Layers"
            >
              <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
              <span className="text-[11px] hidden sm:inline font-semibold">Layers</span>
            </button>

            {/* Floating Layer Selection Dropdown */}
            {showLayerMenu && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-64 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-200 dark:border-cyan-500/40 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-2xl z-[500] text-xs space-y-3 animate-fadeIn text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                {/* Basemap Styles */}
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-1 mb-2">
                    Basemap Theme
                  </span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
                    {[
                      { id: 'topo', label: 'Topographic', desc: 'Elevation relief' },
                      { id: 'osm', label: 'Street Map', desc: 'OpenStreetMap' },
                      { id: 'satellite', label: 'Satellite 3D', desc: 'Terrain imagery' },
                      { id: 'dark', label: 'Dark Tactical', desc: 'Command center' },
                    ].map((bm) => (
                      <button
                        key={bm.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setBasemapStyle(bm.id as BasemapStyle)}
                        className={`px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-left transition-all border ${
                          basemapStyle === bm.id
                            ? 'bg-cyan-50 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/60'
                            : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950/60 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-800'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="font-bold text-[11px] leading-tight">{bm.label}</div>
                        <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{bm.desc}</div>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* GIS Layer Toggles */}
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-1 mb-2">
                    GIS Overlays
                  </span>
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={showZones}
                        onChange={(e) => setShowZones(e.target.checked)}
                        className="accent-cyan-600 rounded"
                      />
                      <span className="text-[11px]">22 Hazard Zones Polygons</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={showSensors}
                        onChange={(e) => setShowSensors(e.target.checked)}
                        className="accent-emerald-600 rounded"
                      />
                      <span className="text-[11px]">IoT Sensors Mesh (25)</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={showRoads}
                        onChange={(e) => setShowRoads(e.target.checked)}
                        className="accent-amber-600 rounded"
                      />
                      <span className="text-[11px]">NH Arterial Corridors</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={showVillages}
                        onChange={(e) => setShowVillages(e.target.checked)}
                        className="accent-indigo-600 rounded"
                      />
                      <span className="text-[11px]">Hill Settlements & Villages</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={showIncidents}
                        onChange={(e) => setShowIncidents(e.target.checked)}
                        className="accent-rose-600 rounded"
                      />
                      <span className="text-[11px]">Landslide Incidents</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={showReports}
                        onChange={(e) => setShowReports(e.target.checked)}
                        className="accent-sky-600 rounded"
                      />
                      <span className="text-[11px]">Citizen Ground Reports</span>
                    </label>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Recenter Button */}
          <button
            onClick={handleRecenter}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white dark:border-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-xs"
            title="Reset Map View over North Eastern Region"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
          </button>

          {/* Fullscreen Toggle */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsFullscreen(!isFullscreen)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white dark:border-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-xs"
            title={isFullscreen ? 'Exit Fullscreen' : 'Expand Fullscreen Map'}
          >
            {isFullscreen ? <Minimize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" /> : <Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Map Container */}
      <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full relative">
        <div ref={mapContainerRef} className="w-full h-full min-h-[460px]" />

        {/* Floating Map Legend */}
        <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 z-[400] bg-white/90 dark:bg-slate-950/85 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-2.5 shadow-md dark:shadow-xl text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 space-y-1.5 pointer-events-auto">
          <div className="text-[9px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            AI Landslide Risk
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-red-600 animate-pulse"></span>
            <span>Critical (≥75)</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-orange-500"></span>
            <span>High Risk (50-74)</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500"></span>
            <span>Moderate (25-49)</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600"></span>
            <span>Low Risk (&lt;25)</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Selected Zone Quick Tooltip Banner on Top Right */}
        {selectedZone && (
          <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 z-[400] bg-white/95 dark:bg-slate-950/90 backdrop-blur-md border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/40 rounded-xl p-2.5 shadow-xl text-xs max-w-[220px] sm:max-w-xs animate-fadeIn pointer-events-auto">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1 gap-2">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 font-bold truncate">{selectedZone.id}</span>
              <span className={`text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold font-mono whitespace-nowrap ${
                selectedZone.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-300' :
                selectedZone.current_risk_level === 'HIGH' ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-300' :
                selectedZone.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-300' : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300'
              }`}>
                {selectedZone.current_risk_level}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 truncate text-[11px] sm:text-xs">{selectedZone.name}</div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">{selectedZone.district}, {selectedZone.state}</div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default GISMap;
```

## File 37/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/GlobalSearchModal.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { 
  Search, 
  MapPin, 
  Radio, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  ChevronRight, 
  X, 
  Sliders, 
  Layers, 
  Sparkles 
} from 'lucide-react';
import { MonitoringZone, SensorDevice, AlertItem } from '../types';

interface GlobalSearchModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  zones: MonitoringZone[];
  sensors: SensorDevice[];
  alerts: AlertItem[];
  onSelectZone: (zone: MonitoringZone) => void;
  onNavigateTab: (tab: string) => void;
}

export const GlobalSearchModal: React.FC<GlobalSearchModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  zones,
  sensors,
  alerts,
  onSelectZone,
  onNavigateTab,
}) => {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 50);
    } else {
      setQuery('');
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  // Handle escape key
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();

  const matchingZones = q
    ? zones.filter(
        (z) =>
          z.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          z.district.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          z.state.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          z.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          z.lithology_type.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      ).slice(0, 6)
    : zones.slice(0, 4);

  const matchingSensors = q
    ? sensors.filter(
        (s) =>
          s.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          s.sensor_type.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          s.zone_id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          s.model_name.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      ).slice(0, 4)
    : [];

  const matchingAlerts = q
    ? alerts.filter(
        (a) =>
          a.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          a.district.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          a.severity.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          a.reason.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      ).slice(0, 3)
    : [];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-start justify-center pt-16 sm:pt-24 p-4 bg-black/40 dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fadeIn">
      <div 
        className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[80vh] transition-colors duration-200"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Search Bar Header */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center space-x-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60">
          <Search className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            type="text"
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search 22 NER monitoring zones, districts, IoT sensors, alerts (e.g. Cherrapunji, Sikkim, MPU6050)..."
            className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder-slate-400 dark:placeholder-slate-500 text-sm focus:outline-none"
          />
          {query && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setQuery('')}
              className="p-1 rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          )}
          <kbd className="hidden sm:inline-block px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded">
            ESC
          </kbd>
        </div>

        {/* Search Results */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          {/* Quick Categories Header if empty */}
          {!q && (
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between pb-1 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80">
              <span className="font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-[10px]">
                Featured High-Risk Corridors
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 font-mono font-bold">22 MONITORED NER ZONES</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Zones Section */}
          {matchingZones.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                <span>Monitoring Zones & Hill Sectors</span>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {matchingZones.map((z) => (
                  <div
                    key={z.id}
                    onClick={() => {
                      onSelectZone(z);
                      onNavigateTab('map');
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-cyan-50/70 dark:bg-slate-950/50 dark:hover:bg-cyan-950/40 border border-slate-200 hover:border-cyan-300 dark:border-slate-800/80 dark:hover:border-cyan-500/40 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between transition-all group"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                      <div className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${
                        z.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL' ? 'bg-red-600 animate-pulse' :
                        z.current_risk_level === 'HIGH' ? 'bg-orange-500' :
                        z.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE' ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-emerald-600'
                      }`} />
                      <div>
                        <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 group-hover:text-cyan-800 dark:group-hover:text-cyan-300 transition-colors">
                          {z.name}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                          {z.district}, <span className="text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 font-medium">{z.state}</span> • Slope: {z.slope_degree}° • Elev: {z.elevation}m
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono font-bold ${
                        z.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-300' :
                        z.current_risk_level === 'HIGH' ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border border-orange-300 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-300' :
                        z.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-300' :
                        'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300'
                      }`}>
                        {z.current_risk_score} / 100 ({z.current_risk_level})
                      </span>
                      <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-cyan-600 dark:group-hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Sensors Section */}
          {matchingSensors.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <Radio className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                <span>IoT Geotechnical Sensors</span>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {matchingSensors.map((s) => (
                  <div
                    key={s.id}
                    onClick={() => {
                      onNavigateTab('sensors');
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-emerald-50/70 dark:bg-slate-950/50 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/40 border border-slate-200 hover:border-emerald-300 dark:border-slate-800/80 dark:hover:border-emerald-500/40 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between transition-all group"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 group-hover:text-emerald-700 dark:group-hover:text-emerald-300">
                        {s.id}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                        {s.model_name} • In {s.zone_id}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300 font-semibold">
                      Reading: {s.latest_reading} {s.unit}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Alerts Section */}
          {matchingAlerts.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orange-600 dark:text-orange-400" />
                <span>Early Warning Alerts</span>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {matchingAlerts.map((a) => (
                  <div
                    key={a.id}
                    onClick={() => {
                      onNavigateTab('alerts');
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-orange-50/70 dark:bg-slate-950/50 dark:hover:bg-orange-950/40 border border-slate-200 hover:border-orange-300 dark:border-slate-800/80 dark:hover:border-orange-500/40 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between transition-all group"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 group-hover:text-orange-700 dark:group-hover:text-orange-300">
                        {a.title}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                        {a.district} • Status: {a.status}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-red-100 text-red-800 border border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-300 font-bold">
                      {a.severity}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* No results */}
          {q && matchingZones.length === 0 && matchingSensors.length === 0 && matchingAlerts.length === 0 && (
            <div className="text-center py-8 text-slate-500 text-xs">
              No matching zones or sensors found for "<span className="text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 font-semibold">{query}</span>".
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Try searching by state name (Meghalaya, Sikkim, Assam, Mizoram) or sensor type.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer Shortcut hints */}
        <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            <span>Press <kbd className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 rounded font-mono text-[10px]">↵</kbd> to select</span>
            <span><kbd className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 rounded font-mono text-[10px]">ESC</kbd> to close</span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold">AVERTIS Intelligence Index</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default GlobalSearchModal;
```

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { LandslideIncident, CitizenReport, UserRole } from '../types';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  CheckCircle, 
  Clock, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Check, 
  X, 
  Filter, 
  FileCheck, 
  Building 
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../services/api';

interface IncidentsViewProps {
  incidents: LandslideIncident[];
  reports: CitizenReport[];
  userRole: UserRole;
  onRefresh: () => void;
}

export const IncidentsView: React.FC<IncidentsViewProps> = ({
  incidents,
  reports,
  userRole,
  onRefresh,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'incidents' | 'review'>('incidents');
  const [actionLoading, setActionLoading] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resolutionNotes, setResolutionNotes] = useState<string>('');
  const [resolvingId, setResolvingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleUpdateReportStatus = async (reportId: string, status: string) => {
    try {
      setActionLoading(reportId);
      await api.updateReportStatus(reportId, status, `${userRole} Officer`);
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(null);
    }
  };

  const handleResolveIncident = async (incidentId: string) => {
    try {
      setActionLoading(incidentId);
      await api.resolveIncident(incidentId, resolutionNotes || 'Stabilization works completed.');
      setResolvingId(null);
      setResolutionNotes('');
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(null);
    }
  };

  const unverifiedReports = reports.filter((r) => r.status !== 'VERIFIED');

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Header & Tabs */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl transition-colors duration-200">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Landslide Incident & Verification Management
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Authority review workflow transitioning unverified reports into verified disaster incidents.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('incidents')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'incidents'
                ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            Verified Incidents ({incidents.length})
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('review')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'review'
                ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            Review Pending ({unverifiedReports.length})
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {activeTab === 'incidents' ? (
        /* Incidents Table */
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 font-bold">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Incident Code</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Title & Description</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Location</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Severity</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Reporter</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Status</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800/60 font-sans">
                {incidents.map((inc) => (
                  <tr key={inc.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/30 transition-colors">
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400">{inc.id}</td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 max-w-sm">
                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs">{inc.title}</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 line-clamp-1 mt-0.5">{inc.description}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">{inc.location_name}</td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${
                        inc.severity === 'CRITICAL' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400' :
                        inc.severity === 'HIGH' ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-400' :
                        'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-300'
                      }`}>
                        {inc.severity}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      {inc.reporter_name}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${
                        inc.verification_status === 'RESOLVED'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/30'
                          : 'bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/30'
                      }`}>
                        {inc.verification_status}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 px-4">
                      {inc.verification_status !== 'RESOLVED' ? (
                        resolvingId === inc.id ? (
                          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1">
                            <input
                              type="text"
                              placeholder="Notes..."
                              value={resolutionNotes}
                              onChange={(e) => setResolutionNotes(e.target.value)}
                              className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200"
                            />
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleResolveIncident(inc.id)}
                              className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-xs"
                            >
                              Save
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setResolvingId(inc.id)}
                            className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:hover:bg-emerald-500/30 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/40 text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs"
                          >
                            Resolve Incident
                          </button>
                        )
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono font-medium">Mitigated</span>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Authority Review Queue */
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {unverifiedReports.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-12 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-slate-500 text-xs shadow-xs">
              No pending citizen reports in review queue.
            </div>
          ) : (
            unverifiedReports.map((r) => (
              <div
                key={r.id}
                className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{r.report_type}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-400 bg-cyan-100 dark:bg-cyan-950 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-800/60 font-semibold">
                      {r.id}
                    </span>
                    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 text-[10px] font-bold">
                      {r.status}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mt-1.5 max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">{r.description}</p>
                  
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-4 text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-2 font-mono">
                    <span>📍 {r.location_name}</span>
                    <span>👤 Reported by {r.reporter_name}</span>
                    <span>🕒 {new Date(r.timestamp).toLocaleString('en-IN')}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Authority Actions */}
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 flex-shrink-0">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleUpdateReportStatus(r.id, 'UNDER_REVIEW')}
                    disabled={actionLoading === r.id || r.status === 'UNDER_REVIEW'}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:hover:bg-amber-500/30 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-500/40 text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs"
                  >
                    Mark Under Review
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleUpdateReportStatus(r.id, 'VERIFIED')}
                    disabled={actionLoading === r.id}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1 shadow-xs"
                  >
                    <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>Verify as Incident</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleUpdateReportStatus(r.id, 'REJECTED')}
                    disabled={actionLoading === r.id}
                    className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-red-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-red-700 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-red-950 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-red-400"
                    title="Reject report"
                  >
                    <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default IncidentsView;
```
