## File 40/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/LoginModal.tsx`

- **Path:** `Prototype/frontend/src/components/LoginModal.tsx`
- **Category:** 08. Frontend - UI Components & Views
- **Language:** tsx
- **Lines:** 669
- **Size:** 29,811 bytes

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  Smartphone, 
  KeyRound, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle, 
  ArrowRight, 
  User, 
  Sparkles, 
  LogOut, 
  RotateCw, 
  Edit2, 
  X,
  BadgeCheck,
  Shield,
  Radio,
  Clock
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AuthUser, UserRole } from '../types';
import { api } from '../services/api';

interface LoginModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  currentUser: AuthUser | null;
  onAuthSuccess: (user: AuthUser) => void;
  onLogout: () => void;
}

// Preset Quick Accounts for instant evaluation
const DEMO_PRESETS = [
  {
    label: 'State Admin',
    role: 'ADMIN' as UserRole,
    phone: '+91 98765 43210',
    rawPhone: '+919876543210',
    name: 'State Disaster Controller',
    badge: 'State DM Level'
  },
  {
    label: 'SDMA Authority',
    role: 'AUTHORITY' as UserRole,
    phone: '+91 94361 00001',
    rawPhone: '+919436100001',
    name: 'NESAC / SDMA Authority',
    badge: 'NESAC / NIDM'
  },
  {
    label: 'Field Officer',
    role: 'FIELD_OFFICER' as UserRole,
    phone: '+91 94361 23456',
    rawPhone: '+919436123456',
    name: 'Officer L. Kharkongor',
    badge: 'Shillong Sector'
  },
  {
    label: 'Citizen Reporter',
    role: 'CITIZEN' as UserRole,
    phone: '+91 98620 99887',
    rawPhone: '+919862099887',
    name: 'Community Reporter',
    badge: 'Public Portal'
  }
];

export const LoginModal: React.FC<LoginModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  currentUser,
  onAuthSuccess,
  onLogout
}) => {
  // Authentication Step: 1 = Phone Input, 2 = OTP Entry, 3 = Success
  const [step, setStep] = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(1);
  const [phoneNumber, setPhoneNumber] = useState<string>('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState<string>('');
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState<UserRole>('CITIZEN');

  // OTP State: 6 individual digit strings
  const [otpDigits, setOtpDigits] = useState<string[]>(['', '', '', '', '', '']);
  const otpInputsRef = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([]);

  // UX Feedback States
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [simulatedOtp, setSimulatedOtp] = useState<string>('123456');
  const [cooldown, setCooldown] = useState<number>(0);
  const [successUser, setSuccessUser] = useState<AuthUser | null>(null);
  const [isNewUserRegistered, setIsNewUserRegistered] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Focus first input on step transition
  useEffect(() => {
    if (step === 2) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        otpInputsRef.current[0]?.focus();
      }, 100);
    }
  }, [step]);

  // Cooldown countdown timer for OTP resend
  useEffect(() => {
    if (cooldown <= 0) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setCooldown((prev) => prev - 1);
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [cooldown]);

  // Reset internal states when modal opens
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      if (!currentUser) {
        setStep(1);
        setOtpDigits(['', '', '', '', '', '']);
      }
    }
  }, [isOpen, currentUser]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Format phone number clean digits
  const handlePhoneChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    let val = e.target.value.replace(/[^\d+]/g, '');
    setPhoneNumber(val);
    setErrorMessage(null);
  };

  // Quick Preset Selection
  const selectPreset = (preset: typeof DEMO_PRESETS[0]) => {
    setPhoneNumber(preset.rawPhone);
    setFullName(preset.name);
    setSelectedRole(preset.role);
    setErrorMessage(null);
  };

  // Step 1: Send OTP handler
  const handleSendOtp = async (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMessage(null);

    const cleanPhone = phoneNumber.trim();
    if (!cleanPhone || cleanPhone.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 10) {
      setErrorMessage('Please enter a valid 10-digit mobile number.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.sendOtp(cleanPhone, selectedRole, fullName || undefined);
      setSimulatedOtp(res.simulated_otp || '123456');
      setCooldown(60); // 60s cooldown
      setStep(2);
      setOtpDigits(['', '', '', '', '', '']);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMessage(err.message || 'Failed to send OTP. Please verify your phone number.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Step 2: Handle Individual OTP Digits
  const handleOtpDigitChange = (index: number, value: string) => {
    const digit = value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-1);
    const newDigits = [...otpDigits];
    newDigits[index] = digit;
    setOtpDigits(newDigits);
    setErrorMessage(null);

    // Auto-advance to next input
    if (digit && index < 5) {
      otpInputsRef.current[index + 1]?.focus();
    }

    // If all 6 digits entered, auto-verify!
    if (digit && index === 5 && newDigits.every((d) => d !== '')) {
      handleVerifyOtp(newDigits.join(''));
    }
  };

  const handleOtpKeyDown = (index: number, e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
      if (!otpDigits[index] && index > 0) {
        otpInputsRef.current[index - 1]?.focus();
      }
    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && index > 0) {
      otpInputsRef.current[index - 1]?.focus();
    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && index < 5) {
      otpInputsRef.current[index + 1]?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handleOtpPaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const pasteData = e.clipboardData.getData('text').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6);
    if (!pasteData) return;

    const newDigits = [...otpDigits];
    for (let i = 0; i < pasteData.length; i++) {
      newDigits[i] = pasteData[i];
    }
    setOtpDigits(newDigits);

    // Focus last filled digit or next empty
    const focusIndex = Math.min(pasteData.length, 5);
    otpInputsRef.current[focusIndex]?.focus();

    if (pasteData.length === 6) {
      handleVerifyOtp(pasteData);
    }
  };

  const handleAutoFillOtp = () => {
    const code = simulatedOtp || '123456';
    const digits = code.split('').slice(0, 6);
    setOtpDigits(digits);
    handleVerifyOtp(code);
  };

  // Step 2: Verify OTP
  const handleVerifyOtp = async (codeToVerify?: string) => {
    const finalOtp = codeToVerify || otpDigits.join('');
    if (finalOtp.length !== 6) {
      setErrorMessage('Please enter all 6 digits of the verification code.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMessage(null);

    try {
      const res = await api.verifyOtp(
        phoneNumber,
        finalOtp,
        selectedRole,
        fullName || undefined
      );

      setSuccessUser(res.user);
      setIsNewUserRegistered(res.is_new_user);
      setStep(3);

      // Notify parent app
      setTimeout(() => {
        onAuthSuccess(res.user);
      }, 1200);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMessage(err.message || 'Verification failed. Incorrect OTP code.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1000] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 dark:bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md p-4 animate-fadeIn">
      <div 
        className="bg-white dark:bg-[#0b1329] border border-slate-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden transition-all text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Decorative background glows */}
        <div className="absolute -top-24 -right-24 w-60 h-60 bg-cyan-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-24 -left-24 w-60 h-60 bg-indigo-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Close Button */}
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-5 right-5 p-2 rounded-full text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:text-white dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
          title="Close Window"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        {/* Modal Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mb-6">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500 via-blue-600 to-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/25 flex-shrink-0">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-black tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                AVERTIS Authentication
              </h2>
              <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/40 font-mono font-bold">
                SIH 2026
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">
              Secure Mobile OTP Verification & Single Sign-On
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* If Already Logged In: Profile Card Mode */}
        {currentUser && step === 1 && (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-3.5">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500 to-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-black text-lg shadow-md">
                  {currentUser.full_name?.charAt(0) || currentUser.username.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white truncate">
                      {currentUser.full_name || currentUser.username}
                    </span>
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/40">
                      {currentUser.role}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">
                    📱 {currentUser.phone_number || 'Mobile Registered'}
                  </p>
                  {currentUser.assigned_zone_id && (
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                      📍 Assigned Zone: {currentUser.assigned_zone_id}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  onLogout();
                  setStep(1);
                  setPhoneNumber('');
                }}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl border border-red-200 dark:border-red-500/30 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-500/10 dark:hover:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-300 font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 transition-all"
              >
                <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Sign Out & Switch Account</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30 transition-all flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5"
              >
                <span>Continue Working</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Main Authentication Flow (When Not Logged In or Switching) */}
        {(!currentUser || step !== 1) && (
          <div>
            {/* Step Progress Pills */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-6">
              <div className={`p-2 rounded-xl border flex items-center space-x-2 transition-all ${
                step === 1 
                  ? 'bg-cyan-50 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-900 dark:bg-cyan-500/15 dark:border-cyan-500/50 dark:text-cyan-200' 
                  : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:border-slate-800'
              }`}>
                <div className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold ${
                  step === 1 ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
                }`}>
                  1
                </div>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-bold leading-none">Mobile Verification</div>
                  <div className="text-[9px] opacity-75">Enter phone number</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className={`p-2 rounded-xl border flex items-center space-x-2 transition-all ${
                step >= 2 
                  ? 'bg-cyan-50 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-900 dark:bg-cyan-500/15 dark:border-cyan-500/50 dark:text-cyan-200' 
                  : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:border-slate-800'
              }`}>
                <div className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold ${
                  step >= 2 ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
                }`}>
                  2
                </div>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-bold leading-none">6-Digit OTP</div>
                  <div className="text-[9px] opacity-75">Instant verification</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Error Banner */}
            {errorMessage && (
              <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-800 dark:bg-red-500/15 dark:border-red-500/40 dark:text-red-200 text-xs flex items-center space-x-2 animate-fadeIn">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400" />
                <span>{errorMessage}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* STEP 1: MOBILE NUMBER ENTRY */}
            {step === 1 && (
              <form onSubmit={handleSendOtp} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1.5">
                    Mobile Number (India / NER)
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 overflow-hidden focus-within:border-cyan-500 focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-cyan-500/20 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 transition-all">
                    <div className="flex items-center px-3 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 border-r border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-800 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 select-none">
                      <span className="mr-1.5">🇮🇳</span>
                      <span>+91</span>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="tel"
                      value={phoneNumber.replace(/^\+91/, '')}
                      onChange={handlePhoneChange}
                      placeholder="98765 43210"
                      className="flex-1 bg-transparent px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold tracking-wider text-slate-900 dark:text-white placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none"
                      autoFocus
                    />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">
                    Unregistered numbers are automatically created as new accounts instantly.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* Optional Full Name (for registration) */}
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1.5">
                    Full Name <span className="font-normal text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">(Optional for new profiles)</span>
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. Tenzen Kharkongor"
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-cyan-500/20"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Role Selector Pill */}
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1.5">
                    Select Access Role
                  </label>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
                    {(['CITIZEN', 'FIELD_OFFICER', 'AUTHORITY', 'ADMIN'] as UserRole[]).map((role) => (
                      <button
                        key={role}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setSelectedRole(role)}
                        className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-left border transition-all ${
                          selectedRole === role
                            ? 'bg-cyan-50 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-900 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:border-cyan-500/60 dark:text-cyan-300 shadow-xs'
                            : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900/60 dark:border-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span>{role.replace('_', ' ')}</span>
                          {selectedRole === role && <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />}
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Quick Demo Account Presets */}
                <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">
                    ⚡ Quick Demo Account Presets (1-Click Test):
                  </span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
                    {DEMO_PRESETS.map((preset) => (
                      <button
                        key={preset.label}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => selectPreset(preset)}
                        className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:hover:bg-cyan-950/40 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 hover:border-cyan-300 dark:hover:border-cyan-500/40 text-left transition-all group"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="font-bold text-[11px] text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 group-hover:text-cyan-600 dark:group-hover:text-cyan-400">
                            {preset.label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono">
                            {preset.role}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 block font-mono mt-0.5">
                          {preset.phone}
                        </span>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Send OTP Submit Button */}
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 via-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-cyan-500 hover:to-blue-500 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30 flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 transition-all hover:scale-[1.01] disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? (
                    <RotateCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <span>Send 6-Digit OTP</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}

            {/* STEP 2: 6-DIGIT OTP VERIFICATION */}
            {step === 2 && (
              <div className="space-y-5 animate-fadeIn">
                {/* Number Display & Edit */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <Smartphone className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">Sent to:</span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      {phoneNumber.startsWith('+') ? phoneNumber : `+91 ${phoneNumber}`}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setStep(1);
                      setErrorMessage(null);
                    }}
                    className="text-xs text-cyan-600 hover:text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:hover:text-cyan-300 font-bold flex items-center space-x-1"
                  >
                    <Edit2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Change</span>
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Prototype Helper Banner */}
                <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-cyan-50/70 dark:bg-cyan-950/40 border border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-cyan-900 dark:text-cyan-200">
                        Demo Simulation Code: <b className="font-mono text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-300 text-xs">{simulatedOtp}</b>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[9px] text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400">
                        Fast test bypass code ready for verification
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleAutoFillOtp}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-xs transition-transform active:scale-95"
                  >
                    ⚡ Auto Fill
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* 6-Digit Pin Input Matrix */}
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-center text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-3">
                    Enter 6-Digit One-Time Password (OTP)
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex justify-center space-x-2 sm:space-x-3">
                    {otpDigits.map((digit, idx) => (
                      <input
                        key={idx}
                        ref={(el) => {
                          otpInputsRef.current[idx] = el;
                        }}
                        type="text"
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        pattern="[0-9]*"
                        maxLength={1}
                        value={digit}
                        onChange={(e) => handleOtpDigitChange(idx, e.target.value)}
                        onKeyDown={(e) => handleOtpKeyDown(idx, e)}
                        onPaste={idx === 0 ? handleOtpPaste : undefined}
                        className="w-11 h-13 sm:w-12 sm:h-14 text-center text-xl font-black font-mono rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-cyan-500/30 transition-all"
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Resend Cooldown & Action */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs pt-1">
                  <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Didn't receive code?
                  </span>
                  {cooldown > 0 ? (
                    <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center space-x-1">
                      <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                      <span>Resend in {cooldown}s</span>
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleSendOtp()}
                      disabled={loading}
                      className="text-cyan-600 hover:text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:hover:text-cyan-300 font-bold flex items-center space-x-1"
                    >
                      <RotateCw className="w-3 h-3" />
                      <span>Resend OTP</span>
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Verify Button */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleVerifyOtp()}
                  disabled={loading || otpDigits.some((d) => d === '')}
                  className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 via-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-cyan-500 hover:to-blue-500 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30 flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 transition-all hover:scale-[1.01] disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? (
                    <RotateCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Verify & Continue</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* STEP 3: SUCCESS CELEBRATION */}
            {step === 3 && (
              <div className="py-6 text-center space-y-4 animate-fadeIn">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 border-2 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/20 animate-bounce">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                    {isNewUserRegistered ? 'Registration Successful!' : 'Welcome Back!'}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mt-1">
                    Authenticated as <b className="text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400">{successUser?.full_name || successUser?.username}</b> ({successUser?.role})
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-left font-mono text-xs space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-slate-500">Mobile:</span>
                    <span className="font-bold">{successUser?.phone_number}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-slate-500">Role:</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400">{successUser?.role}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-slate-500">Security:</span>
                    <span className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">2FA Verified</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/30 transition-all"
                >
                  Enter Command Center Now
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default LoginModal;
```

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Radio, 
  CloudRain, 
  MapPin, 
  FileCheck,
  TrendingUp
} from 'lucide-react';
import { DashboardStats } from '../types';

interface MetricsCardsProps {
  stats: DashboardStats | null;
}

export const MetricsCards: React.FC<MetricsCardsProps> = ({ stats }) => {
  if (!stats) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-7 gap-2.5 mb-4 animate-pulse">
        {[...Array(7)].map((_, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="h-20 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900/60 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800" />
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const cards = [
    {
      label: 'NER Zones Monitored',
      value: `${stats.total_monitoring_zones}`,
      sub: '8 States Active',
      icon: MapPin,
      color: 'from-blue-50/60 via-cyan-50/40 to-white dark:from-blue-500/15 dark:via-cyan-500/10 dark:to-slate-900/40',
      border: 'border-cyan-200 hover:border-cyan-400 dark:border-cyan-500/30 dark:hover:border-cyan-500/60',
      text: 'text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400',
      glow: 'shadow-cyan-100/50 dark:shadow-cyan-950/30'
    },
    {
      label: 'Critical Hazard Zones',
      value: stats.critical_zones_count,
      sub: stats.critical_zones_count > 0 ? 'Immediate Action Needed' : '0 Immediate Threats',
      icon: AlertTriangle,
      color: stats.critical_zones_count > 0 
        ? 'from-red-100/80 via-red-50 to-white dark:from-red-500/30 dark:via-red-950/40 dark:to-slate-900' 
        : 'from-slate-50 to-white dark:from-slate-800/30 dark:to-slate-900',
      border: stats.critical_zones_count > 0 
        ? 'border-red-400 shadow-glow-red animate-pulse dark:border-red-500/60' 
        : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800',
      text: stats.critical_zones_count > 0 ? 'text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 font-black' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400',
      glow: stats.critical_zones_count > 0 ? 'shadow-red-200/60 dark:shadow-red-950/50' : ''
    },
    {
      label: 'High Risk Sectors',
      value: stats.high_risk_zones_count,
      sub: stats.high_risk_zones_count > 0 ? 'Watch Advisory Active' : 'Stable Slopes',
      icon: TrendingUp,
      color: stats.high_risk_zones_count > 0 
        ? 'from-orange-100/80 via-amber-50 to-white dark:from-orange-500/25 dark:via-amber-950/30 dark:to-slate-900' 
        : 'from-slate-50 to-white dark:from-slate-800/30 dark:to-slate-900',
      border: stats.high_risk_zones_count > 0 
        ? 'border-orange-400 shadow-glow-amber dark:border-orange-500/50' 
        : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800',
      text: stats.high_risk_zones_count > 0 ? 'text-orange-700 dark:text-orange-400' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400',
      glow: stats.high_risk_zones_count > 0 ? 'shadow-orange-200/60 dark:shadow-orange-950/40' : ''
    },
    {
      label: 'Early Warning Alerts',
      value: stats.active_alerts_count,
      sub: stats.active_alerts_count > 0 ? 'Active Advisories' : 'No Active Alarms',
      icon: ShieldAlert,
      color: stats.active_alerts_count > 0 
        ? 'from-rose-100/80 via-rose-50 to-white dark:from-rose-500/25 dark:via-rose-950/30 dark:to-slate-900' 
        : 'from-slate-50 to-white dark:from-slate-800/30 dark:to-slate-900',
      border: stats.active_alerts_count > 0 ? 'border-rose-400 dark:border-rose-500/50' : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800',
      text: stats.active_alerts_count > 0 ? 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-black' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400',
      glow: ''
    },
    {
      label: 'IoT Sensors Mesh',
      value: `${stats.online_sensors_count} / ${stats.online_sensors_count + stats.offline_sensors_count}`,
      sub: stats.offline_sensors_count > 0 ? `${stats.offline_sensors_count} Nodes Offline` : '100% Online',
      icon: Radio,
      color: 'from-emerald-50/70 via-emerald-50/30 to-white dark:from-emerald-500/20 dark:via-emerald-950/20 dark:to-slate-900',
      border: 'border-emerald-200 hover:border-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-500/30 dark:hover:border-emerald-500/60',
      text: 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400',
      glow: 'shadow-emerald-100/50 dark:shadow-emerald-950/30'
    },
    {
      label: 'Avg 24h Rainfall',
      value: `${stats.average_rainfall_24h}`,
      unit: 'mm',
      sub: 'Regional Telemetry',
      icon: CloudRain,
      color: 'from-sky-50/70 via-sky-50/30 to-white dark:from-sky-500/20 dark:via-sky-950/20 dark:to-slate-900',
      border: 'border-sky-200 hover:border-sky-400 dark:border-sky-500/30 dark:hover:border-sky-500/60',
      text: 'text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-400',
      glow: 'shadow-sky-100/50 dark:shadow-sky-950/30'
    },
    {
      label: 'Ground Reports',
      value: stats.total_citizen_reports,
      sub: `${stats.active_incidents_count} Incidents Verified`,
      icon: FileCheck,
      color: 'from-purple-50/70 via-indigo-50/30 to-white dark:from-purple-500/20 dark:via-indigo-950/20 dark:to-slate-900',
      border: 'border-purple-200 hover:border-purple-400 dark:border-purple-500/30 dark:hover:border-purple-500/60',
      text: 'text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-400',
      glow: 'shadow-purple-100/50 dark:shadow-purple-950/30'
    }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-7 gap-2.5 mb-4">
      {cards.map((card, idx) => {
        const Icon = card.icon;
        return (
          <div
            key={idx}
            className={`bg-gradient-to-b ${card.color} ${card.border} border rounded-xl p-3 backdrop-blur-md flex flex-col justify-between transition-all hover:scale-[1.02] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md dark:shadow-lg ${card.glow}`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-tight line-clamp-1">
                {card.label}
              </span>
              <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${card.text} flex-shrink-0`} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className={`text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black font-mono leading-none ${card.text}`}>
                {card.value}
                {card.unit && <span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 ml-1">{card.unit}</span>}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 truncate font-medium">
                {card.sub}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

export default MetricsCards;
```

## File 42/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/MonitoringZonesView.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { MonitoringZone, RiskLevel } from '../types';
import { 
  Sliders, 
  Search, 
  MapPin, 
  Mountain, 
  CloudRain, 
  Gauge, 
  ExternalLink, 
  ChevronRight, 
  TrendingUp 
} from 'lucide-react';

interface MonitoringZonesViewProps {
  zones: MonitoringZone[];
  onSelectZone: (zone: MonitoringZone) => void;
}

export const MonitoringZonesView: React.FC<MonitoringZonesViewProps> = ({
  zones,
  onSelectZone,
}) => {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [filterState, setFilterState] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [filterRisk, setFilterRisk] = useState<string>('ALL');

  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'grid' | 'table'>('grid');
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<string>('risk_desc');

  const states = Array.from(new Set(zones.map((z) => z.state)));

  let filteredZones = zones.filter((z) => {
    if (filterState !== 'ALL' && z.state !== filterState) return false;
    if (filterRisk !== 'ALL' && z.current_risk_level !== filterRisk) return false;
    if (searchQuery) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      return (
        z.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        z.district.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        z.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        z.state.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    }
    return true;
  });

  // Sort
  filteredZones = [...filteredZones].sort((a, b) => {
    if (sortBy === 'risk_desc') return b.current_risk_score - a.current_risk_score;
    if (sortBy === 'risk_asc') return a.current_risk_score - b.current_risk_score;
    if (sortBy === 'slope_desc') return b.slope_degree - a.slope_degree;
    if (sortBy === 'name') return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
    return 0;
  });

  const getRiskBadge = (lvl: RiskLevel) => {
    switch (lvl) {
      case 'CRITICAL': return 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-500/40 animate-pulse font-bold';
      case 'HIGH': return 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-300 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-400 dark:border-orange-500/40 font-bold';
      case 'MODERATE': return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-500/40 font-semibold';
      case 'LOW':
      default: return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-500/40 font-semibold';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl transition-colors duration-200">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Sliders className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Monitored Regional Sectors ({zones.length} Zones)
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Georeferenced monitoring sectors across 8 North Eastern Region states with slope, lithology, and IoT sensors.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* View Toggle & Filters */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* View Mode Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-xs">
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-bold ${
                viewMode === 'grid' ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              Cards
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode('table')}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-bold ${
                viewMode === 'table' ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500 dark:text-slate-950' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              Table
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Search */}
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search zone, district..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* State Filter */}
          <select
            value={filterState}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterState(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="ALL">All States</option>
            {states.map((st) => (
              <option key={st} value={st}>{st}</option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {/* Risk Level Filter */}
          <select
            value={filterRisk}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterRisk(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="ALL">All Risk Levels</option>
            <option value="CRITICAL">CRITICAL</option>
            <option value="HIGH">HIGH</option>
            <option value="MODERATE">MODERATE</option>
            <option value="LOW">LOW</option>
          </select>

          {/* Sort */}
          <select
            value={sortBy}
            onChange={(e) => setSortBy(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="risk_desc">Risk: High → Low</option>
            <option value="risk_asc">Risk: Low → High</option>
            <option value="slope_desc">Slope: Steepest</option>
            <option value="name">Name (A-Z)</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {viewMode === 'grid' ? (
        /* Grid of Zone Cards */
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {filteredZones.map((z) => (
            <div
              key={z.id}
              onClick={() => onSelectZone(z)}
              className="bg-white hover:bg-slate-50/80 dark:bg-slate-900/80 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200 hover:border-cyan-400 dark:border-slate-800 dark:hover:border-cyan-500/50 rounded-xl p-4 transition-all cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm hover:shadow-md group"
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400">
                    {z.id}
                  </span>
                  <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold border uppercase ${getRiskBadge(z.current_risk_level)}`}>
                    {z.current_risk_level} • {z.current_risk_score.toFixed(0)}/100
                  </span>
                </div>

                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white group-hover:text-cyan-700 dark:group-hover:text-cyan-300 transition-colors">
                  {z.name}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5 font-medium">
                  {z.district}, {z.state}
                </p>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 my-3 text-[11px] font-mono">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 block uppercase font-sans font-semibold">Slope</span>
                    <span className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold">{z.slope_degree}°</span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 block uppercase font-sans font-semibold">Elevation</span>
                    <span className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold">{z.elevation}m</span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 block uppercase font-sans font-semibold">Past Slides</span>
                    <span className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold">{z.historical_landslide_count}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 line-clamp-1">
                  Lithology: <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">{z.lithology_type}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="pt-2 mt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 font-bold group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform">
                <span>Inspect Telemetry & Gauges</span>
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Full Monitoring Zones Data Table */
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/90 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 font-bold">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Zone ID & Name</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">District / State</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Risk Level</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Score</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">24h Rainfall</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Soil Sat</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Slope</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Tilt Angle</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Sensor Status</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Action</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800/60 font-mono">
                {filteredZones.map((z) => {
                  let rain = 45.0;
                  let soil = 65.0;
                  let tilt = 0.8;
                  if (z.sensors) {
                    z.sensors.forEach((s) => {
                      if (s.sensor_type.includes('RAIN')) rain = s.latest_reading;
                      if (s.sensor_type.includes('SOIL')) soil = s.latest_reading;
                      if (s.sensor_type.includes('TILT')) tilt = s.latest_reading;
                    });
                  }
                  return (
                    <tr key={z.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <div className="font-bold text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300">{z.id}</div>
                        <div className="font-sans text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs">{z.name}</div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-sans text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">
                        {z.district}, {z.state}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-sans">
                        <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold border ${getRiskBadge(z.current_risk_level)}`}>
                          {z.current_risk_level}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-sm">
                        {z.current_risk_score.toFixed(0)}<span className="text-slate-400 font-normal text-xs">/100</span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-300 font-bold">{rain.toFixed(1)} mm</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 font-bold">{soil.toFixed(1)}%</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{z.slope_degree}°</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 font-bold">{tilt.toFixed(2)}°</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-sans">
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300 text-[10px] font-bold">
                          4 ONLINE
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-sans">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => onSelectZone(z)}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1 shadow-xs"
                        >
                          <span>Inspect →</span>
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default MonitoringZonesView;
```

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  Activity, 
  Radio, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  FileText, 
  Smartphone, 
  Layers, 
  BarChart3, 
  BookOpen, 
  HeartPulse, 
  User, 
  Clock,
  Sliders,
  Search,
  HelpCircle,
  Sparkles,
  Sun,
  Moon,
  CheckCircle2,
  ExternalLink
} from 'lucide-react';
import { UserRole, AuthUser } from '../types';
import { api } from '../services/api';

interface NavbarProps {
  currentTab: string;
  setCurrentTab: (tab: string) => void;
  userRole: UserRole;
  setUserRole: (role: UserRole) => void;
  activeAlertsCount: number;
  onOpenSearch: () => void;
  onOpenTour: () => void;
  isStandalone: boolean;
  onToggleStandalone: (standalone: boolean) => void;
  theme?: 'light' | 'dark';
  onToggleTheme?: () => void;
  currentUser?: AuthUser | null;
  onOpenLogin?: () => void;
}

export const Navbar: React.FC<NavbarProps> = ({
  currentTab,
  setCurrentTab,
  userRole,
  setUserRole,
  activeAlertsCount,
  onOpenSearch,
  onOpenTour,
  isStandalone,
  onToggleStandalone,
  theme = 'light',
  onToggleTheme,
  currentUser = null,
  onOpenLogin,
}) => {
  const [time, setTime] = useState<string>('');
  const [showServerModal, setShowServerModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [serverUrl, setServerUrl] = useState<string>(api.getBaseUrl());
  const [isTesting, setIsTesting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [testResult, setTestResult] = useState<{ ok: boolean; status: string; details?: any; latencyMs?: number; error?: string } | null>(null);

  const handleTestConnection = async () => {
    setIsTesting(true);
    setTestResult(null);
    const res = await api.testConnection(serverUrl);
    setTestResult(res);
    setIsTesting(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => {
      const now = new Date();
      setTime(now.toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false, timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata' }) + ' IST');
    };
    update();
    const interval = setInterval(update, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  // Categorized Navigation Structure
  const navGroups = [
    {
      group: 'COMMAND CENTER',
      items: [
        { id: 'map', label: 'GIS Risk Map', icon: Layers },
        { id: 'zones', label: 'Zones (22)', icon: Sliders },
        { id: 'sensors', label: 'IoT Sensors', icon: Radio },
      ]
    },
    {
      group: 'THREATS & INCIDENTS',
      items: [
        { id: 'alerts', label: 'Alerts', icon: AlertTriangle, badge: activeAlertsCount },
        { id: 'incidents', label: 'Incidents', icon: ShieldAlert },
      ]
    },
    {
      group: 'FIELD & CITIZEN',
      items: [
        { id: 'field', label: 'Field Mode', icon: Smartphone },
        { id: 'citizen', label: 'Citizen Portal', icon: FileText },
      ]
    },
    {
      group: 'INTELLIGENCE',
      items: [
        { id: 'analytics', label: 'AI Analytics', icon: BarChart3 },
        { id: 'health', label: 'System Health', icon: HeartPulse },
        { id: 'references', label: 'Docs', icon: BookOpen },
      ]
    }
  ];

  const allNavItems = navGroups.flatMap(g => g.items);

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 bg-white/95 dark:bg-[#080d1a]/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm dark:shadow-2xl transition-colors duration-200">
      {/* Top Utility Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-50 via-slate-50 to-indigo-50 dark:from-cyan-950/70 dark:via-[#0a1120] dark:to-indigo-950/70 px-4 py-1 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/30 font-mono">
            SIH 2026 • SIH26001
          </span>
          <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hidden md:inline text-[11px] font-medium">
            AI Early Warning & Landslide Risk Monitoring System in NER — Team NEXORA
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          {/* Mode Pill Switcher */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowServerModal(true)}
            className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-bold border transition-all ${
              isStandalone
                ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-500/15 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-500/30'
                : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-500/15 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/30'
            }`}
            title="Click to check or switch backend connectivity mode"
          >
            <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${isStandalone ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse'}`} />
            <span>{isStandalone ? 'STANDALONE SIMULATOR' : 'LIVE FASTAPI BACKEND'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Time */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 font-mono text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-[11px]">
            <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
            <span>{time}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Command Center Header */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-14 gap-2">
          {/* Brand Logo */}
          <div 
            onClick={() => setCurrentTab('map')}
            className="flex items-center space-x-2.5 cursor-pointer group flex-shrink-0"
          >
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500 via-blue-600 to-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-cyan-500/20 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
              <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <span className="font-black text-lg sm:text-xl tracking-wider text-slate-900 dark:text-transparent dark:bg-clip-text dark:bg-gradient-to-r dark:from-white dark:via-cyan-200 dark:to-cyan-400">
                  AVERTIS
                </span>
                <span className="text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-200 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/30 font-mono font-bold">
                  NER
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium tracking-tight -mt-0.5 hidden sm:block">
                Avert Risk Before Disaster
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Search Bar & Quick Jump Trigger */}
          <button
            onClick={onOpenSearch}
            className="hidden md:flex items-center space-x-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200/80 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-900/80 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:border-slate-700/80 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-slate-200 text-xs transition-all w-60 lg:w-72 shadow-xs"
          >
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="flex-1 text-left truncate">Search zones, sensors, alerts...</span>
            <kbd className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-mono text-slate-500 bg-white border border-slate-300 dark:text-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:border-slate-800 rounded">
              Ctrl K
            </kbd>
          </button>

          {/* Navigation Items (Categorized Bar) */}
          <nav className="hidden xl:flex items-center space-x-1">
            {allNavItems.map((item) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;
              const isActive = currentTab === item.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={item.id}
                  onClick={() => setCurrentTab(item.id)}
                  className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all relative ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-cyan-50 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-300 shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/40'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/60'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isActive ? 'text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'}`} />
                  <span>{item.label}</span>
                  {item.badge !== undefined && item.badge > 0 && (
                    <span className="ml-1 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[9px] font-bold bg-red-100 text-red-700 border border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/30 dark:text-red-300 dark:border-red-500/40 animate-pulse">
                      {item.badge}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          {/* Right Action Tools */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 sm:space-x-2">
            {/* Theme Toggle Button */}
            {onToggleTheme && (
              <button
                onClick={onToggleTheme}
                className="flex items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:text-amber-400 dark:border-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-xs"
                title={`Switch to ${theme === 'light' ? 'Dark' : 'Light'} Mode`}
                aria-label="Toggle Theme"
              >
                {theme === 'light' ? (
                  <Moon className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-700" />
                ) : (
                  <Sun className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                )}
              </button>
            )}

            {/* Quick Tour Button */}
            <button
              onClick={onOpenTour}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-50 hover:bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-200 dark:bg-cyan-500/10 dark:hover:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/30 text-xs font-semibold transition-all shadow-xs"
              title="Interactive App Walkthrough & Guide"
            >
              <HelpCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Guide</span>
            </button>

            {/* Authentication / User Profile Button */}
            {onOpenLogin && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenLogin}
                className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-xs border ${
                  currentUser
                    ? 'bg-cyan-50 hover:bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-900 border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:hover:bg-cyan-500/30 dark:text-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/40'
                    : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 to-blue-600 hover:from-cyan-500 hover:to-blue-500 text-white border-transparent shadow-cyan-600/20'
                }`}
                title={currentUser ? `Logged in as ${currentUser.full_name || currentUser.username} (${currentUser.role})` : 'Sign in or register with mobile OTP'}
              >
                {currentUser ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-cyan-700 text-white dark:bg-cyan-400 dark:text-slate-900 flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] font-black">
                      {(currentUser.full_name || currentUser.username).charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                    </div>
                    <span className="hidden sm:inline max-w-[100px] truncate text-[11px]">
                      {currentUser.full_name?.split(' ')[0] || currentUser.username}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[9px] px-1 py-0.2 rounded bg-cyan-200/80 dark:bg-cyan-400/20 text-cyan-900 dark:text-cyan-300 font-mono">
                      {currentUser.role}
                    </span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Smartphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>Sign In</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            )}

            {/* Role Switcher Pill */}
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-900/90 dark:border-slate-700/80 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 shadow-xs">
              <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
              <select
                value={userRole}
                onChange={(e) => setUserRole(e.target.value as UserRole)}
                className="bg-transparent text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 font-semibold focus:outline-none cursor-pointer text-xs"
              >
                <option value="ADMIN" className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">Admin (State DM)</option>
                <option value="AUTHORITY" className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">Authority (SDMA)</option>
                <option value="FIELD_OFFICER" className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">Field Officer</option>
                <option value="CITIZEN" className="bg-white text-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">Citizen / Public</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Server Settings Icon */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowServerModal(true)}
              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:text-cyan-700 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-cyan-300 dark:border-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-xs"
              title="Configure Backend Connection & Diagnostics"
            >
              <Sliders className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Secondary Quick Navigation Bar for Laptops / Tablets */}
      <div className="xl:hidden flex items-center overflow-x-auto space-x-1 px-3 py-1.5 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/80 scrollbar-none">
        {allNavItems.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          const isActive = currentTab === item.id;
          return (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              onClick={() => setCurrentTab(item.id)}
              className={`flex-shrink-0 flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold transition-all ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/30'
                  : 'text-slate-600 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 dark:text-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-900/60 dark:hover:text-slate-200 dark:border-transparent'
              }`}
            >
              <Icon className="w-3 h-3" />
              <span>{item.label}</span>
              {item.badge !== undefined && item.badge > 0 && (
                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-500/30 dark:text-red-300 text-[9px] font-bold border border-red-300 dark:border-red-500/40">
                  {item.badge}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Connection & Diagnostics Modal */}
      {showServerModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1000] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-cyan-500/40 rounded-2xl max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400">
                <Sliders className="w-5 h-5" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Engine Mode & Server Diagnostics</h3>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowServerModal(false)}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white text-lg font-bold"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 text-xs">
              {/* Standalone vs Live Switcher */}
              <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">
                  Operational Execution Engine
                </span>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      onToggleStandalone(false);
                      api.setStandalone(false);
                    }}
                    className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border text-left flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
                      !isStandalone
                        ? 'bg-cyan-50 border-cyan-300 text-cyan-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-cyan-500/15 dark:border-cyan-500/50 dark:text-cyan-200'
                        : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:border-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Live Backend</span>
                      <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${!isStandalone ? 'bg-cyan-500 animate-pulse' : 'bg-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-600'}`} />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">FastAPI REST + WebSockets</span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      onToggleStandalone(true);
                      api.setStandalone(true);
                    }}
                    className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border text-left flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
                      isStandalone
                        ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-amber-500/15 dark:border-amber-500/50 dark:text-amber-200'
                        : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:border-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Standalone Demo</span>
                      <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${isStandalone ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-600'}`} />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">Built-in 22 NER Zones & AI Math</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1 font-semibold">FastAPI Backend URL</label>
                <div className="flex space-x-2">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={serverUrl}
                    onChange={(e) => setServerUrl(e.target.value)}
                    className="flex-1 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 font-mono text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                    placeholder="http://127.0.0.1:8000/api"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleTestConnection}
                    disabled={isTesting}
                    className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 text-white hover:bg-cyan-700 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:hover:bg-cyan-500/30 border border-transparent dark:border-cyan-500/40 font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 transition-colors shadow-xs"
                  >
                    {isTesting ? (
                      <span className="inline-block w-3 h-3 border-2 border-white dark:border-cyan-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></span>
                    ) : (
                      <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    )}
                    <span>{isTesting ? 'Testing...' : 'TEST'}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Quick Presets */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setServerUrl('http://127.0.0.1:8000/api')}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:border-slate-800 text-[10px] font-medium"
                >
                  Localhost (127.0.0.1:8000)
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setServerUrl('http://10.0.2.2:8000/api')}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:border-slate-800 text-[10px] font-medium"
                >
                  Android Emulator (10.0.2.2)
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Diagnostic Test Result Box */}
              {testResult && (
                <div className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-xs animate-fadeIn ${
                  testResult.ok
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-300 text-emerald-900 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:border-emerald-500/40 dark:text-emerald-200'
                    : 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-900 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:border-amber-500/40 dark:text-amber-200'
                }`}>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-bold pb-1 border-b border-black/10 dark:border-white/10">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                      <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${testResult.ok ? 'bg-emerald-600 animate-pulse' : 'bg-amber-600'}`}></span>
                      <span>{testResult.ok ? 'BACKEND CONNECTED' : 'STANDALONE MODE ACTIVE'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] opacity-80">{testResult.latencyMs} ms latency</span>
                  </div>

                  {testResult.ok && testResult.details ? (
                    <div className="mt-2 space-y-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">
                      <div>API: <b className="text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Operational</b></div>
                      <div>AI Engine: <b className="text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Random Forest Active</b></div>
                      <div>Theme: <b className="text-slate-900 dark:text-white">SIH26001 (NER Landslide)</b></div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="mt-2 text-xs space-y-1 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                      <p className="text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200 font-semibold">{testResult.error}</p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                        You can continue using all 22 zones, sensors, simulation triggers, and GIS maps with zero interruption!
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="pt-3 flex justify-end space-x-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowServerModal(false)}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
                >
                  Close
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    api.setBaseUrl(serverUrl);
                    setShowServerModal(false);
                    window.location.reload();
                  }}
                  className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30"
                >
                  Save Configuration
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
};

export default Navbar;
```

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Compass, 
  Sparkles, 
  Radio, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Smartphone, 
  Users, 
  ArrowRight, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  X, 
  Play, 
  Layers, 
  CloudRain, 
  Activity 
} from 'lucide-react';

interface QuickTourModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onNavigateTab: (tab: string) => void;
  onTriggerSimulation?: (type: string) => void;
}

export const QuickTourModal: React.FC<QuickTourModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onNavigateTab,
  onTriggerSimulation,
}) => {
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(0);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const steps = [
    {
      badge: 'Step 1 of 4 • Spatial Intelligence',
      title: 'Live Interactive GIS Risk Map',
      subtitle: 'Monitor 22 geological sectors across all 8 North Eastern Region states in real time.',
      icon: Layers,
      color: 'from-cyan-100 via-blue-50 to-indigo-50 dark:from-cyan-500/20 dark:via-blue-500/10 dark:to-indigo-500/10',
      iconColor: 'text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400',
```
