```tsx
              Key Capabilities & How to Test:
            </span>
            {current.highlights.map((h, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="flex items-start space-x-2 text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span className="leading-relaxed">{h}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Quick Action Button */}
          <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-between">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                onNavigateTab(current.actionTab);
                onClose();
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30 flex items-center space-x-2 transition-transform hover:scale-105"
            >
              <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
              <span>{current.actionLabel}</span>
            </button>

            {/* Step Indicators */}
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
              {steps.map((_, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={idx}
                  onClick={() => setCurrentStep(idx)}
                  className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all ${
                    currentStep === idx ? 'w-6 bg-cyan-600 dark:bg-cyan-400' : 'w-2 bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-500'
                  }`}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer Navigation */}
        <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
          <button
            onClick={() => setCurrentStep((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1))}
            disabled={currentStep === 0}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
              currentStep === 0
                ? 'text-slate-400 cursor-not-allowed'
                : 'text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900 bg-white border border-slate-200 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:text-white dark:bg-slate-900 dark:border-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Previous</span>
          </button>

          <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-mono font-semibold">
            {currentStep + 1} of {steps.length}
          </span>

          {currentStep < steps.length - 1 ? (
            <button
              onClick={() => setCurrentStep((prev) => Math.min(steps.length - 1, prev + 1))}
              className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-cyan-800 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 dark:text-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/30 font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5"
            >
              <span>Next</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/30"
            >
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Start Exploring</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default QuickTourModal;

```

## File 46/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/SensorsView.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { SensorDevice, SensorStatus } from '../types';
import { 
  Radio, 
  Battery, 
  Wifi, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  CheckCircle, 
  Search, 
  Filter, 
  Cpu, 
  Send, 
  Sparkles 
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../services/api';

interface SensorsViewProps {
  sensors: SensorDevice[];
  onRefresh: () => void;
}

export const SensorsView: React.FC<SensorsViewProps> = ({ sensors, onRefresh }) => {
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');

  // Manual Ingestion Simulator Modal State
  const [showInjectModal, setShowInjectModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [injectSensorId, setInjectSensorId] = useState<string>('SENS-NER-001-SOIL-01');
  const [injectZoneId, setInjectZoneId] = useState<string>('ZONE-NER-001');
  const [injectMoisture, setInjectMoisture] = useState<number>(85.0);
  const [injectRainfall, setInjectRainfall] = useState<number>(140.0);
  const [injectTilt, setInjectTilt] = useState<number>(3.5);
  const [injectVibration, setInjectVibration] = useState<number>(0.45);
  const [injectSubmitting, setInjectSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [injectResult, setInjectResult] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleManualInject = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      setInjectSubmitting(true);
      await api.postSensorReading({
        sensor_id: injectSensorId,
        zone_id: injectZoneId,
        soil_moisture: Number(injectMoisture),
        rainfall_24h: Number(injectRainfall),
        tilt_angle: Number(injectTilt),
        vibration_level: Number(injectVibration),
        battery_level: 95,
        signal_strength: -65,
        is_simulated: true,
      });
      setInjectResult('Reading successfully ingested! AI model recalculated hazard.');
      onRefresh();
      setTimeout(() => {
        setInjectResult(null);
        setShowInjectModal(false);
      }, 2000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setInjectResult(`Error: ${err.message}`);
    } finally {
      setInjectSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const filteredSensors = sensors.filter((s) => {
    if (filterType !== 'ALL' && !s.sensor_type.includes(filterType)) return false;
    if (filterStatus !== 'ALL' && s.status !== filterStatus) return false;
    if (searchQuery) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      return s.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.zone_id.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.sensor_type.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    }
    return true;
  });

  const getStatusBadge = (status: SensorStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'ONLINE': return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-500/30';
      case 'OFFLINE': return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-700/40 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-600';
      case 'ANOMALY': return 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-500/40 animate-pulse font-bold';
      case 'LOW_BATTERY': return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-400 dark:border-amber-500/30';
      case 'SIMULATED':
      default: return 'bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/20 dark:text-cyan-400 dark:border-cyan-500/30';
    }
  };

  const totalCount = sensors.length;
  const onlineCount = sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'ONLINE').length;
  const offlineCount = sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'OFFLINE').length;
  const anomalyCount = sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'ANOMALY').length;
  const lowBatCount = sensors.filter((s) => s.battery_level < 25 || s.status === 'LOW_BATTERY').length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Header & Action Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl transition-colors duration-200">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Radio className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              IoT Sensor Network & Health Telemetry
            </h2>
            <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-cyan-100 dark:bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-500/20 font-mono font-semibold">
              {sensors.length} Active Nodes
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Real-time telemetry streams from ESP32 nodes monitoring Soil Saturation, Rain Gauges, MPU6050 Inclinometers, and Piezo Vibration.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => setShowInjectModal(true)}
          className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30 transition-all self-start md:self-auto"
        >
          <Cpu className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Simulate ESP32 Ingestion →</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Sensor Network HUD Metrics Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-5 gap-2.5">
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-xs">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">Total Sensors</span>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold font-mono text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300 mt-0.5">{totalCount}</div>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 font-mono font-medium">Mesh Deployment</span>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-xs">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">Online Nodes</span>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold font-mono text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 mt-0.5">{onlineCount}</div>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-500 font-mono font-medium">Active Streams</span>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-xs">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">Offline Nodes</span>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold font-mono text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">{offlineCount}</div>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 font-mono font-medium">Requires Patrol</span>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-xs">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">Abnormal Nodes</span>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold font-mono text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 mt-0.5">{anomalyCount}</div>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-red-600 dark:text-red-500 font-mono font-medium">Geotech Surge</span>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-xs">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">Low Battery</span>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold font-mono text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 mt-0.5">{lowBatCount}</div>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-500 font-mono font-medium">&lt; 25% Charge</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Search Controls */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 shadow-xs">
        {/* Search */}
        <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[200px]">
          <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search sensor ID, zone code, type..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700/80 rounded-lg pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Type Filter */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">
          <span>Type:</span>
          <select
            value={filterType}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterType(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="ALL">All Sensors</option>
            <option value="SOIL">Soil Moisture</option>
            <option value="RAIN">Rain Gauge</option>
            <option value="TILT">Inclinometer (MPU6050)</option>
            <option value="VIB">Vibration (Piezo)</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        {/* Status Filter */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">
          <span>Status:</span>
          <select
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="ALL">All Statuses</option>
            <option value="ONLINE">ONLINE</option>
            <option value="OFFLINE">OFFLINE</option>
            <option value="ANOMALY">ANOMALY</option>
            <option value="LOW_BATTERY">LOW BATTERY</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sensor Table Grid */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm dark:shadow-xl">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
            <thead className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/80 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 font-bold">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Sensor ID</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Zone</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Type</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Latest Reading</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Battery</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">RSSI Signal</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Status</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Provenance</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800/60 font-mono">
              {filteredSensors.map((s) => (
                <tr key={s.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition-colors">
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300">{s.id}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">{s.zone_id}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">{s.sensor_type.replace('_', ' ')}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-900 dark:text-white font-bold">
                    {s.latest_reading.toFixed(2)} <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-normal">{s.unit}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                      <Battery className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${s.battery_level < 25 ? 'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400' : 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400'}`} />
                      <span>{s.battery_level}%</span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                      <Wifi className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                      <span>{s.signal_strength} dBm</span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans">
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold border ${getStatusBadge(s.status)}`}>
                      {s.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
                    {s.is_simulated ? 'SIMULATED DEMO' : 'PHYSICAL ESP32'}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Manual Ingestion Simulator Modal */}
      {showInjectModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1000] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 dark:bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fadeIn">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-cyan-500/40 rounded-2xl max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400">
                <Cpu className="w-5 h-5" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Manual ESP32 Telemetry Injector</h3>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowInjectModal(false)}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white text-lg font-bold"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleManualInject} className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-xs">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Target Sensor ID</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={injectSensorId}
                  onChange={(e) => setInjectSensorId(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Monitoring Zone ID</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={injectZoneId}
                  onChange={(e) => setInjectZoneId(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Soil Moisture (%)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.1"
                    value={injectMoisture}
                    onChange={(e) => setInjectMoisture(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">24h Rainfall (mm)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.1"
                    value={injectRainfall}
                    onChange={(e) => setInjectRainfall(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Inclinometer Tilt (°)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.1"
                    value={injectTilt}
                    onChange={(e) => setInjectTilt(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">Vibration Index (g)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.01"
                    value={injectVibration}
                    onChange={(e) => setInjectVibration(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {injectResult && (
                <div className="p-2 rounded bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-950 border border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 text-cyan-900 dark:text-cyan-300 text-xs">
                  {injectResult}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="pt-2 flex justify-end space-x-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowInjectModal(false)}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={injectSubmitting}
                  className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-bold flex items-center space-x-1 shadow-md shadow-cyan-600/30"
                >
                  <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{injectSubmitting ? 'Transmitting...' : 'Send Telemetry Payload'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SensorsView;
```

## File 47/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/components/SimulationToolbar.tsx`

- **Path:** `Prototype/frontend/src/components/SimulationToolbar.tsx`
- **Category:** 08. Frontend - UI Components & Views

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  CloudRain, 
  CloudLightning, 
  Activity, 
  AlertOctagon, 
  RotateCcw, 
  Sparkles,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Info
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../services/api';

interface SimulationToolbarProps {
  onSimulationTriggered: () => void;
}

export const SimulationToolbar: React.FC<SimulationToolbarProps> = ({ onSimulationTriggered }) => {
  const [loadingAction, setLoadingAction] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeScenario, setActiveScenario] = useState<string>('NORMAL BASELINE');
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState<boolean>(true);

  const triggerScenario = async (scenarioKey: string, apiCall: () => Promise<any>, label: string, detailDesc: string) => {
    try {
      setLoadingAction(scenarioKey);
      setStatusMessage(`Executing ${label}...`);
      await apiCall();
      setActiveScenario(label);
      setStatusMessage(`Simulation Applied: ${label} — ${detailDesc}`);
      onSimulationTriggered();
      setTimeout(() => setStatusMessage(null), 6000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setStatusMessage(`Error: ${err.message}`);
    } finally {
      setLoadingAction(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gradient-to-r dark:from-slate-900 dark:via-[#101a2e] dark:to-slate-900 border border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 rounded-2xl p-3 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md dark:shadow-xl dark:shadow-cyan-950/25 mb-4 transition-all">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-2.5">
        {/* Title & Active Scenario Indicator */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full md:w-auto">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
            <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-700 border border-cyan-300 dark:bg-cyan-500/15 dark:border-cyan-500/30 dark:text-cyan-400">
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 animate-pulse" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300">
                  SIH Demonstration Simulation Engine
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-mono font-bold border border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/30">
                  {activeScenario}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                Trigger real-time stress scenarios to test AI risk engine & alert triggers.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile Collapse Toggle */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
            className="md:hidden p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white"
          >
            {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Buttons (Section 9 Controls) */}
        {isExpanded && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 pt-2 md:pt-0">
            {/* Normal Conditions */}
            <button
              onClick={() => triggerScenario('normal', api.resetSimulation, 'NORMAL BASELINE', 'Restored normal geotechnical baseline.')}
              disabled={loadingAction !== null}
              className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all shadow-xs ${
                activeScenario === 'NORMAL BASELINE' || activeScenario === 'NORMAL CONDITIONS'
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold shadow-emerald-600/30 ring-2 ring-emerald-500'
                  : 'bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-500/15 dark:hover:bg-emerald-500/25 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/30'
              }`}
            >
              <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Normal</span>
            </button>

            {/* Heavy Rain */}
            <button
              onClick={() => triggerScenario('heavy', api.simulateHeavyRainfall, 'HEAVY RAINFALL (168mm)', 'Precipitation surge to 168mm, raising soil saturation to 88%.')}
              disabled={loadingAction !== null}
              className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all shadow-xs ${
                activeScenario === 'HEAVY RAINFALL (168mm)'
                  ? 'bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold shadow-amber-600/30 ring-2 ring-amber-500'
                  : 'bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200 dark:bg-amber-500/15 dark:hover:bg-amber-500/25 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-500/30'
              }`}
            >
              <CloudRain className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Heavy Rain (168mm)</span>
            </button>

            {/* Extreme Deluge */}
            <button
              onClick={() => triggerScenario('extreme', api.simulateExtremeRainfall, 'EXTREME DELUGE (225mm)', 'Catastrophic monsoon rainfall trigger across sensitive slopes.')}
              disabled={loadingAction !== null}
              className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all shadow-xs ${
                activeScenario === 'EXTREME DELUGE (225mm)'
                  ? 'bg-red-600 text-white font-bold shadow-red-600/30 ring-2 ring-red-500'
                  : 'bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-800 border border-red-200 dark:bg-red-500/15 dark:hover:bg-red-500/25 dark:text-red-300 dark:border-red-500/30'
              }`}
            >
              <CloudLightning className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Extreme Deluge (225mm)</span>
            </button>

            {/* Ground Shift / Inclinometer Surge */}
            <button
              onClick={() => triggerScenario('tilt', api.simulateGroundMovement, 'GROUND SHIFT (4.2° Tilt)', 'Severe inclinometer displacement detected.')}
              disabled={loadingAction !== null}
              className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all shadow-xs ${
                activeScenario === 'GROUND SHIFT (4.2° Tilt)'
                  ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white font-bold shadow-purple-600/30 ring-2 ring-purple-500'
                  : 'bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 dark:bg-purple-500/15 dark:hover:bg-purple-500/25 dark:text-purple-300 dark:border-purple-500/30'
              }`}
            >
              <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Ground Shift (4.2°)</span>
            </button>

            {/* Sensor Anomaly */}
            <button
              onClick={() => triggerScenario('anomaly', api.simulateSensorFailure, 'SENSOR HARDWARE FAULT', 'Simulated telemetry anomalies and offline dropouts.')}
              disabled={loadingAction !== null}
              className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all shadow-xs ${
                activeScenario === 'SENSOR HARDWARE FAULT'
                  ? 'bg-slate-800 text-white font-bold shadow-slate-800/30 ring-2 ring-slate-600'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-800/80 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              <AlertOctagon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Fault</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Live Status Toast / Notification */}
      {statusMessage && (
        <div className="mt-2.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-950/50 border border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/40 text-[11px] text-cyan-900 dark:text-cyan-300 flex items-center space-x-2 animate-fadeIn">
          {loadingAction ? (
            <span className="inline-block w-3 h-3 border-2 border-cyan-600 dark:border-cyan-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin flex-shrink-0" />
          ) : (
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          )}
          <span className="font-medium truncate">{statusMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SimulationToolbar;

```

```tsx
import { DashboardStats } from '../types';

interface SystemHealthViewProps {
  stats: DashboardStats | null;
}

export const SystemHealthView: React.FC<SystemHealthViewProps> = ({ stats }) => {
  if (!stats) return null;

  const subsystems = [
    {
      name: 'Weather Data Grid',

      icon: Layers,
      latency: '110 ms',

    {

            <HeartPulse className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              System Health & Telemetry Diagnostics
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Real-time connectivity and status verification across all AVERTIS microservices and data pipelines.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300 font-mono text-xs font-bold shadow-xs">
          <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 animate-ping-slow"></span>
          <span>ALL SUBSYSTEMS HEALTHY</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Subsystem Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        {subsystems.map((sub, idx) => {
          const Icon = sub.icon;
          return (
            <div
              key={idx}
              className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-sm hover:shadow-md"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400">
                  <Icon className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wide font-semibold">
                    {sub.type}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">
                    {sub.name}
                  </h3>
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 mt-1 flex items-center space-x-1">
                    <CheckCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>{sub.status}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="text-right">
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block uppercase font-mono font-medium">Response</span>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300">{sub.latency}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SystemHealthView;
```

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { 
  MonitoringZone, 
  RiskLevel 
} from '../types';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  CloudRain, 
  Gauge, 
  Mountain, 
  Compass, 
  Activity, 
  AlertCircle, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Radio, 
  Layers, 
  Sparkles, 
  Info 
} from 'lucide-react';

interface ZoneDetailPanelProps {
  zone: MonitoringZone | null;
  onClose?: () => void;
}

export const ZoneDetailPanel: React.FC<ZoneDetailPanelProps> = ({ zone }) => {
  if (!zone) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#0f172a]/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-6 text-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full min-h-[400px] shadow-sm">
        <Compass className="w-12 h-12 text-cyan-600/40 dark:text-cyan-500/40 mb-3 animate-spin-slow" />
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">Select a Monitored Zone</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
          Click any zone polygon or marker on the GIS map or choose from the zones list to inspect real-time AI hazard telemetry.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const score = zone.current_risk_score;
  const level = zone.current_risk_level;

  const getBadgeStyle = (lvl: RiskLevel) => {
    switch (lvl) {
      case 'CRITICAL': return 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300 dark:bg-red-500/20 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-500/40 animate-pulse font-black';
      case 'HIGH': return 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-300 dark:bg-orange-500/20 dark:text-orange-400 dark:border-orange-500/40 font-bold';
      case 'MODERATE': return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-amber-400 dark:border-amber-500/40 font-semibold';
      case 'LOW':
      default: return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-500/40 font-semibold';
    }
  };

  const getMeterColor = (lvl: RiskLevel) => {
    switch (lvl) {
      case 'CRITICAL': return 'bg-red-600 shadow-glow-red';
      case 'HIGH': return 'bg-orange-500 shadow-glow-amber';
      case 'MODERATE': return 'bg-amber-500';
      case 'LOW':
      default: return 'bg-emerald-500 shadow-glow-emerald';
    }
  };

  // Find rain, soil, tilt from sensors if available
  let rain24 = 45.0;
  let soil = 65.0;
  let tilt = 0.8;
  let vib = 0.05;

  if (zone.sensors && zone.sensors.length > 0) {
    zone.sensors.forEach((s) => {
      if (s.sensor_type.includes('RAIN')) rain24 = s.latest_reading;
      if (s.sensor_type.includes('SOIL')) soil = s.latest_reading;
      if (s.sensor_type.includes('TILT')) tilt = s.latest_reading;
      if (s.sensor_type.includes('VIB')) vib = s.latest_reading;
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gradient-to-b dark:from-slate-900 dark:to-[#0d1527] border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm dark:shadow-2xl flex flex-col h-full overflow-y-auto transition-colors duration-200">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="pb-3 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
            {zone.id}
          </span>
          <span className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] tracking-wide border uppercase ${getBadgeStyle(level)}`}>
            {level} RISK
          </span>
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-1 leading-tight">
          {zone.name}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">
          {zone.district} District, {zone.state}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Primary Risk Gauge Card */}
      <div className="my-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">
            Composite Hazard Score
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-baseline space-x-1.5 mt-0.5">
            <span className="text-3xl font-black font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              {score.toFixed(0)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">/100</span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">
            Failure Probability: <b className="text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300 font-mono">{(score / 100).toFixed(2)}</b>
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Circular / Progress Indicator */}
        <div className="w-24">
          <div className="h-2.5 w-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className={`h-full transition-all duration-700 ${getMeterColor(level)}`}
              style={{ width: `${Math.min(score, 100)}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500 text-center block mt-1 font-mono font-medium">
            {score < 25 ? '0-24: LOW' : score < 50 ? '25-49: MOD' : score < 75 ? '50-74: HIGH' : '75-100: CRITICAL'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Telemetry Metrics Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs mb-3">
        {/* Rainfall */}
        <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/70 rounded-lg p-2.5">

            {rain24.toFixed(1)} <span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">mm</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate font-medium">
            {rain24 > 120 ? 'Torrential Overload' : rain24 > 60 ? 'Moderate Rain' : 'Normal'}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Soil Moisture */}
        <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/70 rounded-lg p-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 mb-1">

      <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 mb-3">

            <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">Rainfall 24h:</span>
            <span className={`font-bold ${rain24 > 120 ? 'text-red-700 dark:text-red-400' : rain24 > 50 ? 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400' : 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400'}`}>
              {rain24 > 120 ? 'CRITICAL' : rain24 > 50 ? 'MODERATE' : 'LOW'}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-1.5 rounded bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200/60 dark:border-transparent">
```

## File 50/83: `Prototype/frontend/src/services/api.ts`

- **Path:** `Prototype/frontend/src/services/api.ts`
- **Category:** 09. Frontend - Services, API Client & Type Definitions
- **Language:** typescript
- **Lines:** 588
- **Size:** 21,461 bytes

```typescript
import {
  MonitoringZone,
  SensorDevice,
  AlertItem,
  CitizenReport,
  LandslideIncident,
  DashboardStats,
  RiskPrediction,
  TimelineEvent,
  AuthUser,
  SendOtpResponse,
  VerifyOtpResponse
} from '../types';
import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';
import { mockStore, calculateRisk } from './mockData';

export const getApiBaseUrl = (): string => {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('avertis_api_base_url');
  if (saved) return saved;

  if (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') {
    // Android emulator host loopback is 10.0.2.2
    return 'http://10.0.2.2:8000/api';
  }
  return import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api';
};

export const setApiBaseUrl = (newUrl: string): void => {
  localStorage.setItem('avertis_api_base_url', newUrl);
};

let standaloneModeOverride = localStorage.getItem('avertis_force_standalone') === 'true';

export const api = {
  // Config
  getBaseUrl(): string {
    return getApiBaseUrl();
  },
  setBaseUrl(url: string): void {
    setApiBaseUrl(url);
  },
  isStandalone(): boolean {
    return standaloneModeOverride;
  },
  setStandalone(val: boolean): void {
    standaloneModeOverride = val;
    localStorage.setItem('avertis_force_standalone', val ? 'true' : 'false');
  },

  // Connection Health & Diagnostics
  async testConnection(targetUrl?: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean; status: string; details?: any; latencyMs?: number; error?: string }> {
    const base = targetUrl || getApiBaseUrl();
    const endpoint = base.endsWith('/api') ? `${base}/health` : `${base}/api/health`;
    const startTime = performance.now();
    try {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3500);
      const res = await fetch(endpoint, { signal: controller.signal });
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startTime);

      if (!res.ok) {
        return { ok: false, status: `HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.statusText}`, latencyMs, error: 'Server returned error status' };
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      return { ok: true, status: 'CONNECTED', details: data, latencyMs };
    } catch (err: any) {
      const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startTime);
      return { 
        ok: false, 
        status: 'STANDALONE SIMULATOR', 
        latencyMs, 
        error: err.name === 'AbortError' ? 'Connection timed out (3.5s)' : 'FastAPI Backend offline. Seamlessly utilizing Built-in Standalone Simulator.' 
      };
    }
  },

  // Zones
  async getZones(): Promise<MonitoringZone[]> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) return mockStore.zones;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/zones`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000) });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch monitoring zones');
      const data = await res.json();
      return data && data.length > 0 ? data : mockStore.zones;
    } catch {
      return mockStore.zones;
    }
  },

  async getZoneRisk(zoneId: string): Promise<any> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) {
      const zone = mockStore.zones.find((z) => z.id === zoneId) || mockStore.zones[0];
      return {
        zone_id: zone.id,
        zone_name: zone.name,
        risk_score: zone.current_risk_score,
        risk_level: zone.current_risk_level,
        factors: ['Precipitation & Soil Pore Pressure Saturation', 'Steep Slopes', 'Disang Group Lithology']
      };
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/zones/${zoneId}/risk`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000) });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch risk detail for ${zoneId}`);
      return res.json();
    } catch {
      const zone = mockStore.zones.find((z) => z.id === zoneId) || mockStore.zones[0];
      return {
        zone_id: zone.id,
        zone_name: zone.name,
        risk_score: zone.current_risk_score,
        risk_level: zone.current_risk_level,
        factors: ['Precipitation & Soil Pore Pressure Saturation', 'Steep Slopes', 'Disang Group Lithology']
      };
    }
  },

  // Sensors
  async getSensors(): Promise<SensorDevice[]> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) return mockStore.sensors;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/sensors`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000) });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch sensor devices');
      const data = await res.json();
      return data && data.length > 0 ? data : mockStore.sensors;
    } catch {
      return mockStore.sensors;
    }
  },

  async postSensorReading(reading: any): Promise<any> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) {
      const sens = mockStore.sensors.find((s) => s.id === reading.sensor_id);
      if (sens) {
        if (reading.soil_moisture !== undefined && sens.sensor_type === 'SOIL_MOISTURE') {
          sens.latest_reading = reading.soil_moisture;
        }
        if (reading.rainfall_24h !== undefined && sens.sensor_type === 'RAIN_GAUGE') {
          sens.latest_reading = reading.rainfall_24h;
        }
        if (reading.tilt_angle !== undefined && sens.sensor_type === 'TILT_INCLINOMETER') {
          sens.latest_reading = reading.tilt_angle;
        }
      }
      return { status: 'SUCCESS', reading };
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/sensors/readings`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(reading),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000)
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to submit sensor reading');
      return res.json();
    } catch {
      return { status: 'SUCCESS_FALLBACK', reading };
    }
  },

  // Alerts
  async getAlerts(status?: string, severity?: string): Promise<AlertItem[]> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) {
      return mockStore.alerts.filter((a) => {
        if (status && a.status !== status) return false;
        if (severity && a.severity !== severity) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (status) params.append('status', status);
      if (severity) params.append('severity', severity);
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/alerts?${params.toString()}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000) });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch alerts');
      const data = await res.json();
      return data && data.length > 0 ? data : mockStore.alerts;
    } catch {
      return mockStore.alerts;
    }
  },

  async acknowledgeAlert(alertId: string, officerName: string): Promise<any> {
    const alert = mockStore.alerts.find((a) => a.id === alertId);
    if (alert) {
      alert.status = 'ACKNOWLEDGED';
      alert.acknowledged_by = officerName;
      alert.acknowledged_at = new Date().toISOString();
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/alerts/${alertId}/acknowledge?officer_name=${encodeURIComponent(officerName)}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000)
      });
      if (res.ok) return res.json();
    } catch {}
    return { status: 'ACKNOWLEDGED', alert_id: alertId };
  },

  async resolveAlert(alertId: string, notes: string, officerName: string): Promise<any> {
    const alert = mockStore.alerts.find((a) => a.id === alertId);

      alert.status = 'RESOLVED';

      alert.resolved_at = new Date().toISOString();

      });
      if (res.ok) return res.json();
    } catch {}
    return { status: 'RESOLVED', alert_id: alertId };
  },

  // Reports
  async getReports(status?: string): Promise<CitizenReport[]> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) {
      return mockStore.reports.filter((r) => !status || r.status === status);

      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/reports?${params.toString()}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000) });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch reports');
      const data = await res.json();
      return data && data.length > 0 ? data : mockStore.reports;
    } catch {
      return mockStore.reports;
    }
  },

  async submitReport(report: Partial<CitizenReport>): Promise<CitizenReport> {
    const newReport: CitizenReport = {
      id: `REP-CIT-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      zone_id: report.zone_id,
      report_type: report.report_type || 'Landslide Observation',
      description: report.description || '',
      location_name: report.location_name || 'Geo-Tagged Field Coordinates',
      latitude: report.latitude || 27.34,
      longitude: report.longitude || 88.62,
      severity: report.severity || 'HIGH',
      image_url: report.image_url,
      reporter_name: report.reporter_name || 'Citizen Reporter',
      reporter_phone: report.reporter_phone || '+91 90000 00000',
      reporter_role: report.reporter_role || 'CITIZEN',
      status: 'UNDER_REVIEW',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      is_offline_synced: !!report.is_offline_synced
    };
    mockStore.reports.unshift(newReport);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/reports`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(report),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000)
      });
      if (res.ok) return res.json();
    } catch {}
    return newReport;
  },

  async updateReportStatus(reportId: string, status: string, reviewerName: string = 'Authority Reviewer'): Promise<any> {
    const report = mockStore.reports.find((r) => r.id === reportId);
    if (report) {
      report.status = status as any;
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/reports/${reportId}/status?new_status=${status}&reviewer_name=${encodeURIComponent(reviewerName)}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000)
      });
      if (res.ok) return res.json();
    } catch {}
    return { status, report_id: reportId };
  },

  // Incidents

  async createIncident(incident: Partial<LandslideIncident>): Promise<LandslideIncident> {

  async resolveIncident(incidentId: string, notes: string): Promise<any> {

  // Prediction on-demand
  async predictRisk(features: any): Promise<RiskPrediction> {
    const calculated = calculateRisk({
      slope: features.slope_degree || 35,
      r24: features.rainfall_24h || 50,
      soil: features.soil_moisture || 60,
      tilt: features.tilt_angle || 0.5,
      vib: features.vibration_level || 0.05,
      hist: features.historical_landslides || 5,
      dist: features.distance_to_drainage_km || 1.0,
      veg: features.vegetation_cover_pct || 60
    });

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/predict`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(features),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2500)
      });
      if (res.ok) return res.json();
    } catch {}

    return {
      zone_id: features.zone_id || 'ZONE-NER-001',
      risk_probability: calculated.prob,
      risk_score: calculated.score,
      risk_level: calculated.level,
      contributors: {
        rainfall: `${features.rainfall_24h || 45} mm`,
        soil_moisture: `${features.soil_moisture || 60}%`,
        slope: `${features.slope_degree || 35}°`,
        historical_activity: 'Moderate',
        satellite_change: 'Normal',
        tilt: `${features.tilt_angle || 0.5}°`,
        vibration: `${features.vibration_level || 0.05}g`
      },
      explainability_summary: `Hazard score estimated at ${calculated.score}/100 driven by terrain slope and precipitation saturation.`,
      recommendations: ['Maintain standard telemetry watch', 'Clear surface stormwater channels'],
      model_version: 'RandomForest-NER-v1.0',
      is_simulated: true
    };
  },

  // Mobile Number OTP Authentication
  async sendOtp(phoneNumber: string, role?: string, fullName?: string): Promise<SendOtpResponse> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) {
      return mockStore.sendOtp(phoneNumber, role, fullName);
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/auth/send-otp`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          phone_number: phoneNumber,
          role: role || 'CITIZEN',
          full_name: fullName
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000)
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(err.detail || `Server error (${res.status})`);
      }
      return res.json();
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === 'AbortError' || err.message?.includes('Failed to fetch')) {
        console.warn('Backend offline, using standalone OTP simulator.');
        return mockStore.sendOtp(phoneNumber, role, fullName);
      }
      throw err;
    }
  },

  async verifyOtp(
    phoneNumber: string, 
    otp: string, 
    role?: string, 
    fullName?: string, 
    assignedZoneId?: string
  ): Promise<VerifyOtpResponse> {
    if (standaloneModeOverride) {
      const authRes = mockStore.verifyOtp(phoneNumber, otp, role, fullName, assignedZoneId);
      this.setStoredUser(authRes.user as AuthUser, authRes.access_token);
      return authRes;
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/auth/verify-otp`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          phone_number: phoneNumber,
          otp,
          role,
          full_name: fullName,
          assigned_zone_id: assignedZoneId
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000)
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(err.detail || 'OTP verification failed. Please try again.');
      }
      const data: VerifyOtpResponse = await res.json();
      this.setStoredUser(data.user, data.access_token);
      return data;
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === 'AbortError' || err.message?.includes('Failed to fetch')) {
        console.warn('Backend offline, using standalone OTP simulator.');
        const authRes = mockStore.verifyOtp(phoneNumber, otp, role, fullName, assignedZoneId);
        this.setStoredUser(authRes.user as AuthUser, authRes.access_token);
        return authRes;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  },

  // Auth Session Storage Helpers
  getStoredUser(): AuthUser | null {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('avertis_auth_user');
      if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved);
    } catch {}
    return null;
  },

  setStoredUser(user: AuthUser, token?: string): void {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('avertis_auth_user', JSON.stringify(user));
      if (token) localStorage.setItem('avertis_auth_token', token);
      if (user.role) localStorage.setItem('avertis_user_role', user.role);
    } catch {}
  },

  clearStoredUser(): void {
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem('avertis_auth_user');
      localStorage.removeItem('avertis_auth_token');
    } catch {}
  },

  async getCurrentUser(): Promise<AuthUser | null> {
    const stored = this.getStoredUser();
    if (!stored) return null;
    if (standaloneModeOverride) return stored;
    try {
      const url = stored.phone_number 
        ? `${getApiBaseUrl()}/auth/me?phone=${encodeURIComponent(stored.phone_number)}`
        : `${getApiBaseUrl()}/auth/me?username=${encodeURIComponent(stored.username)}`;
      const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2500) });
      if (res.ok) {
        const liveUser = await res.json();
        this.setStoredUser(liveUser);
        return liveUser;
      }
    } catch {}
    return stored;
  },

  // Legacy password login
  async login(username: string, password: string = 'demo123'): Promise<any> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${getApiBaseUrl()}/auth/login`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000)
      });
      if (res.ok) return res.json();
    } catch {}
    return { access_token: 'mock-jwt-token', token_type: 'bearer', role: 'ADMIN', username };
  }
};
```

```typescript
import {
  MonitoringZone,
  SensorDevice,
  AlertItem,
  CitizenReport,
  LandslideIncident,
  DashboardStats,
  TimelineEvent,
  RiskLevel
} from '../types';

export const makeGeoJsonPolygon = (lat: number, lon: number, offset: number = 0.035): string => {
  const coords = [
    [lon - offset, lat - offset * 0.7],
    [lon + offset, lat - offset * 0.7],
    [lon + offset * 1.2, lat + offset * 0.8],
    [lon - offset * 0.8, lat + offset * 0.9],
    [lon - offset, lat - offset * 0.7]
  ];
  return JSON.stringify({
    type: "Polygon",
    coordinates: [coords]
  });
};

// Calculate Risk Score & Level based on AI features
export const calculateRisk = (features: {
  slope: number;
  r24: number;
  soil: number;
  tilt: number;
  vib: number;
  hist: number;
  dist: number;
  veg: number;
}): { score: number; level: RiskLevel; prob: number; factors: string[] } => {
  // Geotechnical weighted scoring model
  const slopeWeight = (features.slope / 50) * 22;
  const rainWeight = Math.min((features.r24 / 200) * 35, 38);
  const soilWeight = (features.soil / 100) * 25;
  const tiltWeight = Math.min(features.tilt * 8, 12);
  const vibWeight = Math.min(features.vib * 30, 8);
  const histWeight = Math.min(features.hist * 1.2, 10);
  const drainWeight = Math.max(0, (3 - features.dist) * 2);
  const vegDiscount = (features.veg / 100) * 8;

  let rawScore = slopeWeight + rainWeight + soilWeight + tiltWeight + vibWeight + histWeight + drainWeight - vegDiscount;
  rawScore = Math.max(5, Math.min(98, Math.round(rawScore)));

  let level: RiskLevel = 'LOW';
  if (rawScore >= 75) level = 'CRITICAL';
  else if (rawScore >= 50) level = 'HIGH';
  else if (rawScore >= 25) level = 'MODERATE';

  const factors: string[] = [];
  if (features.r24 >= 80) factors.push(`Excessive 24h Rainfall (${features.r24.toFixed(1)}mm)`);
  if (features.soil >= 75) factors.push(`High Pore Pressure & Soil Saturation (${features.soil.toFixed(0)}%)`);
  if (features.slope >= 38) factors.push(`Steep Slope Angle (${features.slope.toFixed(1)}°)`);
  if (features.tilt >= 1.0) factors.push(`Active Ground Inclinometer Tilt (${features.tilt.toFixed(2)}°)`);
  if (features.vib >= 0.10) factors.push(`Piezo Micro-Seismic Vibration (${features.vib.toFixed(2)}g)`);

  return {
    score: rawScore,
    level,
    prob: parseFloat((rawScore / 100).toFixed(2)),
    factors: factors.length > 0 ? factors : ['Baseline Normal Geotechnical Stability']
  };
};

export const createInitialZones = (): MonitoringZone[] => {
  return INITIAL_ZONE_DEFS.map((def) => {
    const risk = calculateRisk({
      slope: def.slope,
      r24: def.r24,
      soil: def.soil,
      tilt: def.tilt,
      vib: def.vib,
      hist: def.hist,
      dist: def.dist,
      veg: def.veg
    });

    return {
      id: def.id,
      name: def.name,
      district: def.district,
      state: def.state,
      latitude: def.lat,
      longitude: def.lon,
      slope_degree: def.slope,
      elevation: def.elevation,
      lithology_type: def.lithology,
      vegetation_cover: def.veg,
      historical_landslide_count: def.hist,
      distance_to_previous_landslide: def.dist,
      satellite_change_indicator: def.sat,
      citizen_report_count: 0,
      boundary_geojson: makeGeoJsonPolygon(def.lat, def.lon),
      current_risk_score: risk.score,
      current_risk_level: risk.level,
      active: true,
      sensors: []
    };
  });
};

export const createInitialSensors = (zones: MonitoringZone[]): SensorDevice[] => {
  const list: SensorDevice[] = [];
  zones.forEach((z, index) => {
    const isTopVulnerable = index < 6;
    const def = INITIAL_ZONE_DEFS[index] || INITIAL_ZONE_DEFS[0];

    list.push({
      id: `SENS-${z.id.replace('ZONE-', '')}-SOIL-01`,
      zone_id: z.id,
      sensor_type: 'SOIL_MOISTURE',
      model_name: 'Decagon 5TM Soil Moisture & Temp Sensor',
      latitude: z.latitude + 0.003,
      longitude: z.longitude - 0.002,
      status: 'ONLINE',
      battery_level: 92,
      signal_strength: -68,
      latest_reading: def.soil,
      unit: '% VWC',
      is_simulated: false,
      last_seen: new Date().toISOString()
    });

    list.push({
      id: `SENS-${z.id.replace('ZONE-', '')}-RAIN-01`,
      zone_id: z.id,
      sensor_type: 'RAIN_GAUGE',
      model_name: 'Davis Instruments Tipping Bucket 0.2mm',
      latitude: z.latitude - 0.002,
      longitude: z.longitude + 0.003,
      status: 'ONLINE',
      battery_level: 96,
      signal_strength: -64,
      latest_reading: def.r24,
      unit: 'mm',
      is_simulated: false,
      last_seen: new Date().toISOString()
    });

    if (isTopVulnerable) {
      list.push({
        id: `SENS-${z.id.replace('ZONE-', '')}-TILT-01`,
        zone_id: z.id,
        sensor_type: 'TILT_INCLINOMETER',
        model_name: 'MPU6050 6-DoF Subsurface Inclinometer',
        latitude: z.latitude + 0.001,
        longitude: z.longitude + 0.001,
        status: 'ONLINE',
        battery_level: 88,
        signal_strength: -72,
        latest_reading: def.tilt,
        unit: 'degrees',
        is_simulated: false,
        last_seen: new Date().toISOString()
      });
    }
  });

  return list;
};

// In-Memory Simulation State Store
class MockDataStore {
  zones: MonitoringZone[];
  sensors: SensorDevice[];
  alerts: AlertItem[];
  reports: CitizenReport[];
  incidents: LandslideIncident[];
  timeline: TimelineEvent[];

  constructor() {
    this.zones = createInitialZones();
    this.sensors = createInitialSensors(this.zones);
    // Attach sensors to zones
    this.zones.forEach((z) => {
      z.sensors = this.sensors.filter((s) => s.zone_id === z.id);
    });
    this.alerts = createInitialAlerts();
    this.reports = createInitialReports();
    this.incidents = createInitialIncidents();
    this.timeline = createInitialTimeline();
  }

  getDashboardStats(): DashboardStats {
    const crit = this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL').length;
    const high = this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'HIGH').length;
    const mod = this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE').length;
    const low = this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'LOW').length;
    const online = this.sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'ONLINE').length;
    const offline = this.sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'OFFLINE').length;
    const abnormal = this.sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'ANOMALY').length;
    const activeAlerts = this.alerts.filter((a) => a.status === 'ACTIVE').length;
    const activeIncidents = this.incidents.filter((i) => i.verification_status !== 'RESOLVED').length;

    let totalRain = 0;
    let rainCount = 0;
    this.sensors.forEach((s) => {
      if (s.sensor_type === 'RAIN_GAUGE') {
        totalRain += s.latest_reading;
        rainCount++;
      }
    });
    const avgRain = rainCount > 0 ? parseFloat((totalRain / rainCount).toFixed(1)) : 48.5;

    return {
      total_monitoring_zones: this.zones.length,
      critical_zones_count: crit,
      high_risk_zones_count: high,
      moderate_risk_zones_count: mod,
      low_risk_zones_count: low,
      active_alerts_count: activeAlerts,
      online_sensors_count: online,
      offline_sensors_count: offline,
      abnormal_sensors_count: abnormal,
      average_rainfall_24h: avgRain,
      total_citizen_reports: this.reports.length,
      active_incidents_count: activeIncidents,
      system_health: {
        weather_data: 'LIVE SIMULATOR (IMD NER Radar Grid)',
        sensor_network: 'OPERATIONAL (25 Node ESP32 Mesh)',
        satellite_feed: 'CONNECTED (ISRO Bhuvan / Sentinel-1 SAR)',
        ai_engine: 'OPERATIONAL (Random Forest v1.0 Geotechnical)',
        database: 'STANDALONE IN-MEMORY REPOSITORY',
        alert_engine: 'OPERATIONAL (Dynamic Rule Dispatch)',
        api_gateway: 'STANDALONE SIMULATOR / DEMO MODE'
      }
    };
  }

  getAnalytics() {
    return {
      rain_risk_correlation: this.zones.map((z) => {
        const rainSens = z.sensors?.find((s) => s.sensor_type === 'RAIN_GAUGE');
        return {
          zone: z.name.split(' ')[0] + ' (' + z.state.substring(0, 3) + ')',
          rainfall: rainSens ? rainSens.latest_reading : 45,
          risk_score: z.current_risk_score
        };
      }),
      risk_distribution: [
        { name: 'Low Risk', value: this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'LOW').length, color: '#10b981' },
        { name: 'Moderate', value: this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'MODERATE').length, color: '#f59e0b' },
        { name: 'High Risk', value: this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'HIGH').length, color: '#f97316' },
        { name: 'Critical', value: this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL').length, color: '#ef4444' }
      ],
      seven_day_trend: [
        { day: 'Mon', avg_rainfall: 28, critical_zones: 0, high_zones: 2 },
        { day: 'Tue', avg_rainfall: 42, critical_zones: 0, high_zones: 3 },
        { day: 'Wed', avg_rainfall: 65, critical_zones: 1, high_zones: 4 },
        { day: 'Thu', avg_rainfall: 82, critical_zones: 2, high_zones: 5 },
        { day: 'Fri', avg_rainfall: 58, critical_zones: 2, high_zones: 4 },
        { day: 'Sat', avg_rainfall: 48, critical_zones: 1, high_zones: 3 },
        { day: 'Sun (Today)', avg_rainfall: 54, critical_zones: this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'CRITICAL').length, high_zones: this.zones.filter((z) => z.current_risk_level === 'HIGH').length }
      ],
      sensor_health: {
        online: this.sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'ONLINE').length,
        offline: this.sensors.filter((s) => s.status === 'OFFLINE').length,
        battery_avg: 91
      }
    };
  }

  // Simulations
  simulateHeavyRainfall() {
    this.zones.forEach((z) => {
      const isHilly = z.slope_degree > 35;
      const r24 = isHilly ? 168.0 : 95.0;
      const soil = isHilly ? 88.0 : 72.0;

      z.sensors?.forEach((s) => {
        if (s.sensor_type === 'RAIN_GAUGE') s.latest_reading = r24;
        if (s.sensor_type === 'SOIL_MOISTURE') s.latest_reading = soil;
      });

      const risk = calculateRisk({
        slope: z.slope_degree,
        r24,
        soil,
        tilt: 1.2,
        vib: 0.10,
        hist: z.historical_landslide_count,
        dist: z.distance_to_previous_landslide,
        veg: z.vegetation_cover
      });

      z.current_risk_score = risk.score;
      z.current_risk_level = risk.level;
    });

    this.alerts.unshift({
      id: `ALT-SIM-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      zone_id: 'ZONE-NER-001',
      district: 'East Khasi Hills',
      severity: 'CRITICAL',
      risk_score: 92,
      title: 'SIMULATED HEAVY MONSOON: Torrential Rainfall Spike (168mm)',
      reason: 'Precipitation exceeded 150mm threshold. Soil saturation surged to 88%. AI risk escalated to CRITICAL.',
      message: 'Precipitation exceeded 150mm threshold. Soil saturation surged to 88%. AI risk escalated to CRITICAL.',
      status: 'ACTIVE',
      recommended_actions_json: JSON.stringify(['Broadcast emergency advisory', 'Issue red alert to district disaster officers']),
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    });

    this.timeline.unshift({
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false }),
      type: 'SIMULATION_TRIGGER',
      title: 'Heavy Rainfall Simulated',
      detail: 'Executed Heavy Rainfall Scenario (168mm surge). AI recalculated all NER hill sectors.'
    });
  }

  simulateExtremeRainfall() {
    this.zones.forEach((z) => {
      const r24 = 225.0;
      const soil = 94.0;
      z.sensors?.forEach((s) => {
        if (s.sensor_type === 'RAIN_GAUGE') s.latest_reading = r24;
        if (s.sensor_type === 'SOIL_MOISTURE') s.latest_reading = soil;
      });

      const risk = calculateRisk({
        slope: z.slope_degree,
        r24,
        soil,
        tilt: 2.1,
        vib: 0.18,
        hist: z.historical_landslide_count,
        dist: z.distance_to_previous_landslide,
        veg: z.vegetation_cover
      });

      z.current_risk_score = risk.score;
      z.current_risk_level = risk.level;
    });

    this.alerts.unshift({
      id: `ALT-SIM-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      zone_id: 'ZONE-NER-004',
      district: 'East Sikkim',
      severity: 'CRITICAL',
      risk_score: 96,
      title: 'EXTREME MONSOON DELUGE (225mm) - MULTI-ZONE EMERGENCY',
      reason: 'Regional deluge triggered massive pore pressure surge. Multi-state red alert active.',
      message: 'Regional deluge triggered massive pore pressure surge. Multi-state red alert active.',
      status: 'ACTIVE',
      recommended_actions_json: JSON.stringify(['Activate SDRF rapid mobilization protocol', 'Initiate hillside evacuation']),
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    });

    this.timeline.unshift({
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false }),
      type: 'SIMULATION_TRIGGER',
      title: 'Extreme Monsoon Deluge',
      detail: 'Executed Extreme Rainfall Surge (225mm). Widespread critical alerts generated.'
    });
  }

  simulateGroundMovement() {
    this.zones.forEach((z) => {
      if (z.slope_degree > 36) {
        z.sensors?.forEach((s) => {
          if (s.sensor_type === 'TILT_INCLINOMETER') s.latest_reading = 4.25;
        });

        const risk = calculateRisk({
          slope: z.slope_degree,
          r24: 95.0,
          soil: 80.0,
          tilt: 4.25,
          vib: 0.68,
          hist: z.historical_landslide_count,
          dist: z.distance_to_previous_landslide,
          veg: z.vegetation_cover
        });
        z.current_risk_score = risk.score;
        z.current_risk_level = risk.level;
      }
    });

    this.alerts.unshift({
      id: `ALT-SIM-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      zone_id: 'ZONE-NER-012',
      district: 'Kohima',
      severity: 'CRITICAL',
      risk_score: 90,
      title: 'SUBSURFACE SHEAR DETECTED: Tilt Angle 4.25° Surge',
      reason: 'Inclinometer recorded severe subsurface lateral displacement. Active slope creep in progress.',
      message: 'Inclinometer recorded severe subsurface lateral displacement. Active slope creep in progress.',
      status: 'ACTIVE',
      recommended_actions_json: JSON.stringify(['Close NH-29 bypass', 'Deploy geotechnical inspection team']),
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    });

    this.timeline.unshift({
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false }),
      type: 'STRAIN_ANOMALY',
      title: 'Ground Inclinometer Surge',
      detail: 'Simulated Inclinometer Tilt & Vibration spike. IoT strain alerts broadcast.'
    });
  }

  simulateSensorFailure() {
    const target = this.sensors.find((s) => s.status === 'ONLINE');
    if (target) {
      target.status = 'OFFLINE';
      target.signal_strength = 0;
    }

    this.timeline.unshift({
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false }),
      type: 'NODE_FAILURE',
      title: 'Sensor Node Heartbeat Lost',
      detail: `Sensor Node ${target?.id || 'SENS-01'} lost heartbeat. AI activated spatial imputation fallback.`
    });
  }

  resetSimulation() {
    this.zones = createInitialZones();
    this.sensors = createInitialSensors(this.zones);
    this.zones.forEach((z) => {
      z.sensors = this.sensors.filter((s) => s.zone_id === z.id);
    });
    this.alerts = createInitialAlerts();
    this.reports = createInitialReports();
    this.incidents = createInitialIncidents();
    this.timeline = createInitialTimeline();

    this.timeline.unshift({
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour12: false }),
      type: 'SYSTEM_RESET',
      title: 'Geotechnical Baseline Restored',
      detail: 'Restored baseline geotechnical telemetry and environmental equilibrium.'
    });
  }

  // Auth & OTP Simulator
  mockUsers: Record<string, any> = {
    '+919876543210': { id: 1, username: 'admin', phone_number: '+919876543210', role: 'ADMIN', full_name: 'State Disaster Controller', email: 'admin@avertis.gov.in' },
    '+919436100001': { id: 2, username: 'authority', phone_number: '+919436100001', role: 'AUTHORITY', full_name: 'NESAC / SDMA Authority', email: 'authority@nesac.gov.in' },
    '+919436123456': { id: 3, username: 'field_shillong', phone_number: '+919436123456', role: 'FIELD_OFFICER', full_name: 'Officer L. Kharkongor', assigned_zone_id: 'ZONE-NER-001', email: 'field.shillong@avertis.gov.in' },
    '+919862099887': { id: 4, username: 'citizen_demo', phone_number: '+919862099887', role: 'CITIZEN', full_name: 'Community Reporter (Shillong)', email: 'citizen@gmail.com' }
  };

  sendOtp(phone: string, role?: string, fullName?: string) {
    const normalized = phone.startsWith('+') ? phone : `+91${phone.replace(/\D/g, '')}`;
    return {
      status: 'SUCCESS',
      message: `A 6-digit verification code has been dispatched to ${normalized}`,
      phone_number: normalized,
      expires_in_seconds: 300,
      simulated_otp: '123456'
    };
  }

  verifyOtp(phone: string, otp: string, role?: string, fullName?: string, zoneId?: string) {
    const normalized = phone.startsWith('+') ? phone : `+91${phone.replace(/\D/g, '')}`;
    if (otp !== '123456') {
      throw new Error('Invalid 6-digit verification code. Please check and try again.');
    }

    let existing = this.mockUsers[normalized];
    let isNew = false;
    if (!existing) {
      isNew = true;
      const digits = normalized.replace(/\D/g, '');
      const suffix = digits.slice(-6);
      const userRole = (role as any) || 'CITIZEN';
      existing = {
        id: Object.keys(this.mockUsers).length + 1,
        username: `user_${suffix}`,
        phone_number: normalized,
        role: userRole,
        full_name: fullName || `Citizen (${suffix})`,
        assigned_zone_id: zoneId || (userRole === 'FIELD_OFFICER' ? 'ZONE-NER-001' : null),
        email: `user_${suffix}@avertis.internal`,
        created_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      this.mockUsers[normalized] = existing;
    }

    return {
      access_token: `avertis_mock_token_${existing.username}`,
      token_type: 'bearer',
      is_new_user: isNew,
      user: existing
    };
  }
}

export const mockStore = new MockDataStore();

```
